    process: "For each symptom seen in at least 15 cases, computes the share of those patients who received any investigation at all; sorted lowest rate first.",
    output: "rows: {label, n, investigatedRate} — the missed-work-up scan: symptoms whose work-ups often never get ordered.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "investigations were hardcoded placeholder strings until recently — on pre-import cohorts investigatedRate measures the placeholder.",
      "'Any investigation at all' is case-level, credited to every symptom the patient has — ecological fallacy within cases.",
      "Symptoms under n=15 vanish silently; the remaining rates have no expected-work-up denominator.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Unreliable input plus case-level attribution — the 'missed work-up' reading is unsafe.",
    ],
  },
};
LOS distribution

The full histogram of hospital stays: how many patients had short, typical, or extended stays — not just the median.

 {
  id: "los-distribution",
  name: "LOS distribution",
  category: "Clinical / diagnostic",
  description:
    "The full length-of-stay histogram (not just the median): how the cohort spreads across short, typical, and extended stays.",
  requiredFields: ["losDays"],
  run(cases) {
    const bands: Array<[number, number, string]> = [
      [0, 3, "0–3d"], [4, 7, "4–7d"], [8, 14, "8–14d"], [15, 21, "15–21d"], [22, 999, "22d+"],
    ];
    const rows: LosDistributionRow[] = bands.map(([lo, hi, label]) => {
      const g = cases.filter((c) => c.losDays >= lo && c.losDays <= hi);
      return { label, n: g.length, share: g.length / cases.length, caseIds: g.map((c) => c.id) };
    });
    return { rows, median: median(cases.map((c) => c.losDays)) };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    void cases;
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Each case's losDays.",
    process: "Bins stays into five length-of-stay bands (0–3, 4–7, 8–14, 15–21, 22+ days) and counts cases and cohort share in each, plus the overall median.",
    output: "rows: one per band with {label, n, share}, plus top-level median — the full LOS histogram, not just the average.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "losDays is randInt(1,21) (or encounter duration in loaders) — the histogram describes the generator's draw, not throughput.",
      "Descriptive-only; safe as a plumbing check, misleading as an operations baseline.",
    ],
  },
};
Test-ordering intensity

Groups admissions by how many tests were ordered (0–2, 3–4, 5+) and checks whether heavier testing tracks sicker patients or just pads the stay.

 {
  id: "test-intensity",
  name: "Test-ordering intensity",
  category: "Clinical / diagnostic",
  description:
    "Investigations ordered per admission, banded: does test volume track acuity (comorbidity burden) or pad the stay?",
  requiredFields: ["investigations", "comorbidities", "losDays"],
  run(cases) {
    const bands: Array<[number, number, string]> = [
      [0, 2, "0–2 tests"], [3, 4, "3–4 tests"], [5, 99, "5+ tests"],
    ];
    const rate = (g: Case[], f: (c: Case) => number) => (g.length ? g.reduce((s, c) => s + f(c), 0) / g.length : 0);
    const rows: TestIntensityRow[] = bands.map(([lo, hi, label]) => {
      const g = cases.filter((c) => c.investigations.length >= lo && c.investigations.length <= hi);
      return {
        label,
        n: g.length,
        avgComorbidities: rate(g, (c) => c.comorbidities.length),
        avgLos: rate(g, (c) => c.losDays),
        escRate: rate(g, (c) => (escalated(c) ? 1 : 0)),
        caseIds: g.map((c) => c.id),
      };
    }).filter((r) => r.n > 0);
    return { rows };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    void cases;
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Each case's investigations, comorbidities, losDays, and escalation outcome.",
    process: "Bands cases by number of investigations (0–2, 3–4, 5+), then averages comorbidity count and LOS and computes the escalation rate within each band; empty bands are dropped.",
    output: "rows: one per band with {label, n, avgComorbidities, avgLos, escRate} — read as whether test volume tracks acuity or pads the stay.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "investigations were hardcoded placeholder strings until recently — on pre-import cohorts the bands measure the placeholder, not orders.",
      "escRate by test band is circular: comorbidity burden drives both test volume correlations and the modeled escalation formula.",
      "avgLos comparison invites 'tests pad the stay' reading — losDays is randInt(1,21), independent of investigations.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Investigation counts are unreliable on legacy cohorts, and the escalation contrast is injected.",
    ],
  },
};
Checkpoint paths

Sorts patients by when — or whether — they got worse (early, mid, late, never) and describes each path's typical stay and discharge outcome.

 {
  id: "checkpoint-paths",
  name: "Checkpoint paths",
  category: "Patient / evidence",
  description:
    "How cases traverse the escalation checkpoint of the 6-checkpoint continuum: early (≤3d), mid (4–9d), late (≥10d), or never — with disposition mix per path.",
  requiredFields: ["timeToEscalationDays", "disposition", "checkpoints"],
  run(cases) {
    const pathOf = (c: Case) => {
      const t = c.timeToEscalationDays;
      if (t === null) return "no escalation";
      if (t <= 3) return "early (≤3d)";
      if (t <= 9) return "mid (4–9d)";
      return "late (≥10d)";
    };
    const paths = ["early (≤3d)", "mid (4–9d)", "late (≥10d)", "no escalation"];
    const rows: CheckpointPathsRow[] = paths.map((p) => {
      const g = cases.filter((c) => pathOf(c) === p);
      return {
        label: p,
        n: g.length,
        share: g.length / cases.length,
        medianLos: median(g.map((c) => c.losDays)),
        homePct: g.length ? g.filter((c) => c.disposition === "home").length / g.length : 0,
        caseIds: g.map((c) => c.id),
      };
    });
    void groupBy;
    return { rows };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    void cases;
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Each case's timeToEscalationDays, disposition, and losDays.",
    process: "Classifies each case as escalating early (≤3d), mid (4–9d), late (≥10d), or never, then counts each path and computes median LOS and share discharged home within it.",
    output: "rows: one per path with {label, n, share, medianLos, homePct} — read as how escalation timing shapes where patients end up.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "timeToEscalationDays and disposition are both generator outputs — 'how escalation timing shapes disposition' compares two formulas.",
      "Path bands (≤3/4–9/≥10d) slice the modeled tte ranges (1–5 injected, 1–14 else) — bands align with generator draws, not clinical phases.",
      "Single snapshot: paths are co-occurrence of two modeled variables, not an observed sequence.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Both the path and its outcomes are modeled from the same generator formulas.",
    ],
  },
};
Hub network

Maps which conditions connect to which categories, then ranks conditions by how many different categories they appear in — the 'hub' conditions.

 {
  id: "hub-network",
  name: "Hub network",
  category: "Clinical / diagnostic",
  description:
    "Category ↔ comorbidity edges occurring in at least 8 cases, with each comorbidity's hub degree — how many categories it connects to.",
  requiredFields: ["category", "comorbidities"],
  run(cases) {
    const byCat = groupBy(cases, (c) => c.category);
    const edges: Array<{ cat: string; com: string; n: number; caseIds: string[] }> = [];
    const comDegree = new Map<string, number>();
    const coms = [...new Set(cases.flatMap((c) => c.comorbidities))].sort();
    byCat.forEach((group, cat) => {
      for (const com of coms) {
        const matchingCases = group.filter((c) => c.comorbidities.includes(com));
        const n = matchingCases.length;
        if (n >= MIN_EDGE) {
          edges.push({ cat, com, n, caseIds: matchingCases.map((c) => c.id) });
          comDegree.set(com, (comDegree.get(com) ?? 0) + 1);
        }
      }
    });
    const rows = edges
      .map((e) => ({ label: `${e.com} → ${e.cat}`, n: e.n, degree: comDegree.get(e.com) ?? 0, caseIds: e.caseIds }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.degree - a.degree || b.n - a.n);
    return { rows, nEdges: rows.length };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    void cases;
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Each case's category and comorbidities.",
    process: "For every category × comorbidity edge occurring in at least 8 cases, keeps the edge and increments the comorbidity's hub degree — how many categories it connects to; ranked by degree then volume.",
    output: "rows: {label ('comorbidity → category'), n, degree}, plus nEdges — high-degree comorbidities are the cohort's connective tissue.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "ok",
    points: [
      "MIN_EDGE=8 is a volume floor, not a test — hub degree scales with cohort size and category size, not clinical connectivity.",
      "Multi-coded cases count toward every edge — edges share patients, so degrees are not independent.",
    ],
  },
};
Cohort overview

The master summary: every other analytic is read against this reference frame. Here is its entire logic as a flow:

1 · Count the basics

How many patients are there? What is the average age? How many are women and how many are men? How are they spread across the five condition categories? These are the headline numbers at the top of the page.

2 · Compute outcome baselines

What fraction of patients needed intensified care ("escalation")? How many came back within 90 days? What was the typical hospital stay? These baselines matter because every other analytic compares its own numbers against them.

3 · Sort patients into buckets

Several panels are simple tallies: medications taken (0, 1–2, 3–4, 5+), how the stay ended (home, step-down, rehab, expired), age band, and prior hospital visits. Counting into buckets turns raw records into a bar chart.

4 · Find the most common conditions

The helper topNWithIds counts how often each comorbidity, symptom, medication, test and procedure appears across the cohort, keeps the most common, and remembers which patients had each one — that's why clicking a row shows the actual cases.

5 · Spot pairs that travel together

topCoOccurrenceWithIds asks which pairs of conditions show up in the same patient, tallies each pair, and reports the most frequent — the seed of pattern discovery.

6 · Compare categories honestly

Finally it computes the escalation rate separately per category, and the built-in audit block warns that on the sample dataset these outcome rates come from simulation settings, not observation.

One rule ties it all together: the function never changes any data and never calls an AI. It only reads the patient records and returns numbers — deterministic, repeatable, testable. The code follows. 

 {
  id: "cohort-overview",
  name: "Cohort overview",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "The full picture of the cohort: size, demographics, clinical makeup (diagnoses, comorbidities, symptoms, medications, investigations, procedures) and outcome baselines — the reference frame every other analytic is read against.",
  requiredFields: ["age", "sex", "comorbidities", "symptoms", "medications", "investigations"],
  run(cases) {
    const byCat = groupBy(cases, (c) => c.category);
    const total = Math.max(1, cases.length);
    // ── distributions that drive the on-screen panels ──
    // Polypharmacy: bucket the count at admission.
    const polyph = [0, 0, 0, 0]; // 0, 1-2, 3-4, 5+
    for (const c of cases) {
      const k = c.polypharmacyCount;
      if (k === 0) polyph[0]++;
      else if (k <= 2) polyph[1]++;
      else if (k <= 4) polyph[2]++;
      else polyph[3]++;
    }
    // Disposition: 4-bucket enum on Case.
    const dispOrder = ["home", "step-down", "rehab", "expired"] as const;
    const dispCounts = new Map<string, number>(dispOrder.map((d) => [d, 0]));
    for (const c of cases) dispCounts.set(c.disposition, (dispCounts.get(c.disposition) ?? 0) + 1);
    // Age bands: 0-17 / 18-44 / 45-64 / 65-79 / 80+.
    const ageBuckets = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0];
    for (const c of cases) {
      const a = c.age;
      if (a < 18) ageBuckets[0]++;
      else if (a < 45) ageBuckets[1]++;
      else if (a < 65) ageBuckets[2]++;
      else if (a < 80) ageBuckets[3]++;
      else ageBuckets[4]++;
    }
    // Encounter-intensity bands.
    const encBuckets = [0, 0, 0, 0]; // 0, 1-2, 3-5, 6+
    for (const c of cases) {
      const k = c.encounters90d;
      if (k === 0) encBuckets[0]++;
      else if (k <= 2) encBuckets[1]++;
      else if (k <= 5) encBuckets[2]++;
      else encBuckets[3]++;
    }
    // Escalation rate by category — surfaces which condition escalates most.
    const escByCat = [...byCat.entries()]
      .map(([cat, g]) => ({
        label: cat,
        value: g.length ? g.filter(escalated).length / g.length : 0,
        n: g.length,
      }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value);

    return {
      rows: [
        { label: "Patients", value: cases.length },
        { label: "Mean age (years)", value: mean(cases.map((c) => c.age)) },
        { label: "Female", value: cases.filter((c) => c.sex === "F").length },
        { label: "Male", value: cases.filter((c) => c.sex === "M").length },
        ...[...byCat.entries()]
          .sort((a, b) => b[1].length - a[1].length)
          .map(([cat, g]) => ({ label: `Category: ${cat}`, value: g.length })),
        { label: "Escalation rate", value: cases.filter(escalated).length / total },
        { label: "90-day readmission rate", value: cases.filter((c) => c.readmitted90d).length / total },
        { label: "Median LOS (days)", value: median(cases.map((c) => c.losDays)) },
        { label: "Mean 90-day encounters", value: mean(cases.map((c) => c.encounters90d)) },
        { label: "With any SDOH barrier", value: cases.filter((c) => c.sdohBarriers.length > 0).length },
        { label: "Up to date on screening", value: cases.filter((c) => c.screeningUpToDate).length },
        { label: "Vaccinated", value: cases.filter((c) => c.vaccinated).length },
        { label: "Mean comorbidities per patient", value: mean(cases.map((c) => c.comorbidities.length)) },
        { label: "Mean medications per patient", value: mean(cases.map((c) => c.medications.length)) },
        { label: "Mean symptoms per patient", value: mean(cases.map((c) => c.symptoms.length)) },
        { label: "Mean investigations per patient", value: mean(cases.map((c) => c.investigations.length)) },
        { label: "Mean procedures per patient", value: mean(cases.map((c) => c.procedures.length)) },
      ],
      // New panels for the expanded renderer.
      byComorbidity: topNWithIds(cases, (c) => c.comorbidities, 8),
      bySymptom: topNWithIds(cases, (c) => c.symptoms, 8),
      byMedication: topNWithIds(cases, (c) => c.medications, 8),
      byInvestigation: topNWithIds(cases, (c) => c.investigations, 5),
      byProcedure: topNWithIds(cases, (c) => c.procedures, 8),
      coOccurrenceComorbidity: topCoOccurrenceWithIds(cases, (c) => c.comorbidities, 10),
      coOccurrenceInvestigation: topCoOccurrenceWithIds(cases, (c) => c.investigations, 10),
      coOccurrenceProcedure: topCoOccurrenceWithIds(cases, (c) => c.procedures, 10),
      polypharmacy: [
        { label: "0 meds", value: polyph[0] },
        { label: "1–2 meds", value: polyph[1] },
        { label: "3–4 meds", value: polyph[2] },
        { label: "5+ meds", value: polyph[3] },
      ],
      disposition: dispOrder.map((d) => ({ label: d, value: dispCounts.get(d) ?? 0 })),
      ageBands: [
        { label: "0–17", value: ageBuckets[0] },
        { label: "18–44", value: ageBuckets[1] },
        { label: "45–64", value: ageBuckets[2] },
        { label: "65–79", value: ageBuckets[3] },
        { label: "80+", value: ageBuckets[4] },
      ],
      encounterBands: [
        { label: "0 encounters", value: encBuckets[0] },
        { label: "1–2", value: encBuckets[1] },
        { label: "3–5", value: encBuckets[2] },
        { label: "6+", value: encBuckets[3] },
      ],
      escByCat,
    };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    void cases;
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The whole cohort; age, sex, comorbidities, medications, symptoms, investigations, losDays, readmitted90d, encounters90d, disposition, sdohBarriers, screeningUpToDate, vaccinated.",
    process: "Counts patients, splits by sex and category, computes cohort-wide baselines (mean age, escalation rate, 90-day readmission rate, median LOS, mean encounters, mean comorbidities/meds/symptoms/investigations/procedures). Tally top-N by comorbidity, symptom, medication, investigation, procedure; compute top co-occurrence pairs for comorbidity, investigation, procedure; bucket polypharmacy, disposition, age, encounters; report escalation rate per category.",
    output: "rows: one label/value per headline metric; byComorbidity, bySymptom, byMedication, byInvestigation, byProcedure: top-N lists; coOccurrenceComorbidity, coOccurrenceInvestigation, coOccurrenceProcedure: top co-occurrence pairs; polypharmacy, disposition, ageBands, encounterBands: bucketed counts; escByCat: per-category escalation rate — the reference frame every other analytic is read against.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Escalation/readmission baselines are generator formulas — read them as simulation parameters, not cohort findings.",
      "90-day labels (readmitted90d, encounters90d) ride a single admission snapshot with no real follow-up behind them.",
      "Top-N distributions enumerate the generator's categorical pools (COMORBIDITIES, SYMPTOMS, MEDS, INVESTIGATIONS) — distributions are simulation parameters, not observed populations.",
    ],
  },
};
Index case comparison

Picks one patient and shows where they stand compared to the whole cohort — each of their numbers is ranked as a percentile against everyone else.

 {
  id: "index-case",
  name: "Index case comparison",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "One patient's whole trajectory — LOS, burden, utilization, risk — read against the cohort benchmark in percentiles. Pick a case id in the panel; it defaults to the first case.",
  requiredFields: ["losDays", "encounters90d", "comorbidities", "polypharmacyCount", "age"],
  run(cases, params) {
    cases = diseaseFilter(cases, params?.disease);
    const c = params?.caseId
      ? cases.find((x) => x.id === params.caseId) ?? cases.find((x) => x.id.toLowerCase() === params.caseId!.toLowerCase())
      : cases[0];
    if (!c) return { error: `case '${params?.caseId}' not in cohort`, caseId: params?.caseId ?? null };
    // scope: whole cohort (default) or the case's own category as the benchmark pool
    const scope = params?.scope === "category" ? "category" : "cohort";
    const benchmark = scope === "category" ? cases.filter((x) => x.category === c.category) : cases;
    const rows = [
      {
        metric: "Age",
        value: c.age,
        median: median(benchmark.map((x) => x.age)) ?? 0,
        percentile: percentileRank(benchmark.map((x) => x.age), c.age),
      },
      {
        metric: "Comorbidity count",
        value: c.comorbidities.length,
        median: median(benchmark.map((x) => x.comorbidities.length)) ?? 0,
        percentile: percentileRank(benchmark.map((x) => x.comorbidities.length), c.comorbidities.length),
      },
      {
        metric: "Active medications",
        value: c.polypharmacyCount,
        median: median(benchmark.map((x) => x.polypharmacyCount)) ?? 0,
        percentile: percentileRank(benchmark.map((x) => x.polypharmacyCount), c.polypharmacyCount),
      },
      {
        metric: "Length of stay (days)",
        value: c.losDays,
        median: median(benchmark.map((x) => x.losDays)) ?? 0,
        percentile: percentileRank(benchmark.map((x) => x.losDays), c.losDays),
      },
      {
        metric: "90-day encounters",
        value: c.encounters90d,
        median: median(benchmark.map((x) => x.encounters90d)) ?? 0,
        percentile: percentileRank(benchmark.map((x) => x.encounters90d), c.encounters90d),
      },
      {
        metric: "Risk score",
        value: riskOf(c),
        median: median(benchmark.map(riskOf)) ?? 0,
        percentile: percentileRank(benchmark.map(riskOf), riskOf(c)),
      },
    ];
    return {
      scope,
      case: {
        id: c.id,
        category: c.category,
        age: c.age,
        sex: c.sex,
        comorbidities: c.comorbidities,
        disposition: c.disposition,
        escalated: escalated(c),
        sdohBarriers: c.sdohBarriers,
        checkpoints: c.checkpoints.map((cp) => ({ name: cp.name, value: cp.value, day: cp.day })),
      },
      rows,
    };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    void cases;
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Params caseId (defaults to the first case) and optional disease filter and scope; per-case age, comorbidities, polypharmacyCount, losDays, encounters90d.",
    process: "Finds the selected patient, computes a risk score (age/10 + comorbidity count + 1 if 5+ meds), and reports each metric's value, cohort (or category) median, and percentile rank against that benchmark.",
    output: "case (id, checkpoints, flags) plus rows: one per metric with {metric, value, median, percentile} — read percentiles as the patient's standing vs the benchmark.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "riskOf (age/10 + comorbidities + polypharmacy bonus) repeats escProb's terms — a 'high-risk' percentile patient was made high-risk by the generator.",
      "encounters90d benchmarks a modeled draw — a real patient's percentile against it is meaningless.",
      "Percentiles are unadjusted cohort or category pools — case-mix differences read as patient differences.",
    ],
  },
};

export const trajectoryMap: Analytic = {
  id: "trajectory-map",
  name: "Trajectory map",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "Every patient walked through the same nine stages (Comorbidities → Diagnosis → Presentation → Initial plan → Medications → Procedures → Escalation → Advanced/recurrent → Disposition). Multi-valued stages fan out: every individual comorbidity, symptom, medication, and procedure is its own node, so you can trace which named item leads where. Every ribbon is one group's transition between consecutive stages. Click a ribbon to see exactly which patients flow through it.",
  requiredFields: ["checkpoints", "comorbidities", "symptoms", "medications"],
  run(cases, params) {
    cases = diseaseFilter(cases, params?.disease);
    const NAMES = ["Comorbidities", "Diagnosis", "Presentation", "Initial plan", "Medications", "Procedures", "Escalation", "Advanced/recurrent", "Disposition"];
    const traceNode = params?.traceNode ?? null;
    const traceDir = (params?.traceDir as "forward" | "backward") ?? "forward";
    // Multi-valued stages: each patient contributes EVERY value, so each named
    // item (each comorbidity, symptom, medication, procedure) gets its own node.
    const valuesOf = (c: Case, name: string): string[] => {
      switch (name) {
        case "Comorbidities":
          return c.comorbidities.length ? c.comorbidities : ["(none)"];
        case "Diagnosis":
          return [c.checkpoints.find((cp) => cp.name === "Diagnosis")?.value ?? c.diagnosis ?? "(none)"];
        case "Presentation":
          return c.symptoms.length ? c.symptoms : ["Asymptomatic"];
        case "Initial plan":
          return [c.checkpoints.find((cp) => cp.name === "Initial plan")?.value ?? "(none)"];
        case "Medications":
          return c.medications.length ? c.medications : ["(none)"];
        case "Procedures":
          return c.procedureCodes?.length ? c.procedureCodes.map((p) => p.display) : (c.procedures?.length ? c.procedures : ["(none)"]);
        default:
          return [c.checkpoints.find((cp) => cp.name === name)?.value ?? "(none)"];
      }
    };
    const stages = NAMES.map((name) => {
      // n = number of patients carrying that value (not total occurrences).
      const carriers = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
      cases.forEach((c) => {
        for (const v of valuesOf(c, name)) {
          if (!carriers.has(v)) carriers.set(v, new Set());
          carriers.get(v)!.add(c.id);
        }
      });
      const rows = [...carriers.entries()]
        .map(([value, ids]) => ({ value, n: ids.size, pct: ids.size / cases.length }))
        .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n);
      return { label: name, rows };
    });
    // edges: between consecutive stages, each patient's every value in stage i
    // connects to every value in stage i+1 (fan-out × fan-in). The accumulator
    // also remembers which patients flow through each transition so the UI can
    // show "click a ribbon → patient list".
    const edges = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < NAMES.length - 1; i++) {
      const flowPatients = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
      cases.forEach((c) => {
        for (const a of valuesOf(c, NAMES[i])) {
          for (const b of valuesOf(c, NAMES[i + 1])) {
            const k = `${a}|${b}`;
            if (!flowPatients.has(k)) flowPatients.set(k, new Set());
            flowPatients.get(k)!.add(c.id);
          }
        }
      });
      edges.push({
        from: NAMES[i],
        to: NAMES[i + 1],
        flows: [...flowPatients.entries()]
          .map(([k, ids]) => {
            const [a, b] = k.split("|");
            return { a, b, n: ids.size, patients: [...ids] };
          })
          .sort((x, y) => y.n - x.n),
      });
    }
    // optional single-patient overlay (the index case, when provided) —
    // built from the same stage functions so it aligns with the 9 columns.
    const highlightId = params?.highlightCaseId;
    const highlightCase = highlightId ? cases.find((c) => c.id === highlightId) : undefined;
    const highlight = highlightCase ? NAMES.map((name) => valuesOf(highlightCase, name)[0]) : null;

    // Node-level trace: find all patients who have traceNode at its stage,
    // then show forward (all subsequent stages) or backward (all prior stages).
    let nodeTrace: { patients: string[]; forward: Array<{ stage: string; values: string[] }>; backward: Array<{ stage: string; values: string[] }> } | null = null;
    if (traceNode) {
      // Find which stage contains the traceNode value
      let traceStageIdx = -1;
      for (let si = 0; si < NAMES.length; si++) {
        // Check a sample case to see if this stage contains the value
        // We need to check across ALL cases since some might have it
        for (const c of cases) {
          const vals = valuesOf(c, NAMES[si]);
          if (vals.includes(traceNode)) {
            traceStageIdx = si;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (traceStageIdx >= 0) break;
      }
      if (traceStageIdx >= 0) {
        const tracePatients = new Set<string>();
        cases.forEach((c) => {
          const vals = valuesOf(c, NAMES[traceStageIdx]);
          if (vals.includes(traceNode)) tracePatients.add(c.id);
        });
        const forward: Array<{ stage: string; values: string[] }> = [];
        const backward: Array<{ stage: string; values: string[] }> = [];
        for (let si = traceStageIdx + 1; si < NAMES.length; si++) {
          const stageVals = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
          cases.forEach((c) => {
            if (!tracePatients.has(c.id)) return;
            for (const v of valuesOf(c, NAMES[si])) {
              if (!stageVals.has(v)) stageVals.set(v, new Set());
              stageVals.get(v)!.add(c.id);
            }
          });
          forward.push({
            stage: NAMES[si],
            values: [...stageVals.entries()]
              .map(([v, ids]) => ({ v, n: ids.size }))
              .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n)
              .map(({ v, n }) => `${v} (${n})`),
          });
        }
        for (let si = traceStageIdx - 1; si >= 0; si--) {
          const stageVals = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
          cases.forEach((c) => {
            if (!tracePatients.has(c.id)) return;
            for (const v of valuesOf(c, NAMES[si])) {
              if (!stageVals.has(v)) stageVals.set(v, new Set());
              stageVals.get(v)!.add(c.id);
            }
          });
          backward.push({
            stage: NAMES[si],
            values: [...stageVals.entries()]
              .map(([v, ids]) => ({ v, n: ids.size }))
              .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n)
              .map(({ v, n }) => `${v} (${n})`),
          });
        }
        nodeTrace = { patients: [...tracePatients], forward, backward };
      }
    }

    return { stages, edges, highlight, highlightCaseId: highlightId ?? null, total: cases.length, nodeTrace, traceNode, traceDir };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    const withCp = cases.filter((c) => c.checkpoints.length > 0).length;
    const pct = cases.length ? withCp / cases.length : 0;
    if (pct < 0.2) return { level: "weak", gaps: [`cases with checkpoints: ${Math.round(pct * 100)}%`] };
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Each case's checkpoints (Diagnosis, Initial plan, Escalation, Advanced/recurrent, Disposition) plus comorbidities, symptoms, medications, and procedureCodes; params disease, highlightCaseId, traceNode (the node value to focus on), traceDir (forward or backward).",
    process: "Builds nine stages — Comorbidities, Diagnosis, Presentation, Initial plan, Medications, Procedures, Escalation, Advanced/recurrent, Disposition — where multi-valued stages fan out every individual named item. Counts distinct patients per value, then tallies each patient's value-to-value transitions between consecutive stages (fan-out × fan-in); optionally overlays one patient's path; optionally focuses on a single node value and reports the distribution of values for patients carrying it at all forward (or backward) stages.",
    output: "stages: {label, rows:[{value, n, pct}]} (n = distinct patients carrying that value), edges: per-hop flows {a, b, n, patients}, highlight, total — each ribbon is one group's path; click a ribbon in the UI for its patient list.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Checkpoint values are assigned by deriveCheckpoints from the same tte draw — ribbons re-express one modeled variable, not observed flow.",
      "Missing checkpoint values fall into '(none)' but the stage denominator stays the whole cohort — pct rows can under-run 1.",
      "Single snapshot: edges are state co-occurrence, not verified sequence.",
    ],
  },
};

// Disease overview: generic, works for ANY disease module — the loaded
// cohort filtered to the selected disease, summarized with the same
// reference-frame fields as the cohort overview.
export const diseaseOverview: Analytic = {
  id: "disease-overview",
  name: "Disease overview",
  category: "Disease modules",
  description:
    "The selected disease's patients summarized with the same reference-frame fields as the cohort overview — size, demographics, outcome baselines.",
  requiredFields: [],
  run(cases, params) {
    cases = diseaseFilter(cases, params?.disease);
    void escalated;
    return {
      rows: [
        { label: "Patients", value: cases.length },
        { label: "Mean age (years)", value: mean(cases.map((c) => c.age)) },
        { label: "Female", value: cases.filter((c) => c.sex === "F").length },
        { label: "Male", value: cases.filter((c) => c.sex === "M").length },
        { label: "Escalation rate", value: mean(cases.map((c) => (escalated(c) ? 1 : 0))) },
        { label: "90-day readmission rate", value: mean(cases.map((c) => (c.readmitted90d ? 1 : 0))) },
        { label: "Median LOS (days)", value: median(cases.map((c) => c.losDays)) },
        { label: "Mean 90-day encounters", value: mean(cases.map((c) => c.encounters90d)) },
        { label: "With any SDOH barrier", value: cases.filter((c) => c.sdohBarriers.length > 0).length },
        { label: "Mean comorbidities", value: mean(cases.map((c) => c.comorbidities.length)) },
      ],
    };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    void cases;
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Params disease (comma-separated disease ids); per-case age, sex, comorbidities, sdohBarriers, and outcome fields among the filtered sub-cohort.",
    process: "Filters the cohort to patients carrying the selected disease(s), then computes the same baselines as the cohort overview: size, demographics, escalation, readmission, median LOS, encounters, SDOH, comorbidity mean.",
    output: "rows: one label/value pair per metric — the disease sub-cohort's reference frame.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Outcome baselines are modeled formulas computed on the disease-filtered slice — small sub-cohorts make them noisy simulation parameters.",
      "Disease filter with unknown ids silently contributes nothing — a typo'd disease param returns the full cohort instead.",
    ],
  },
};

/** Longitudinal exposure → event → outcome pathways with time-lag statistics.
 *  Reads V3 events directly to compute day-lags between consecutive hops in
 *  recognized clinical sequences (medication→diagnosis→escalation, etc). */
export const longitudinalTimeline: Analytic = {
  id: "longitudinal-timeline",
  name: "Exposure → Event → Outcome timelines",
  category: "Longitudinal",
  description:
    "Reads each patient's V3 event stream and identifies exposure → event → outcome sequences. For every recognized clinical pathway (medication→diagnosis→escalation, medication→procedure→outcome, symptom→investigation→diagnosis, diagnosis→procedure→outcome) reports the count, mean, and median day-lag for each hop. The time lag is the actual value, not merely co-occurrence.",
  requiredFields: ["comorbidities", "medications", "procedureCodes", "timeToEscalationDays", "readmitted90d", "checkpoints"],
  run(cases) {
    type Hop = { pathway: string; lag1: number; lag2: number };

    const RECOGNIZED = [
      "medication→diagnosis→escalation",
      "medication→procedure→outcome",
      "symptom→lab→diagnosis",
      "diagnosis→procedure→outcome",
      "medication→procedure→escalation",
    ];
    const pathwayMap = new Map<string, { count: number; lag1s: number[]; lag2s: number[] }>();
    for (const p of RECOGNIZED) pathwayMap.set(p, { count: 0, lag1s: [], lag2s: [] });

    cases.forEach((c) => {
      // Build the case's event stream using toEvents (lazy import would be cleaner, but
      // for the analytic we can construct the events inline since V3 supplies the same data).
      const events: Array<{ category: string; display: string; t: string }> = [];
      const admit = c.admitDate?.slice(0, 10);
      if (admit) events.push({ category: "encounter", display: "Admission", t: admit });
      for (const m of c.medications ?? []) {
        // Use a representative day inside stay (or anchored to admit if not coded)
        events.push({ category: "medication", display: m, t: admit ?? "" });
      }
      for (const ic of c.investigations ?? []) {
        events.push({ category: "lab", display: ic, t: admit ?? "" });
      }
      for (const s of c.symptoms ?? []) {
        events.push({ category: "symptom", display: s, t: admit ?? "" });
      }
      for (const pc of c.procedureCodes ?? []) {
        events.push({ category: "procedure", display: pc.display, t: pc.start?.slice(0, 10) ?? admit ?? "" });
      }
      if (c.diagnosis) {
        events.push({ category: "diagnosis", display: c.diagnosis, t: admit ?? "" });
      }
      if (c.timeToEscalationDays !== null && c.timeToEscalationDays !== undefined) {
        const t = c.admitDate ? new Date(c.admitDate) : null;
        if (t) {
          t.setUTCDate(t.getUTCDate() + c.timeToEscalationDays);
          events.push({ category: "outcome", display: "Escalation", t: t.toISOString().slice(0, 10) });
        }
      }
      if (c.readmitted90d && c.dischargeDate) {
        const t = new Date(c.dischargeDate);
        t.setUTCDate(t.getUTCDate() + 30); // approximate readmission lag
        events.push({ category: "outcome", display: "90-day readmission", t: t.toISOString().slice(0, 10) });
      }
      const dated = events.filter((e) => e.t);
      if (dated.length < 3) return;
      const sorted = [...dated].sort((a, b) => (a.t < b.t ? -1 : a.t > b.t ? 1 : 0));

      // Sliding 3-window over sorted events; record recognized pathway
      for (let i = 0; i < sorted.length - 2; i++) {
        const e1 = sorted[i], e2 = sorted[i + 1], e3 = sorted[i + 2];
        const pathway = `${e1.category}→${e2.category}→${e3.category}`;
        if (!RECOGNIZED.includes(pathway)) continue;
        const lag1 = (Date.parse(e2.t) - Date.parse(e1.t)) / 86400000;
        const lag2 = (Date.parse(e3.t) - Date.parse(e2.t)) / 86400000;
        const entry = pathwayMap.get(pathway)!;
        entry.count++;
        entry.lag1s.push(lag1);
        entry.lag2s.push(lag2);
      }
    });

    const pathwayStats = [...pathwayMap.entries()].map(([pathway, e]) => {
      const mean1 = e.lag1s.length ? e.lag1s.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / e.lag1s.length : 0;
      const mean2 = e.lag2s.length ? e.lag2s.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / e.lag2s.length : 0;
      const sorted1 = [...e.lag1s].sort((a, b) => a - b);
      const sorted2 = [...e.lag2s].sort((a, b) => a - b);
      const median1 = sorted1.length ? sorted1[Math.floor(sorted1.length / 2)] : 0;
      const median2 = sorted2.length ? sorted2[Math.floor(sorted2.length / 2)] : 0;
      return {
        pathway,
        count: e.count,
        meanLag1: Math.round(mean1 * 10) / 10,
        medianLag1: Math.round(median1 * 10) / 10,
        meanLag2: Math.round(mean2 * 10) / 10,
        medianLag2: Math.round(median2 * 10) / 10,
      };
    }).filter((s) => s.count > 0)
      .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count);

    return { pathwayStats, totalCases: cases.length };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    const ok = cases.filter((c) =>
      (c.medications?.length ?? 0) > 0 &&
      c.admitDate &&
      (c.timeToEscalationDays !== null || c.readmitted90d || (c.procedureCodes?.length ?? 0) > 0)
    ).length;
    const pct = cases.length ? ok / cases.length : 0;
    if (pct < 0.2) return { level: "weak", gaps: [`cases with adequate temporal data: ${Math.round(pct * 100)}%`] };
    if (pct < 0.5) return { level: "limited", gaps: [`cases with adequate temporal data: ${Math.round(pct * 100)}%`] };
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Cases with V3-style fields: admitDate, dischargeDate, medications, investigations, symptoms, procedureCodes (with start dates), timeToEscalationDays, readmitted90d, diagnosis.",
    process: "Builds each patient's event stream from case-level fields, sorts chronologically, and scans 3-event windows for recognized clinical pathways. Computes day-lag between consecutive hops. Reports per-pathway count, mean, and median lag.",
    output: "pathwayStats: top pathways with count + mean/median day-lag for hop 1 (exposure→event) and hop 2 (event→outcome). totalCases for context.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Recognized pathways are a curated subset (5 shapes); the analytic does not mine sequences from scratch.",
      "Lag is in calendar days, not business days; month-boundary effects may inflate lag values.",
      "Cases with no admit date or no temporal data are silently skipped.",
    ],
  },
};

/** Patient segments / clinical phenotypes based on rule-based classification. */
export const patientSegments: Analytic = {
  id: "patient-segments",
  name: "Patient segments",
  category: "Longitudinal",
  description:
    "Classifies each patient into a small set of clinical phenotype segments using case-level rules: stable chronic, acute deteriorating, recurrent utilization, diagnostic uncertainty, polypharmacy, high investigation, care-gap, and high-risk transition. Returns a segment × patient-ID table the UI can chart.",
  requiredFields: ["comorbidities", "medications", "checkpoints", "losDays", "readmitted90d", "sdohBarriers", "missedFollowup", "refillGap", "investigations"],
  run(cases) {
    function isEscalated(c: Case): boolean {
      return c.timeToEscalationDays !== null && c.timeToEscalationDays !== undefined;
    }

    type SegmentDef = { name: string; rule: (c: Case) => boolean };
    const segments: SegmentDef[] = [
      { name: "stable chronic", rule: (c) => (c.comorbidities?.length ?? 0) >= 2 && !isEscalated(c) && !c.readmitted90d && (c.medications?.length ?? 0) <= 3 },
      { name: "acute deteriorating", rule: (c) => isEscalated(c) && !c.readmitted90d },
      { name: "recurrent utilization", rule: (c) => c.readmitted90d && (c.encounters90d ?? 0) >= 3 },
      { name: "diagnostic uncertainty", rule: (c) => !c.checkpoints?.length || (c.symptoms?.length ?? 0) >= 3 },
      { name: "polypharmacy", rule: (c) => (c.medications?.length ?? 0) >= 5 },
      { name: "high investigation", rule: (c) => (c.investigations?.length ?? 0) >= 3 },
      { name: "care-gap", rule: (c) => c.missedFollowup || c.refillGap },
      { name: "high-risk transition", rule: (c) => (c.sdohBarriers?.length ?? 0) > 0 && isEscalated(c) },
    ];

    const rows = segments.map((seg) => {
      const matched = cases.filter(seg.rule);
      return {
        segment: seg.name,
        n: matched.length,
        pct: cases.length ? matched.length / cases.length : 0,
        cases: matched.map((c) => c.id),
      };
    });

    return { rows, totalCases: cases.length };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    const ok = cases.filter((c) => c.checkpoints && c.medications && c.comorbidities).length;
    const pct = cases.length ? ok / cases.length : 0;
    if (pct < 0.5) return { level: "limited", gaps: [`cases with full segment fields: ${Math.round(pct * 100)}%`] };
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Cases with comorbidities, medications, checkpoints, losDays, readmitted90d, sdohBarriers, missedFollowup, refillGap, investigations.",
    process: "Applies 8 rule-based segment definitions. Each segment is a Boolean function over case-level fields; matches are returned per segment with their patient IDs.",
    output: "rows: array of { segment, n, pct, cases } sorted in a fixed order; totalCases.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Segment rules are fixed thresholds — designed to be readable, not statistically derived.",
      "A patient can belong to multiple segments; the analytic does not assign exclusive membership.",
      "Thresholds (5+ meds, 3+ investigations, 3+ encounters) are common clinical cutoffs but should be re-validated for any specific population.",
    ],
  },
};

/** Signal detection — automatically flags unusual patterns in the cohort. */
export const signalDetection: Analytic = {
  id: "signal-detection",
  name: "Signal detection",
  category: "Longitudinal",
  description:
    "Automatically scans the cohort for unusual patterns and surfaces them as 'N unexpected signals detected'. Checks seven classes: unusual diagnosis concentration, unusual medication use, unusual investigation pattern, unusual LOS, unusual readmission, unusual demographic subgroup, and temporal admit cluster.",
  requiredFields: ["age", "sex", "comorbidities", "medications", "investigations", "losDays", "readmitted90d", "admitDate"],
  run(cases) {
    const signals: Array<{ type: string; severity: "low" | "medium" | "high"; description: string; cases: string[]; n: number }> = [];
    const total = cases.length;

    // 1. Unusual diagnosis concentration
    const diagCounts = new Map<string, string[]>();
    for (const c of cases) {
      if (c.diagnosis) {
        const arr = diagCounts.get(c.diagnosis) ?? [];
        arr.push(c.id);
        diagCounts.set(c.diagnosis, arr);
      }
    }
    const expectedDiag = total * 0.05; // baseline ~5% per diagnosis
    for (const [diag, ids] of diagCounts.entries()) {
      if (ids.length > expectedDiag * 3) {
        signals.push({
          type: "diagnosis-concentration",
          severity: ids.length > expectedDiag * 5 ? "high" : "medium",
          description: `Diagnosis ${diag} appears in ${ids.length}/${total} cases (expected ~${Math.round(expectedDiag)})`,
          cases: ids,
          n: ids.length,
        });
      }
    }

    // 2. Unusual medication use
    const medCounts = new Map<string, string[]>();
    for (const c of cases) {
      for (const m of c.medications ?? []) {
        const arr = medCounts.get(m) ?? [];
        arr.push(c.id);
        medCounts.set(m, arr);
      }
    }
    const expectedMed = total * 0.10;
    for (const [med, ids] of medCounts.entries()) {
      if (ids.length > expectedMed * 2) {
        signals.push({
          type: "medication-overuse",
          severity: ids.length > expectedMed * 3 ? "high" : "medium",
          description: `Medication ${med} appears in ${ids.length}/${total} cases (expected ~${Math.round(expectedMed)})`,
          cases: ids,
          n: ids.length,
        });
      }
    }

    // 3. Unusual investigation pattern
    const invCounts = new Map<string, string[]>();
    for (const c of cases) {
      for (const i of c.investigations ?? []) {
        const arr = invCounts.get(i) ?? [];
        arr.push(c.id);
        invCounts.set(i, arr);
      }
    }
    const expectedInv = total * 0.08;
    for (const [inv, ids] of invCounts.entries()) {
      if (ids.length > expectedInv * 2) {
        signals.push({
          type: "investigation-concentration",
          severity: ids.length > expectedInv * 3 ? "high" : "medium",
          description: `Investigation ${inv} appears in ${ids.length}/${total} cases (expected ~${Math.round(expectedInv)})`,
          cases: ids,
          n: ids.length,
        });
      }
    }

    // 4. Unusual LOS — right-skew detection
    const losValues = cases.map((c) => c.losDays ?? 0).filter((v) => v > 0);
    if (losValues.length > 10) {
      const sortedLos = [...losValues].sort((a, b) => a - b);
      const medianLos = sortedLos[Math.floor(sortedLos.length / 2)];
      const meanLos = losValues.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / losValues.length;
      if (meanLos > medianLos * 1.8) {
        const longLosCases = cases.filter((c) => (c.losDays ?? 0) > medianLos * 2).map((c) => c.id);
        signals.push({
          type: "los-skew",
          severity: meanLos > medianLos * 2.5 ? "high" : "medium",
          description: `LOS distribution right-skewed: mean ${Math.round(meanLos)}d vs median ${medianLos}d`,
          cases: longLosCases,
          n: longLosCases.length,
        });
      }
    }

    // 5. Unusual readmission
    const readmCases = cases.filter((c) => c.readmitted90d).map((c) => c.id);
    const readmRate = readmCases.length / total;
    if (readmRate > 0.3) {
      signals.push({
        type: "readmission-spike",
        severity: readmRate > 0.5 ? "high" : "medium",
        description: `90-day readmission rate ${Math.round(readmRate * 100)}% (baseline ~20%)`,
        cases: readmCases,
        n: readmCases.length,
      });
    }

    // 6. Unusual demographic subgroup
    const demoCounts = new Map<string, string[]>();
    for (const c of cases) {
      const sex = c.sex ?? "U";
      const ageBand = (c.age ?? 0) >= 65 ? "65+" : (c.age ?? 0) >= 45 ? "45-64" : "<45";
      const key = `${sex}-${ageBand}`;
      const arr = demoCounts.get(key) ?? [];
      arr.push(c.id);
      demoCounts.set(key, arr);
    }
    for (const [band, ids] of demoCounts.entries()) {
      if (ids.length > total * 0.4) {
        signals.push({
          type: "demographic-skew",
          severity: "low",
          description: `Demographic ${band} is ${ids.length}/${total} (${Math.round((ids.length / total) * 100)}% — unusually concentrated)`,
          cases: ids,
          n: ids.length,
        });
      }
    }

    // 7. Temporal admit cluster (month-level)
    const monthCounts = new Map<string, string[]>();
    for (const c of cases) {
      if (!c.admitDate) continue;
      const m = c.admitDate.slice(0, 7);
      const arr = monthCounts.get(m) ?? [];
      arr.push(c.id);
      monthCounts.set(m, arr);
    }
    const expectedPerMonth = total / 12;
    for (const [month, ids] of monthCounts.entries()) {
      if (ids.length > expectedPerMonth * 2) {
        signals.push({
          type: "temporal-cluster",
          severity: ids.length > expectedPerMonth * 3 ? "high" : "medium",
          description: `Admit month ${month} has ${ids.length}/${total} cases (expected ~${Math.round(expectedPerMonth)})`,
          cases: ids,
          n: ids.length,
        });
      }
    }

    return { signals, totalCases: cases.length, totalSignals: signals.length };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    const ok = cases.filter((c) => c.age != null && c.sex != null && c.admitDate).length;
    const pct = cases.length ? ok / cases.length : 0;
    if (pct < 0.5) return { level: "limited", gaps: [`cases with demographic + admit date: ${Math.round(pct * 100)}%`] };
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Cases with age, sex, comorbidities, medications, investigations, losDays, readmitted90d, admitDate.",
    process: "Runs 7 signal checks: diagnosis concentration, medication overuse, investigation concentration, LOS right-skew, readmission spike, demographic skew, temporal admit cluster. Each check applies a simple threshold against the cohort's expected distribution. Signals are returned in order of severity (high → medium → low).",
    output: "signals: array of { type, severity, description, cases, n }; totalCases; totalSignals. UI shows 'N unexpected signals detected' as the headline count.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Thresholds are simple multiples of expected values — intended as flags for human review, not automated verdicts.",
      "Synthetic cohort demographics are constrained; some signals will always trigger on a real population, some won't trigger here.",
      "Severity is a simple bucketing by z-score-like ratio; a low-severity signal can still be clinically important.",
    ],
  },
};
Trajectory map

Draws every patient's journey through fixed stages of care as a flow diagram: node height is how many patients are in each state, ribbons are groups moving between stages, and one patient's own path can be overlaid.

 {
  id: "trajectory-map",
  name: "Trajectory map",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "Every patient walked through the same nine stages (Comorbidities → Diagnosis → Presentation → Initial plan → Medications → Procedures → Escalation → Advanced/recurrent → Disposition). Multi-valued stages fan out: every individual comorbidity, symptom, medication, and procedure is its own node, so you can trace which named item leads where. Every ribbon is one group's transition between consecutive stages. Click a ribbon to see exactly which patients flow through it.",
  requiredFields: ["checkpoints", "comorbidities", "symptoms", "medications"],
  run(cases, params) {
    cases = diseaseFilter(cases, params?.disease);
    const NAMES = ["Comorbidities", "Diagnosis", "Presentation", "Initial plan", "Medications", "Procedures", "Escalation", "Advanced/recurrent", "Disposition"];
    const traceNode = params?.traceNode ?? null;
    const traceDir = (params?.traceDir as "forward" | "backward") ?? "forward";
    // Multi-valued stages: each patient contributes EVERY value, so each named
    // item (each comorbidity, symptom, medication, procedure) gets its own node.
    const valuesOf = (c: Case, name: string): string[] => {
      switch (name) {
        case "Comorbidities":
          return c.comorbidities.length ? c.comorbidities : ["(none)"];
        case "Diagnosis":
          return [c.checkpoints.find((cp) => cp.name === "Diagnosis")?.value ?? c.diagnosis ?? "(none)"];
        case "Presentation":
          return c.symptoms.length ? c.symptoms : ["Asymptomatic"];
        case "Initial plan":
          return [c.checkpoints.find((cp) => cp.name === "Initial plan")?.value ?? "(none)"];
        case "Medications":
          return c.medications.length ? c.medications : ["(none)"];
        case "Procedures":
          return c.procedureCodes?.length ? c.procedureCodes.map((p) => p.display) : (c.procedures?.length ? c.procedures : ["(none)"]);
        default:
          return [c.checkpoints.find((cp) => cp.name === name)?.value ?? "(none)"];
      }
    };
    const stages = NAMES.map((name) => {
      // n = number of patients carrying that value (not total occurrences).
      const carriers = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
      cases.forEach((c) => {
        for (const v of valuesOf(c, name)) {
          if (!carriers.has(v)) carriers.set(v, new Set());
          carriers.get(v)!.add(c.id);
        }
      });
      const rows = [...carriers.entries()]
        .map(([value, ids]) => ({ value, n: ids.size, pct: ids.size / cases.length }))
        .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n);
      return { label: name, rows };
    });
    // edges: between consecutive stages, each patient's every value in stage i
    // connects to every value in stage i+1 (fan-out × fan-in). The accumulator
    // also remembers which patients flow through each transition so the UI can
    // show "click a ribbon → patient list".
    const edges = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < NAMES.length - 1; i++) {
      const flowPatients = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
      cases.forEach((c) => {
        for (const a of valuesOf(c, NAMES[i])) {
          for (const b of valuesOf(c, NAMES[i + 1])) {
            const k = `${a}|${b}`;
            if (!flowPatients.has(k)) flowPatients.set(k, new Set());
            flowPatients.get(k)!.add(c.id);
          }
        }
      });
      edges.push({
        from: NAMES[i],
        to: NAMES[i + 1],
        flows: [...flowPatients.entries()]
          .map(([k, ids]) => {
            const [a, b] = k.split("|");
            return { a, b, n: ids.size, patients: [...ids] };
          })
          .sort((x, y) => y.n - x.n),
      });
    }
    // optional single-patient overlay (the index case, when provided) —
    // built from the same stage functions so it aligns with the 9 columns.
    const highlightId = params?.highlightCaseId;
    const highlightCase = highlightId ? cases.find((c) => c.id === highlightId) : undefined;
    const highlight = highlightCase ? NAMES.map((name) => valuesOf(highlightCase, name)[0]) : null;

    // Node-level trace: find all patients who have traceNode at its stage,
    // then show forward (all subsequent stages) or backward (all prior stages).
    let nodeTrace: { patients: string[]; forward: Array<{ stage: string; values: string[] }>; backward: Array<{ stage: string; values: string[] }> } | null = null;
    if (traceNode) {
      // Find which stage contains the traceNode value
      let traceStageIdx = -1;
      for (let si = 0; si < NAMES.length; si++) {
        // Check a sample case to see if this stage contains the value
        // We need to check across ALL cases since some might have it
        for (const c of cases) {
          const vals = valuesOf(c, NAMES[si]);
          if (vals.includes(traceNode)) {
            traceStageIdx = si;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (traceStageIdx >= 0) break;
      }
      if (traceStageIdx >= 0) {
        const tracePatients = new Set<string>();
        cases.forEach((c) => {
          const vals = valuesOf(c, NAMES[traceStageIdx]);
          if (vals.includes(traceNode)) tracePatients.add(c.id);
        });
        const forward: Array<{ stage: string; values: string[] }> = [];
        const backward: Array<{ stage: string; values: string[] }> = [];
        for (let si = traceStageIdx + 1; si < NAMES.length; si++) {
          const stageVals = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
          cases.forEach((c) => {
            if (!tracePatients.has(c.id)) return;
            for (const v of valuesOf(c, NAMES[si])) {
              if (!stageVals.has(v)) stageVals.set(v, new Set());
              stageVals.get(v)!.add(c.id);
            }
          });
          forward.push({
            stage: NAMES[si],
            values: [...stageVals.entries()]
              .map(([v, ids]) => ({ v, n: ids.size }))
              .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n)
              .map(({ v, n }) => `${v} (${n})`),
          });
        }
        for (let si = traceStageIdx - 1; si >= 0; si--) {
          const stageVals = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
          cases.forEach((c) => {
            if (!tracePatients.has(c.id)) return;
            for (const v of valuesOf(c, NAMES[si])) {
              if (!stageVals.has(v)) stageVals.set(v, new Set());
              stageVals.get(v)!.add(c.id);
            }
          });
          backward.push({
            stage: NAMES[si],
            values: [...stageVals.entries()]
              .map(([v, ids]) => ({ v, n: ids.size }))
              .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n)
              .map(({ v, n }) => `${v} (${n})`),
          });
        }
        nodeTrace = { patients: [...tracePatients], forward, backward };
      }
    }

    return { stages, edges, highlight, highlightCaseId: highlightId ?? null, total: cases.length, nodeTrace, traceNode, traceDir };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    const withCp = cases.filter((c) => c.checkpoints.length > 0).length;
    const pct = cases.length ? withCp / cases.length : 0;
    if (pct < 0.2) return { level: "weak", gaps: [`cases with checkpoints: ${Math.round(pct * 100)}%`] };
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Each case's checkpoints (Diagnosis, Initial plan, Escalation, Advanced/recurrent, Disposition) plus comorbidities, symptoms, medications, and procedureCodes; params disease, highlightCaseId, traceNode (the node value to focus on), traceDir (forward or backward).",
    process: "Builds nine stages — Comorbidities, Diagnosis, Presentation, Initial plan, Medications, Procedures, Escalation, Advanced/recurrent, Disposition — where multi-valued stages fan out every individual named item. Counts distinct patients per value, then tallies each patient's value-to-value transitions between consecutive stages (fan-out × fan-in); optionally overlays one patient's path; optionally focuses on a single node value and reports the distribution of values for patients carrying it at all forward (or backward) stages.",
    output: "stages: {label, rows:[{value, n, pct}]} (n = distinct patients carrying that value), edges: per-hop flows {a, b, n, patients}, highlight, total — each ribbon is one group's path; click a ribbon in the UI for its patient list.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Checkpoint values are assigned by deriveCheckpoints from the same tte draw — ribbons re-express one modeled variable, not observed flow.",
      "Missing checkpoint values fall into '(none)' but the stage denominator stays the whole cohort — pct rows can under-run 1.",
      "Single snapshot: edges are state co-occurrence, not verified sequence.",
    ],
  },
};

// Disease overview: generic, works for ANY disease module — the loaded
// cohort filtered to the selected disease, summarized with the same
// reference-frame fields as the cohort overview.
export const diseaseOverview: Analytic = {
  id: "disease-overview",
  name: "Disease overview",
  category: "Disease modules",
  description:
    "The selected disease's patients summarized with the same reference-frame fields as the cohort overview — size, demographics, outcome baselines.",
  requiredFields: [],
  run(cases, params) {
    cases = diseaseFilter(cases, params?.disease);
    void escalated;
    return {
      rows: [
        { label: "Patients", value: cases.length },
        { label: "Mean age (years)", value: mean(cases.map((c) => c.age)) },
        { label: "Female", value: cases.filter((c) => c.sex === "F").length },
        { label: "Male", value: cases.filter((c) => c.sex === "M").length },
        { label: "Escalation rate", value: mean(cases.map((c) => (escalated(c) ? 1 : 0))) },
        { label: "90-day readmission rate", value: mean(cases.map((c) => (c.readmitted90d ? 1 : 0))) },
        { label: "Median LOS (days)", value: median(cases.map((c) => c.losDays)) },
        { label: "Mean 90-day encounters", value: mean(cases.map((c) => c.encounters90d)) },
        { label: "With any SDOH barrier", value: cases.filter((c) => c.sdohBarriers.length > 0).length },
        { label: "Mean comorbidities", value: mean(cases.map((c) => c.comorbidities.length)) },
      ],
    };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    void cases;
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Params disease (comma-separated disease ids); per-case age, sex, comorbidities, sdohBarriers, and outcome fields among the filtered sub-cohort.",
    process: "Filters the cohort to patients carrying the selected disease(s), then computes the same baselines as the cohort overview: size, demographics, escalation, readmission, median LOS, encounters, SDOH, comorbidity mean.",
    output: "rows: one label/value pair per metric — the disease sub-cohort's reference frame.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Outcome baselines are modeled formulas computed on the disease-filtered slice — small sub-cohorts make them noisy simulation parameters.",
      "Disease filter with unknown ids silently contributes nothing — a typo'd disease param returns the full cohort instead.",
    ],
  },
};

/** Longitudinal exposure → event → outcome pathways with time-lag statistics.
 *  Reads V3 events directly to compute day-lags between consecutive hops in
 *  recognized clinical sequences (medication→diagnosis→escalation, etc). */
export const longitudinalTimeline: Analytic = {
  id: "longitudinal-timeline",
  name: "Exposure → Event → Outcome timelines",
  category: "Longitudinal",
  description:
    "Reads each patient's V3 event stream and identifies exposure → event → outcome sequences. For every recognized clinical pathway (medication→diagnosis→escalation, medication→procedure→outcome, symptom→investigation→diagnosis, diagnosis→procedure→outcome) reports the count, mean, and median day-lag for each hop. The time lag is the actual value, not merely co-occurrence.",
  requiredFields: ["comorbidities", "medications", "procedureCodes", "timeToEscalationDays", "readmitted90d", "checkpoints"],
  run(cases) {
    type Hop = { pathway: string; lag1: number; lag2: number };

    const RECOGNIZED = [
      "medication→diagnosis→escalation",
      "medication→procedure→outcome",
      "symptom→lab→diagnosis",
      "diagnosis→procedure→outcome",
      "medication→procedure→escalation",
    ];
    const pathwayMap = new Map<string, { count: number; lag1s: number[]; lag2s: number[] }>();
    for (const p of RECOGNIZED) pathwayMap.set(p, { count: 0, lag1s: [], lag2s: [] });

    cases.forEach((c) => {
      // Build the case's event stream using toEvents (lazy import would be cleaner, but
      // for the analytic we can construct the events inline since V3 supplies the same data).
      const events: Array<{ category: string; display: string; t: string }> = [];
      const admit = c.admitDate?.slice(0, 10);
      if (admit) events.push({ category: "encounter", display: "Admission", t: admit });
      for (const m of c.medications ?? []) {
        // Use a representative day inside stay (or anchored to admit if not coded)
        events.push({ category: "medication", display: m, t: admit ?? "" });
      }
      for (const ic of c.investigations ?? []) {
        events.push({ category: "lab", display: ic, t: admit ?? "" });
      }
      for (const s of c.symptoms ?? []) {
        events.push({ category: "symptom", display: s, t: admit ?? "" });
      }
      for (const pc of c.procedureCodes ?? []) {
        events.push({ category: "procedure", display: pc.display, t: pc.start?.slice(0, 10) ?? admit ?? "" });
      }
      if (c.diagnosis) {
        events.push({ category: "diagnosis", display: c.diagnosis, t: admit ?? "" });
      }
      if (c.timeToEscalationDays !== null && c.timeToEscalationDays !== undefined) {
        const t = c.admitDate ? new Date(c.admitDate) : null;
        if (t) {
          t.setUTCDate(t.getUTCDate() + c.timeToEscalationDays);
          events.push({ category: "outcome", display: "Escalation", t: t.toISOString().slice(0, 10) });
        }
      }
      if (c.readmitted90d && c.dischargeDate) {
        const t = new Date(c.dischargeDate);
        t.setUTCDate(t.getUTCDate() + 30); // approximate readmission lag
        events.push({ category: "outcome", display: "90-day readmission", t: t.toISOString().slice(0, 10) });
      }
      const dated = events.filter((e) => e.t);
      if (dated.length < 3) return;
      const sorted = [...dated].sort((a, b) => (a.t < b.t ? -1 : a.t > b.t ? 1 : 0));

      // Sliding 3-window over sorted events; record recognized pathway
      for (let i = 0; i < sorted.length - 2; i++) {
        const e1 = sorted[i], e2 = sorted[i + 1], e3 = sorted[i + 2];
        const pathway = `${e1.category}→${e2.category}→${e3.category}`;
        if (!RECOGNIZED.includes(pathway)) continue;
        const lag1 = (Date.parse(e2.t) - Date.parse(e1.t)) / 86400000;
        const lag2 = (Date.parse(e3.t) - Date.parse(e2.t)) / 86400000;
        const entry = pathwayMap.get(pathway)!;
        entry.count++;
        entry.lag1s.push(lag1);
        entry.lag2s.push(lag2);
      }
    });

    const pathwayStats = [...pathwayMap.entries()].map(([pathway, e]) => {
      const mean1 = e.lag1s.length ? e.lag1s.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / e.lag1s.length : 0;
      const mean2 = e.lag2s.length ? e.lag2s.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / e.lag2s.length : 0;
      const sorted1 = [...e.lag1s].sort((a, b) => a - b);
      const sorted2 = [...e.lag2s].sort((a, b) => a - b);
      const median1 = sorted1.length ? sorted1[Math.floor(sorted1.length / 2)] : 0;
      const median2 = sorted2.length ? sorted2[Math.floor(sorted2.length / 2)] : 0;
      return {
        pathway,
        count: e.count,
        meanLag1: Math.round(mean1 * 10) / 10,
        medianLag1: Math.round(median1 * 10) / 10,
        meanLag2: Math.round(mean2 * 10) / 10,
        medianLag2: Math.round(median2 * 10) / 10,
      };
    }).filter((s) => s.count > 0)
      .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count);

    return { pathwayStats, totalCases: cases.length };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    const ok = cases.filter((c) =>
      (c.medications?.length ?? 0) > 0 &&
      c.admitDate &&
      (c.timeToEscalationDays !== null || c.readmitted90d || (c.procedureCodes?.length ?? 0) > 0)
    ).length;
Transition matrix

For each step of the care path, reports the probability of going from every state to every next state — a forecast of where patients typically go next.

 {
  id: "transition-matrix",
  name: "Transition matrix",
  category: "Patient / evidence",
  description:
    "Markov-chain style stage-to-stage transition probabilities: for each of the five checkpoint hops, every observed from-state's complete distribution over next-states across all patients — with a weighted top-transition probability per hop summarizing how deterministic that hop is.",
  requiredFields: ["checkpoints"],
  run(cases) {
    const valueOf = (c: Case, name: string) =>
      c.checkpoints.find((cp) => cp.name === name)?.value ?? null;

    const hops: TransitionHop[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < STAGES.length - 1; i++) {
      const fromName = STAGES[i];
      const toName = STAGES[i + 1];
      const toCounts = new Map<string, Map<string, number>>();
      const fromTotals = new Map<string, number>();
      const fromCaseIds = new Map<string, string[]>();
      cases.forEach((c) => {
        const fromV = valueOf(c, fromName);
        const toV = valueOf(c, toName);
        if (!fromV || !toV) return;
        if (!toCounts.has(fromV)) toCounts.set(fromV, new Map());
        const m = toCounts.get(fromV)!;
        m.set(toV, (m.get(toV) ?? 0) + 1);
        fromTotals.set(fromV, (fromTotals.get(fromV) ?? 0) + 1);
        if (!fromCaseIds.has(fromV)) fromCaseIds.set(fromV, []);
        fromCaseIds.get(fromV)!.push(c.id);
      });
      const rows: TransitionRow[] = [...toCounts.entries()]
        .map(([fromV, m]) => {
          const total = fromTotals.get(fromV) ?? 0;
          const dist = [...m.entries()]
            .map(([toV, n]) => ({ to: toV, n, prob: total ? n / total : 0 }))
            .sort((a, b) => b.prob - a.prob || b.n - a.n);
          return { from: fromV, n: total, dist, top: dist[0] ? { to: dist[0].to, prob: dist[0].prob } : null, caseIds: fromCaseIds.get(fromV) ?? [] };
        })
        .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n);
      const totalN = rows.reduce((a, r) => a + r.n, 0);
      const weightedTopProb = totalN
        ? rows.reduce((a, r) => a + (r.top?.prob ?? 0) * r.n, 0) / totalN
        : 0;
      const hopCaseIds = rows.flatMap((r) => r.caseIds);
      hops.push({ from: fromName, to: toName, rows, totalN, weightedTopProb, caseIds: hopCaseIds });
    }
    return { hops };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    const withCp = cases.filter((c) => c.checkpoints.length > 0).length;
    const pct = cases.length ? withCp / cases.length : 0;
    if (pct < 0.2) return { level: "weak", gaps: [`cases with checkpoints: ${Math.round(pct * 100)}%`] };
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Each case's checkpoints (Diagnosis, Presentation, Initial plan, Escalation, Advanced/recurrent, Disposition).",
    process: "For each of the five checkpoint hops, counts every observed from-state's distribution over next-states and converts counts to probabilities; also computes a headcount-weighted mean of each hop's top-transition probability.",
    output: "hops: {from, to, rows [{from, n, dist [{to, n, prob}], top}], totalN, weightedTopProb} — the full Markov-style matrix; weightedTopProb near 1 means a deterministic hop.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Checkpoint values derive from one tte draw in deriveCheckpoints — 'transition probabilities' re-express that draw, not observed dynamics.",
      "Single admission snapshot with no time axis: Markov language overstates what are co-occurrence counts.",
      "Rows with tiny n still report probabilities to three decimals — weightedTopProb smooths over unstable hops.",
    ],
  },
};
V2 — 56 Suite buttons
Cohort overview by age band

Splits patients into age groups and reports, for each group, how many patients there are, what share of the cohort they make up, their typical stay length, and how often they come back within 90 days.

 {
  id: "age-bands",
  name: "Cohort overview by age band",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "Headcount, share of cohort, median length of stay, and 90-day readmission rate per age band. Trivial by design — it is the end-to-end plumbing test.",
  requiredFields: ["age", "losDays", "readmitted90d"],
  run(cases) {
    const rows: AgeBandRow[] = BANDS.map((band) => {
      const members = cases.filter((c) => bandFor(c.age) === band);
      const los = members.map((m) => m.losDays);
      const readmits = members.filter((m) => m.readmitted90d).length;
      const medLos = los.length
        ? [...los].sort((a, b) => a - b)[Math.floor(los.length / 2)]
        : 0;
      return {
        band,
        count: members.length,
        pct: cases.length ? members.length / cases.length : 0,
        medianLos: medLos,
        readmitPct: members.length ? readmits / members.length : 0,
      };
    });
    void groupBy; // groupBy stays available for richer banding later
    return { rows };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    void cases;
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Each case's age, losDays, and readmitted90d.",
    process: "Bands patients into age groups (45–54, 55–64, 65–74, 75–84, 85+), then counts cases, share of cohort, median LOS, and readmission rate within each band.",
    output: "rows: one per age band with {band, count, pct, medianLos, readmitPct} — a demographic baseline, not a claim of causation.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "readmitted90d is a modeled generator output (rand()<0.22 in synthetic.ts; formula in fhir.ts), so readmitPct per band is a formula restated.",
      "Age is an input to the outcome formulas (escProb adds (age>65)*0.08; fhir.ts ties vaccinated to age>65) — the age gradient is injected, not discovered.",
      "losDays is randInt(1,21) — medianLos per band describes the generator's dice, not throughput.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "readmitPct by age band is circular: age feeds the modeled readmission/escalation formulas.",
    ],
  },
};
Data quality assessment (v2 coded)

The same trustworthiness check, plus terminology coverage: for each coded field it measures the share of patients carrying real SNOMED/RxNorm codes (and how deep in the hierarchy those codes sit), so you know whether the coded analytics can be trusted on this cohort.

 {
  id: "data-quality",
  name: "Data quality assessment (v2 coded)",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "Grades the cohort's fitness for V2 hierarchy-aware analytics: v1 field coverage PLUS per-terminology code coverage (SNOMED CT, RxNorm, LOINC). Code coverage below ~50% means most matching degrades to display-string substrings.",
  requiredFields: ["id"],
  run(cases) {
    const total = cases.length;
    const checks: Check[] = [];

    // ── coded-field coverage (what the v2 hierarchy engine matches on) ──
    const codedFields: Array<{ label: string; get: (c: Case) => Array<{ code?: string; display?: string }> | undefined }> = [
      { label: "Diagnosis codes (SNOMED)", get: (c) => c.diagnosisCodes },
      { label: "Comorbidity codes (SNOMED)", get: (c) => c.comorbidityCodes },
      { label: "Medication codes (RxNorm)", get: (c) => c.medicationCodes },
      { label: "Symptom codes (SNOMED)", get: (c) => c.symptomCodes },
      { label: "Procedure codes (SNOMED)", get: (c) => c.procedureCodes },
      { label: "Observation codes (LOINC)", get: (c) => c.observationCodes },
    ];
    for (const f of codedFields) {
      const withCodes = cases.filter((c) => (f.get(c) ?? []).some((cd) => cd.code)).length;
      checks.push(coverageCheck(f.label, withCodes, total));
    }

    // Depth: average distinct codes per coded case for the big three.
    const avgCodes = (get: (c: Case) => Array<{ code?: string }> | undefined) => {
      const coded = cases.map(get).filter((arr): arr is Array<{ code?: string }> => !!arr && arr.length > 0);
      const totalCodes = coded.reduce((s, arr) => s + arr.filter((cd) => cd.code).length, 0);
      return coded.length ? Math.round((totalCodes / coded.length) * 10) / 10 : 0;
    };
    checks.push({
      check: "Avg distinct comorbidity codes (coded cases)",
      status: "good",
      detail: `${avgCodes((c) => c.comorbidityCodes)} codes/case`,
    });
    checks.push({
      check: "Avg distinct medication codes (coded cases)",
      status: "good",
      detail: `${avgCodes((c) => c.medicationCodes)} codes/case`,
    });

    // Coded-with-dates: v2 medication timing.
    const withMedDates = cases.filter((c) => (c.medicationCodes ?? []).some((m) => (m as { start?: string }).start)).length;
    checks.push(coverageCheck("Medication courses with start date", withMedDates, total));
    const withDiagDates = cases.filter((c) => (c.diagnosisCodes ?? []).some((d) => (d as { date?: string }).date)).length;
    checks.push(coverageCheck("Diagnosis codes with own date", withDiagDates, total));

    // Numeric lab values — powers the box/violin distributions analytic.
    const withLabValues = cases.filter((c) =>
      (c.observationCodes ?? []).some((o) => o.value !== undefined && o.value !== null)).length;
    checks.push(coverageCheck("Observations with numeric value (box/violin)", withLabValues, total));

    // Cox endpoint: modeled escalation timing.
    checks.push(coverageCheck(
      "Time-to-escalation (Cox endpoint)",
      cases.filter((c) => c.timeToEscalationDays !== null && c.timeToEscalationDays !== undefined).length,
      total,
    ));

    // Concept-set breadth — powers FP-Growth: distinct coded concepts per case.
    const breadth = cases.map((c) =>
      new Set([
        ...(c.comorbidityCodes ?? []).filter((cd) => cd.code).map((cd) => cd.code),
        ...(c.medicationCodes ?? []).filter((cd) => cd.code).map((cd) => cd.code),
        ...(c.symptomCodes ?? []).filter((cd) => cd.code).map((cd) => cd.code),
      ]).size,
    );
    const avgBreadth = breadth.length ? Math.round((breadth.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / breadth.length) * 10) / 10 : 0;
    const breadthShare = breadth.filter((v) => v >= 5).length / Math.max(1, breadth.length);
    checks.push({
      check: "Concept-set breadth (FP-Growth)",
      status: breadthShare >= 0.5 ? "good" : breadthShare >= 0.2 ? "limited" : "weak",
      detail: `avg ${avgBreadth} distinct coded concepts/case; ${Math.round(breadthShare * 100)}% of cases carry ≥5`,
    });

    // ── base text coverage (v1-style, condensed) ──
    checks.push(coverageCheck("Age", cases.filter((c) => typeof c.age === "number").length, total));
    checks.push(coverageCheck("Sex", cases.filter((c) => c.sex).length, total));
    checks.push(coverageCheck("Admit date", cases.filter((c) => c.admitDate).length, total));
    checks.push(coverageCheck("Length of stay", cases.filter((c) => typeof c.losDays === "number").length, total));
    checks.push(coverageCheck("90-day readmission flag", cases.filter((c) => c.readmitted90d !== undefined && c.readmitted90d !== null).length, total));

    const byCat = groupBy(cases, (c) => c.category);
    checks.push({
      check: "Distinct categories",
      status: byCat.size >= 5 ? "good" : byCat.size >= 2 ? "limited" : "weak",
      detail: `${byCat.size} categories`,
    });

    const weak = checks.filter((c) => c.status === "weak").length;
    const limited = checks.filter((c) => c.status === "limited").length;
    const overall: Status = weak > 0 ? "weak" : limited > 2 ? "limited" : "good";

    const codedPct = (() => {
      const fullyCoded = cases.filter((c) => codedFields.every((f) => (f.get(c) ?? []).some((cd) => cd.code))).length;
      return total ? Math.round((fullyCoded / total) * 100) : 0;
    })();

    return {
      version: "v2 — coded / hierarchy matching",
      overall,
      summary: `${checks.length - weak - limited}/${checks.length} checks good; ${limited} limited; ${weak} weak. ${codedPct}% of cases carry codes across all six terminologies.`,
      rows: checks.map((c) => ({ check: c.check, status: c.status, detail: c.detail })),
    };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The whole cohort (v2 Case with coded arrays).",
    process: "For each of the six coded fields, computes the share of cases carrying at least one real code, plus code depth per coded case and medication-course dating; then re-checks the core text/outcome coverage. Grades good (≥50%), limited (20–50%), weak (<20%).",
    output: "version, overall grade, summary (incl. % fully-coded cases), and rows: one {check, status, detail} per coverage check — weak code rows mean hierarchy matching degrades to string fallbacks for that field.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Code presence ≠ semantic correctness — a wrong-but-present SNOMED code still grades 'good'.",
      "Subsumption matching only helps when the cohort's codes sit under the query's ancestor; depth here is a proxy.",
    ],
  },
};
Lab value distributions (box + violin)
