Backward lift (Coded)

The same lift scan as Backward lift, but over SNOMED-coded conditions rather than free text — and it returns the full 2×2 table for every candidate so each number can be checked by hand.

 {
  id: "backward-lift-coded",
  name: "Backward lift (Coded)",
  category: "Discovery & replication",
  description:
    "Per category, scans every comorbidity code (SNOMED CT) and reports the one whose presence lifts the escalation rate the most. The full 2×2 is returned so every lift is auditable. Cases with no comorbidityCodes fall back to the string list.",
  requiredFields: ["category", "comorbidities", "comorbidityCodes", "timeToEscalationDays"],
  run(cases) {
    const byCat = groupBy(cases, (c) => c.category);
    const rows: BackwardLiftCodedRow[] = [];
    byCat.forEach((group, cat) => {
      // Build the comorbidity-code universe from this category's cases.
      const universe = new Map<string, CodeKey>();
      for (const c of group) {
        for (const k of comKey(c)) {
          const key = `${k.system}:${k.code}`;
          if (!universe.has(key)) universe.set(key, k);
        }
      }
      let best: BackwardLiftCodedRow | null = null;
      for (const k of universe.values()) {
        const codeTag = `${k.system}:${k.code}`;
        const withCom = group.filter((c) => comKey(c).some((x) => `${x.system}:${x.code}` === codeTag));
        const withoutCom = group.filter((c) => !comKey(c).some((x) => `${x.system}:${x.code}` === codeTag));
        if (withCom.length < MIN_WITH || withoutCom.length < MIN_WITHOUT) continue;
        const a = withCom.filter(escalated).length;
        const b = withoutCom.filter(escalated).length;
        const rWith = a / withCom.length;
        const rWithout = b / withoutCom.length;
        const lift = rWithout > 0 ? rWith / rWithout : rWith > 0 ? LIFT_CAP : 0;
        if (!best || lift > best.lift) {
          best = {
            category: cat,
            code: k.code,
            system: k.system,
            display: k.display,
            a, b,
            c: withCom.length - a,
            d: withoutCom.length - b,
            n: withCom.length,
            rWith, rWithout, lift,
          };
        }
      }
      if (best) rows.push(best);
    });
    return { rows: rows.sort((x, y) => y.lift - x.lift) };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    const withEsc = cases.filter(escalated).length;
    const pct = cases.length ? withEsc / cases.length : 0;
    if (pct < 0.2) {
      return { level: "weak", gaps: [`escalation outcome present in only ${Math.round(pct * 100)}% of cohort`] };
    }
    const coded = cases.filter((c) => c.comorbidityCodes && c.comorbidityCodes.length > 0).length;
    if (coded / Math.max(1, cases.length) < 0.5) {
      return { level: "limited", gaps: [`comorbidityCodes populated for only ${Math.round((coded / cases.length) * 100)}% of cohort; falling back to strings`] };
    }
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Each case's category, comorbidityCodes (SNOMED CT), and escalation outcome. Falls back to comorbidities strings when codes are absent.",
    process: "Within each category, scans every comorbidity code with at least 4 patients having it and 2 without, computes escalation rate with vs without, and keeps the code with the highest lift (with ÷ without).",
    output: "rows: {category, code, system, display, a, b, c, d, n, rWith, rWithout, lift} — the full 2×2 is returned so every lift is auditable.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "comorbidities drive escalation in the generator (synthetic.ts plants 90% escalation for the INJECTED_LIFT condition) — the top lift is often literally planted.",
      "Scanning the comorbidity-code universe per category and keeping the max is a winner's curse: no multiplicity penalty, extreme lifts win.",
      "MIN_WITH=4 / MIN_WITHOUT=2 let four-patient cells post lift 99 via the LIFT_CAP sentinel.",
      "When comorbidityCodes is sparse, this analytic silently mixes coded and string-keyed rows in the same table.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Comorbidity is an input to the modeled escalation formula; the scan-max then overstates the planted effect.",
    ],
  },
};
Equity check (Coded)

For each common coded condition, reports outcomes and then looks for unfair gaps INSIDE that diagnosis — between sexes, insurance types and regions — rather than only across demographics.

 {
  id: "equity-coded",
  name: "Equity check (Coded)",
  category: "Public health / population",
  description:
    "For each common comorbidity code (SNOMED CT), the per-code outcomes (escalation, readmission, LOS) plus within-code disparity across sex, insurance, and region. Surfaces disparity *within* a diagnosis, not just across demographics.",
  requiredFields: ["comorbidities", "comorbidityCodes", "timeToEscalationDays", "readmitted90d", "losDays"],
  run(cases) {
    const minN = 10;
    const codeCounts = new Map<string, CodeKey & { n: number; cases: Case[] }>();
    for (const c of cases) {
      for (const k of comKey(c)) {
        const key = `${k.system}:${k.code}`;
        let rec = codeCounts.get(key);
        if (!rec) {
          rec = { system: k.system, code: k.code, display: k.display, n: 0, cases: [] };
          codeCounts.set(key, rec);
        }
        rec.n++;
        rec.cases.push(c);
      }
    }
    const rows: CodeStratRow[] = [];
    for (const rec of codeCounts.values()) {
      if (rec.n < minN) continue;
      rows.push({
        code: rec.code,
        system: rec.system,
        display: rec.display,
        n: rec.n,
        escRate: mean(rec.cases.map((c) => (escalated(c) ? 1 : 0))),
        readmitRate: mean(rec.cases.map((c) => (c.readmitted90d ? 1 : 0))),
        meanLos: mean(rec.cases.map((c) => c.losDays)),
        sexGap: gapAcross(rec.cases, (c) => c.sex),
        insuranceGap: gapAcross(rec.cases, (c) => c.insurance),
        regionGap: gapAcross(rec.cases, (c) => c.region),
      });
    }
    rows.sort((a, b) => b.sexGap + b.insuranceGap + b.regionGap - (a.sexGap + a.insuranceGap + a.regionGap));
    return { rows, minN };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    const coded = cases.filter((c) => c.comorbidityCodes && c.comorbidityCodes.length > 0).length;
    if (cases.length === 0) return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
    if (coded / cases.length < 0.3) {
      return { level: "weak", gaps: [`comorbidityCodes populated for only ${Math.round((coded / cases.length) * 100)}% of cohort; stratifier falls back to strings`] };
    }
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Each case's comorbidityCodes (SNOMED CT) plus outcome fields (escalation, readmitted90d, losDays) and demographic stratifiers (sex, insurance, region).",
    process: "For each comorbidity code carried by at least 10 patients, computes overall escRate/readmitRate/meanLos and the gap (max − min) in escRate across sex, insurance, and region strata within the code. Sorted by total gap descending.",
    output: "rows: {code, system, display, n, escRate, readmitRate, meanLos, sexGap, insuranceGap, regionGap} — the codes where the demographic disparity is widest.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Same modeled-outcome caveat as equity.ts: age, comorbidity burden, and polypharmacy feed escProb directly.",
      "Gap = max − min within a code; small cells (<4) are skipped, so the gap can be measured on a subset of the code's patients.",
      "Codes with sparse demographic coverage (e.g., one region) will report gap = 0 by construction — this hides, not signals.",
    ],
  },
};
Phenotype clusters (Coded)

The same condition-based clustering as Phenotype clusters, but built on standard codes so differently-spelled names can't split a group.

 {
  id: "phenotype-clusters-coded",
  name: "Phenotype clusters (Coded)",
  category: "Clinical / diagnostic",
  description:
    "Deterministic k-means over SNOMED-coded comorbidity profiles. Same algorithm as phenotype-clusters, but the multi-hot vector uses codes (no fuzzy-string artifacts) and the hallmark reports codes rather than text names. Falls back to strings when codes are absent.",
  requiredFields: ["comorbidities", "comorbidityCodes"],
  run(cases, params) {
    const k = Math.min(Math.max(Number(params?.k ?? 4), 2), 6);
    const pool = cases.filter((c) => comKey(c).length > 0);
    if (pool.length < k * 6) {
      return { k, n: pool.length, clusters: [], ready: false };
    }

    // Build the dim universe from the pool.
    const universe = new Map<string, Dim>();
    for (const c of pool) {
      for (const d of comKey(c)) {
        const key = `${d.system}:${d.code}`;
        if (!universe.has(key)) universe.set(key, d);
      }
    }
    const dims = [...universe.values()];

    const vecOf = (c: Case): number[] => {
      const set = new Set(comKey(c).map((d) => `${d.system}:${d.code}`));
      return dims.map((d) => (set.has(`${d.system}:${d.code}`) ? 1 : 0));
    };
    const vectors = pool.map(vecOf);
    const dist2 = (a: number[], b: number[]) => a.reduce((s, v, i) => s + (v - b[i]) * (v - b[i]), 0);

    const order = [...pool.keys()].sort(
      (a, b) => comKey(pool[b]).length - comKey(pool[a]).length || pool[a].id.localeCompare(pool[b].id)
    );
    let centroids = Array.from({ length: k }, (_, i) => vectors[order[Math.floor((i * order.length) / k)]].slice());
    let assign = new Array(vectors.length).fill(0);

    for (let iter = 0; iter < 30; iter++) {
      let changed = false;
      vectors.forEach((v, i) => {
        let best = 0;
        let bestD = Infinity;
        centroids.forEach((c, ci) => {
          const d = dist2(v, c);
          if (d < bestD) {
            bestD = d;
            best = ci;
          }
        });
        if (assign[i] !== best) {
          assign[i] = best;
          changed = true;
        }
      });
      centroids = centroids.map((c, ci) => {
        const members = vectors.filter((_, i) => assign[i] === ci);
        return members.length ? c.map((_, d) => mean(members.map((m) => m[d]))) : c;
      });
      if (!changed) break;
    }

    const clusters: ClusterCoded[] = centroids
      .map((_, ci) => {
        const members = pool.filter((_, i) => assign[i] === ci);
        if (!members.length) return null;
        const profile = dims.map((d) => ({
          system: d.system,
          code: d.code,
          display: d.display,
          rate: mean(members.map((m) => (comKey(m).some((x) => x.system === d.system && x.code === d.code) ? 1 : 0))),
        })).sort((a, b) => b.rate - a.rate);
        const hallmark = profile.filter((p) => p.rate >= 0.35).slice(0, 3);
        const byCat = groupBy(members, (m) => m.category);
        const [dominantCategory, dominantGroup] = [...byCat.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1].length - a[1].length)[0];
        const cluster: ClusterCoded = {
          id: ci,
          n: members.length,
          hallmark: hallmark.length ? hallmark : profile.slice(0, 1),
          avgAge: mean(members.map((m) => m.age)),
          avgComCount: mean(members.map((m) => comKey(m).length)),
          escRate: mean(members.map((m) => (escalated(m) ? 1 : 0))),
          readmitRate: mean(members.map((m) => (m.readmitted90d ? 1 : 0))),
          avgLos: mean(members.map((m) => m.losDays)),
          dominantCategory,
          dominantCategoryShare: dominantGroup.length / members.length,
        };
        return cluster;
      })
      .filter((c): c is ClusterCoded => c !== null)
      .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n);

    return { k, n: pool.length, clusters, ready: true };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    const eligible = cases.filter((c) => comKey(c).length > 0).length;
    if (eligible < 24) return { level: "weak", gaps: ["too few cases with comorbidities to cluster"] };
    const coded = cases.filter((c) => c.comorbidityCodes && c.comorbidityCodes.length > 0).length;
    if (coded / Math.max(1, cases.length) < 0.5) {
      return { level: "limited", gaps: [`comorbidityCodes populated for only ${Math.round((coded / cases.length) * 100)}% of cohort; falling back to strings`] };
    }
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Each case's comorbidityCodes (SNOMED CT) as a multi-hot vector; param k (default 4, clamped 2–6).",
    process: "Runs deterministic k-means (up to 30 iterations, seeded from evenly-spaced points along a comorbidity-count sort — never randomly) so the same cohort always yields the same clusters.",
    output: "k, n, ready, and clusters {id, n, hallmark:[{system, code, display, rate}], avgAge, avgComCount, escRate, readmitRate, avgLos, dominantCategory, dominantCategoryShare}.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "Cluster escRate/readmitRate are modeled from comorbidity burden — clusters re-display the injection, not discovered risk.",
      "Centroids seed along a comorbidity-count sort — cluster ordering is biased toward burden gradient, not neutral initialization.",
      "Multi-hot vectors treat each code equally; hallmark at rate ≥0.35 can rest on a handful of members.",
      "Mixed code/string inputs produce a hybrid vector — code-keyed dimensions may dominate, silently downweighting string-only patients.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Cluster outcomes are formulas of the clustering variable.",
    ],
  },
};
Pathway frequencies

Walks each patient's actual sequence of care states, counts every step from one state to the next, and flags steps that happen far more often than average.

 {
  id: "pathway-frequencies",
  name: "Pathway frequencies",
  category: "Longitudinal",
  description:
    "Real state-to-state transition matrix over each case's checkpoint sequence. Counts every consecutive from→to hop in the cohort, reports the most common paths, and flags hops whose count is more than 2σ above the matrix mean.",
  requiredFields: ["checkpoints"],
  run(cases) {
    const hopCounts = new Map<string, { from: string; to: string; n: number }>();
    let totalHops = 0;
    let patientsWithPathway = 0;
    for (const c of cases) {
      const path = checkpointList(c);
      if (path.length < 2) continue;
      patientsWithPathway++;
      for (let i = 0; i < path.length - 1; i++) {
        const from = path[i].name;
        const to = path[i + 1].name;
        if (!from || !to) continue;
        const key = `${from} → ${to}`;
        let rec = hopCounts.get(key);
        if (!rec) {
          rec = { from, to, n: 0 };
          hopCounts.set(key, rec);
        }
        rec.n++;
        totalHops++;
      }
    }

    if (totalHops === 0) {
      return {
        n: cases.length,
        pathways: 0,
        totalHops: 0,
        rows: [],
        ready: false,
      };
    }

    // Compute mean and SD of hop counts to flag concentrated hops.
    const counts = [...hopCounts.values()].map((r) => r.n);
    const mean = counts.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / counts.length;
    const variance = counts.reduce((s, v) => s + (v - mean) ** 2, 0) / counts.length;
    const sd = Math.sqrt(variance);
    const flagThreshold = mean + 2 * sd;

    const rows: PathwayRow[] = [...hopCounts.values()]
      .map((r) => ({
        from: r.from,
        to: r.to,
        n: r.n,
        share: r.n / totalHops,
        top: false,
        flagged: r.n > flagThreshold,
      }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n);

    // Mark the top-3 by count as "top" as well as flagged if they pass threshold.
    rows.slice(0, 3).forEach((r) => { r.top = true; });

    // Build a from-summary (how many distinct outgoing hops each from-state has)
    // to help the renderer describe pathway diversity.
    const outgoing = groupBy(rows, (r) => r.from);
    const fromSummary = [...outgoing.entries()].map(([from, hops]) => ({
      from,
      n: hops.reduce((s, h) => s + h.n, 0),
      distinctTo: hops.length,
    }));

    return {
      n: cases.length,
      pathways: patientsWithPathway,
      totalHops,
      mean: Number(mean.toFixed(2)),
      sd: Number(sd.toFixed(2)),
      rows,
      fromSummary,
      ready: true,
    };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    const eligible = cases.filter((c) => checkpointList(c).length >= 2).length;
    const pct = cases.length ? eligible / cases.length : 0;
    if (pct < 0.2) return { level: "weak", gaps: [`cases with a multi-checkpoint pathway: ${Math.round(pct * 100)}%`] };
    if (pct < 0.5) return { level: "limited", gaps: [`cases with a multi-checkpoint pathway: ${Math.round(pct * 100)}%`] };
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Each case's checkpoints (per-case name-keyed record).",
    process: "Walks each case's checkpoint list and counts every consecutive from→to hop. Builds a from→to matrix; flags hops whose count exceeds mean + 2 SD.",
    output: "rows: {from, to, n, share, top, flagged}, plus n, pathways (cases with ≥2 checkpoints), totalHops, mean, sd, and fromSummary [{from, n, distinctTo}].",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "checkpoints is a single-snapshot model in v1/v2 — the 'from→to' sequence is whatever the cohort was generated with, not an observed clinical journey.",
      "Flagging at 2σ of the matrix mean is a coarse rarity filter, not a clinical significance test.",
      "A pathway of length 1 (single checkpoint) is excluded — cases with sparse checkpoint coverage vanish from the matrix.",
    ],
  },
};
Signal detection (coded)

An automated scan for seven kinds of unusual patterns — one diagnosis dominating, drugs over-used, odd stay lengths, readmission clusters, skewed subgroups and more — each with a numeric threshold.

 {
  id: "signal-detection-coded",
  name: "Signal detection (coded)",
  category: "Public health / surveillance",
  description:
    "Scans the cohort for seven classes of unusual patterns — diagnosis concentration, medication overuse, investigation concentration, LOS outliers, readmission clustering, demographic subgroup skew, and temporal admit clustering — preferring SNOMED/RxNorm coded fields over strings when available.",
  run(cases, params) {
    const signals: Signal[] = [];
    const total = cases.length;
    if (total === 0) return { signals: [], nSignals: 0 };

    // 1. Unusual diagnosis concentration (string only — no diagnosisCodes field)
    const diagCounts = new Map<string, string[]>();
    for (const c of cases) {
      if (c.diagnosis) {
        const arr = diagCounts.get(c.diagnosis) ?? [];
        arr.push(c.id);
        diagCounts.set(c.diagnosis, arr);
      }
    }
    const expectedDiag = total * 0.05;
    for (const [diag, ids] of diagCounts.entries()) {
      if (ids.length > expectedDiag * 3) {
        signals.push({
          type: "diagnosis-concentration",
          severity: ids.length > expectedDiag * 5 ? "high" : "medium",
          description: `Diagnosis "${diag}" in ${ids.length}/${total} cases (expected ~${Math.round(expectedDiag)})`,
          n: ids.length,
          pct: Math.round((ids.length / total) * 1000) / 10,
          expected: Math.round(expectedDiag),
        });
      }
    }

    // 2. Unusual medication use (code-preferring)
    const medCounts = new Map<string, { ids: string[]; code?: string; display: string }>();
    for (const c of cases) {
      const meds = medKey(c);
      for (const m of meds) {
        const rec = medCounts.get(m) ?? { ids: [], display: m };
        rec.ids.push(c.id);
        medCounts.set(m, rec);
      }
    }
    const expectedMed = total * 0.10;
    for (const [med, rec] of medCounts.entries()) {
      if (rec.ids.length > expectedMed * 2) {
        signals.push({
          type: "medication-overuse",
          severity: rec.ids.length > expectedMed * 3 ? "high" : "medium",
          description: `Medication "${rec.display}" in ${rec.ids.length}/${total} cases (expected ~${Math.round(expectedMed)})`,
          code: med,
          display: rec.display,
          n: rec.ids.length,
          pct: Math.round((rec.ids.length / total) * 1000) / 10,
          expected: Math.round(expectedMed),
        });
      }
    }

    // 3. Unusual comorbidity concentration (coded)
    const comCounts = new Map<string, { ids: string[]; display: string }>();
    for (const c of cases) {
      for (const com of comKey(c)) {
        const rec = comCounts.get(com) ?? { ids: [], display: com };
        rec.ids.push(c.id);
        comCounts.set(com, rec);
      }
    }
    const expectedCom = total * 0.08;
    for (const [com, rec] of comCounts.entries()) {
      if (rec.ids.length > expectedCom * 2.5) {
        signals.push({
          type: "comorbidity-concentration",
          severity: rec.ids.length > expectedCom * 4 ? "high" : "medium",
          description: `Comorbidity "${rec.display}" in ${rec.ids.length}/${total} cases (expected ~${Math.round(expectedCom)})`,
          code: com,
          display: rec.display,
          n: rec.ids.length,
          pct: Math.round((rec.ids.length / total) * 1000) / 10,
          expected: Math.round(expectedCom),
        });
      }
    }

    // 4. Unusual LOS (right-skew)
    const losValues = cases.map((c) => c.losDays ?? 0).filter((v) => v > 0);
    if (losValues.length > 10) {
      const mean = losValues.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / losValues.length;
      const variance = losValues.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - mean) ** 2, 0) / losValues.length;
      const sd = Math.sqrt(variance);
      const threshold = mean + 2 * sd;
      const outliers = cases.filter((c) => (c.losDays ?? 0) > threshold);
      if (outliers.length > 3) {
        signals.push({
          type: "los-outliers",
          severity: outliers.length > cases.length * 0.15 ? "high" : "medium",
          description: `${outliers.length} cases with LOS > mean+2σ (${Math.round(threshold)} days); mean=${Math.round(mean)}d, σ=${Math.round(sd)}d`,
          n: outliers.length,
          pct: Math.round((outliers.length / total) * 1000) / 10,
          expected: Math.round(total * 0.0228), // ~2.28% beyond μ+2σ
        });
      }
    }

    // 5. Unusual readmission clustering by demographic
    const byInsurance = groupBy(cases.filter((c) => c.insurance), (c) => c.insurance!);
    for (const [ins, g] of byInsurance.entries()) {
      const escRate = g.filter(escalated).length / g.length;
      const overallEsc = cases.filter(escalated).length / total;
      if (g.length >= 10 && escRate > overallEsc * 1.8) {
        signals.push({
          type: "readmission-demographic-skew",
          severity: escRate > overallEsc * 2.5 ? "high" : "medium",
          description: `Insurance "${ins}" has ${Math.round(escRate * 100)}% escalation vs ${Math.round(overallEsc * 100)}% overall`,
          display: ins,
          n: g.length,
          pct: Math.round(escRate * 1000) / 10,
          expected: Math.round(overallEsc * 1000) / 10,
        });
      }
    }

    // 6. Medication + comorbidity co-occurrence anomaly
    // Flag if a med+combo pair appears in >30% of cases (unusually coupled)
    const medComPairs = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const c of cases) {
      const meds = medKey(c);
      const coms = comKey(c);
      for (const med of meds) {
        for (const com of coms) {
          medComPairs.set(`${med}|${com}`, (medComPairs.get(`${med}|${com}`) ?? 0) + 1);
        }
      }
    }
    for (const [pair, count] of medComPairs.entries()) {
      if (count > total * 0.30) {
        const [med, com] = pair.split("|");
        signals.push({
          type: "med-comorbidity-coupling",
          severity: count > total * 0.5 ? "high" : "medium",
          description: `Medication "${med}" co-occurs with comorbidity "${com}" in ${count}/${total} cases (${Math.round((count / total) * 100)}%)`,
          code: pair,
          n: count,
          pct: Math.round((count / total) * 1000) / 10,
          expected: Math.round(total * 0.08),
        });
      }
    }

    // 7. Procedure concentration (coded)
    const procCounts = new Map<string, { ids: string[]; display: string }>();
    for (const c of cases) {
      for (const p of procKey(c)) {
        const rec = procCounts.get(p) ?? { ids: [], display: p };
        rec.ids.push(c.id);
        procCounts.set(p, rec);
      }
    }
    const expectedProc = total * 0.05;
    for (const [proc, rec] of procCounts.entries()) {
      if (rec.ids.length > expectedProc * 3) {
        signals.push({
          type: "procedure-concentration",
          severity: rec.ids.length > expectedProc * 5 ? "high" : "medium",
          description: `Procedure "${rec.display}" in ${rec.ids.length}/${total} cases (expected ~${Math.round(expectedProc)})`,
          code: proc,
          display: rec.display,
          n: rec.ids.length,
          pct: Math.round((rec.ids.length / total) * 1000) / 10,
          expected: Math.round(expectedProc),
        });
      }
    }

    // Sort: high first, then medium, then low; within severity by n desc.
    const severityOrder: Record<string, number> = { high: 0, medium: 1, low: 2 };
    signals.sort((a, b) => severityOrder[a.severity] - severityOrder[b.severity] || b.n - a.n);

    return {
      signals,
      nSignals: signals.length,
      bySeverity: {
        high: signals.filter((s) => s.severity === "high").length,
        medium: signals.filter((s) => s.severity === "medium").length,
        low: signals.filter((s) => s.severity === "low").length,
      },
    };
  },
  quality(cases) {
    if (cases.length < 30)
      return { level: "weak", gaps: ["too few cases for reliable signal detection"] };
    return { level: "good", gaps: [] };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "All case fields including medicationCodes, comorbidityCodes, procedureCodes, losDays, insurance.",
    process: "Seven checks: (1) diagnosis concentration >5× expected, (2) medication overuse >2× expected, (3) comorbidity concentration >2.5× expected, (4) LOS outliers beyond mean+2σ, (5) insurance-group escalation skew >1.8× overall, (6) med+comorbidity coupling >30% of cases, (7) procedure concentration >3× expected.",
    output: "signals: array of {type, severity, description, code, display, n, pct, expected} — sorted by severity desc then n desc.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Thresholds (2×, 3×, 5×) are fixed heuristics; a busy cohort may surface many low-severity signals that are clinically expected.",
      "No multiple-testing correction is applied across the seven signal classes.",
      "Medication overuse signals are raw counts — a commonly prescribed drug will always trigger a signal regardless of clinical appropriateness.",
    ],
  },
};
V3 — 52 Suite buttons
Cohort overview

The same master summary, but rebuilt from each patient's dated event stream: counts come from events, top lists are distinct coded concepts, and everything is measured over each patient's observation window.

 {
  id: "cohort-overview",
  name: "Cohort overview",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "The full picture of the cohort, rebuilt from the event stream: size, demographics, clinical makeup (top comorbidities, symptoms, medications, labs, procedures) and outcome baselines — the reference frame every other analytic is read against.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const stays = episodeStays(views);
    const byCat = groupBy(views, (v) => v.category ?? "(unknown)");
    const total = Math.max(1, views.length);

    // ── per-concept distributions (top-N with patient ids) ──
    type Dist = Array<{ label: string; value: number; caseIds: string[] }>;
    const dist = (category: string, n: number): Dist => {
      const counts = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
      for (const e of events) {
        if (e.category !== category) continue;
        const label = e.display || e.code || "(unknown)";
        if (!counts.has(label)) counts.set(label, new Set());
        counts.get(label)!.add(e.patientId);
      }
      return [...counts.entries()]
        .sort((a, b) => b[1].size - a[1].size)
        .slice(0, n)
        .map(([label, ids]) => ({ label, value: ids.size, caseIds: [...ids] }));
    };
    // ── co-occurrence pairs within a patient's stream ──
    const coDist = (category: string, n: number): Dist => {
      const pairPatients = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
      for (const v of views) {
        const labels = [...new Set(v.events
          .filter((e) => e.category === category)
          .map((e) => e.display || e.code || "(unknown)"))];
        for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) {
          for (let j = i + 1; j < labels.length; j++) {
            const a = labels[i], b = labels[j];
            const key = a < b ? `${a}~~${b}` : `${b}~~${a}`;
            if (!pairPatients.has(key)) pairPatients.set(key, new Set());
            pairPatients.get(key)!.add(v.patientId);
          }
        }
      }
      return [...pairPatients.entries()]
        .sort((a, b) => b[1].size - a[1].size)
        .slice(0, n)
        .map(([label, ids]) => ({ label: label.replace("~~", " + "), value: ids.size, caseIds: [...ids] }));
    };

    // ── buckets ──
    const polyph = [0, 0, 0, 0]; // 0, 1-2, 3-4, 5+
    for (const v of views) {
      const k = v.polypharmacyCount ?? 0;
      if (k === 0) polyph[0]++;
      else if (k <= 2) polyph[1]++;
      else if (k <= 4) polyph[2]++;
      else polyph[3]++;
    }
    const ageBuckets = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0];
    for (const v of views) {
      const a = v.age ?? -1;
      if (a < 18) ageBuckets[0]++;
      else if (a < 45) ageBuckets[1]++;
      else if (a < 65) ageBuckets[2]++;
      else if (a < 80) ageBuckets[3]++;
      else ageBuckets[4]++;
    }
    const escByCat = [...byCat.entries()]
      .map(([cat, g]) => ({
        label: cat,
        value: rate(g, (v) => v.escalated === true),
        n: g.length,
      }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value);

    const countOf = (category: string) =>
      mean(views, (v) => v.events.filter((e) => e.category === category).length);

    return {
      rows: [
        { label: "Patients", value: views.length },
        { label: "Events", value: events.length },
        { label: "Mean age (years)", value: mean(views, (v) => v.age) },
        { label: "Female", value: views.filter((v) => v.sex === "F").length },
        { label: "Male", value: views.filter((v) => v.sex === "M").length },
        ...[...byCat.entries()]
          .sort((a, b) => b[1].length - a[1].length)
          .map(([cat, g]) => ({ label: `Category: ${cat}`, value: g.length })),
        { label: "Escalation rate", value: rate(views, (v) => v.escalated === true) },
        { label: "90-day readmission rate", value: rate(views, (v) => v.readmitted90d === true) },
        { label: "Median LOS (days)", value: median(views.map((v) => v.losDays ?? NaN)) },
        { label: "Episodes (30-day gaps)", value: stays.length },
        { label: "Mean comorbidities per patient", value: countOf("comorbidity") },
        { label: "Mean medications per patient", value: countOf("medication") },
        { label: "Mean symptoms per patient", value: countOf("symptom") },
        { label: "Mean investigations per patient", value: countOf("lab") },
        { label: "Mean procedures per patient", value: countOf("procedure") },
      ],
      byComorbidity: dist("comorbidity", 8),
      bySymptom: dist("symptom", 8),
      byMedication: dist("medication", 8),
      byInvestigation: dist("lab", 5),
      byProcedure: dist("procedure", 8),
      coOccurrenceComorbidity: coDist("comorbidity", 10),
      coOccurrenceInvestigation: coDist("lab", 10),
      coOccurrenceProcedure: coDist("procedure", 10),
      polypharmacy: [
        { label: "0 meds", value: polyph[0] },
        { label: "1–2 meds", value: polyph[1] },
        { label: "3–4 meds", value: polyph[2] },
        { label: "5+ meds", value: polyph[3] },
      ],
      disposition: [],
      ageBands: [
        { label: "0–17", value: ageBuckets[0] },
        { label: "18–44", value: ageBuckets[1] },
        { label: "45–64", value: ageBuckets[2] },
        { label: "65–79", value: ageBuckets[3] },
        { label: "80+", value: ageBuckets[4] },
      ],
      encounterBands: [],
      escByCat,
    };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The whole cohort's flat event stream; per-patient age/sex/category/losDays/polypharmacyCount from the encounter event's attrs, outcome flags from outcome events, stays from 30-day-gap episodes.",
    process: "Indexes one view per patient, counts sex and category splits, computes escalation/readmission rates from outcome events, mean per-category event counts per patient, top-N concept distributions and within-patient co-occurrence pairs, buckets polypharmacy and age, and reports escalation rate per category.",
    output: "rows: one label/value per headline metric; byComorbidity/bySymptom/byMedication/byInvestigation/byProcedure: top-N lists with patient ids; coOccurrenceComorbidity/Investigation/Procedure: top pairs; polypharmacy, ageBands: bucketed counts; escByCat: per-category escalation rate — the reference frame every other analytic is read against.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Escalation/readmission baselines read modeled outcome events — they restate generator formulas, not observed outcomes.",
      "Top-N and co-occurrence distributions enumerate the generator's concept pools — distributions are simulation parameters.",
      "Disposition and 90-day-encounter panels are absent: the event model has no disposition field and no pre-admission encounter history.",
      "Median LOS comes from 30-day-gap episodes; the gap rule, not bed days, defines a stay.",
    ],
  },
};

// ── age-bands ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const ageBands: Analytic = {
  id: "age-bands",
  name: "Cohort overview by age band",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "Headcount, share of cohort, median episode length of stay, and 90-day readmission rate per age band, from each patient's event stream.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter", "outcome"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const stays = episodeStays(views);
    const bands = ["45–64", "65–74", "75+"];
    const rows = bands.map((band) => {
      const members = views.filter((v) => ageBand(v.age) === band);
      const los = stays.filter((s) => members.some((m) => m.patientId === s.patientId)).map((s) => s.los);
      return {
        band,
        count: members.length,
        pct: views.length ? members.length / views.length : 0,
        medianLos: median(los),
        readmitPct: rate(members, (v) => v.readmitted90d === true),
      };
    });
    return { rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; age from the encounter event's attrs, readmission flag from outcome events, stays from 30-day-gap episodes.",
    process: "Bands patients by age (45–64, 65–74, 75+), then counts each band, its share of the cohort, the median episode LOS, and the readmission rate within it.",
    output: "rows: one per age band with {band, count, pct, medianLos, readmitPct} — a demographic baseline, not a claim of causation.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "readmitPct per band is circular: readmission outcome events are modeled with age among the formula inputs.",
      "Age gradient is injected by the generator, not discovered from care.",
      "medianLos describes the modeled event dates and 30-day gap rule, not throughput.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Readmission by age band restates the generator's age-dependent outcome formula.",
    ],
  },
};

const payerMix: Analytic = {

  requiredCategories: ["encounter", "outcome"],

      escRate: rate(g, (v) => v.escalated === true),

// ── los-distribution ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const losDistribution: Analytic = {
  id: "los-distribution",
  name: "LOS distribution",
  category: "Clinical / diagnostic",
  description:
    "The full per-episode length-of-stay histogram (not just the median): how episodes spread across short, typical, and extended stays.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const stays = episodeStays(views);
    const bands: Array<[number, number, string]> = [
      [0, 3, "0–3d"], [4, 7, "4–7d"], [8, 14, "8–14d"], [15, 21, "15–21d"], [22, 999, "22d+"],
    ];
    const rows = bands.map(([lo, hi, label]) => {
      const g = stays.filter((s) => s.los >= lo && s.los <= hi);
      return { label, n: g.length, share: stays.length ? g.length / stays.length : 0 };
    });
    return { rows, median: median(stays.map((s) => s.los)), episodes: stays.length, patients: views.length };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; stays built per patient as 30-day-gap episodes, LOS from each episode's first-to-last event dates.",
    process: "Computes per-episode LOS, bins episodes into five bands (0–3, 4–7, 8–14, 15–21, 22+ days), and counts episodes and share per band, plus the overall median.",
    output: "rows: one per band with {label, n, share}, plus median, episodes, patients — the full episode-level LOS histogram.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Episode LOS is date arithmetic over modeled event dates; the 30-day gap rule defines a stay, not bed days.",
      "Single-day episodes count as 0 days — the 0–3d band absorbs patients whose dated events cluster on one day.",
      "Descriptive-only; safe as a plumbing check, misleading as an operations baseline.",
    ],
  },
};

// ── los-disposition ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const losDisposition: Analytic = {
  id: "los-disposition",
  name: "LOS / outcomes by category",
  category: "Clinical / diagnostic",
  description:
    "Per-episode length-of-stay percentiles for the whole cohort, and the modeled outcome mix (escalation, readmission) per encounter category.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter", "outcome"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const stays = episodeStays(views);
    const los = stays.map((s) => s.los);
    const byCat = [...groupBy(views, (v) => v.category ?? "(unknown)").entries()].map(([cat, g]) => ({
      cat,
      n: g.length,
      medianLos: median(stays.filter((s) => g.some((v) => v.patientId === s.patientId)).map((s) => s.los)),
      escPct: rate(g, (v) => v.escalated === true),
      readmitPct: rate(g, (v) => v.readmitted90d === true),
    }));
    return {
      medianLos: median(los),
      p25: quantile(los, 0.25),
      p75: quantile(los, 0.75),
      byCat: byCat.sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n),
    };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; per-episode LOS from 30-day-gap episodes, encounter category from attrs, outcome flags from outcome events. (No disposition exists in the event model.)",
    process: "Computes cohort-wide median, 25th, and 75th percentile episode LOS, then per category the median episode LOS and escalation/readmission rates.",
    output: "medianLos/p25/p75 over episodes, plus byCat {cat, n, medianLos, escPct, readmitPct} — where stays run long and how outcomes distribute.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Episode percentiles restate modeled event dates under a 30-day gap rule — not operational throughput.",
      "escPct/readmitPct are modeled outcome events — read them as simulation parameters, not performance.",
      "Disposition is not representable in the event model; outcome mix replaces the v1 disposition mix.",
    ],
  },
};

// ── high-utilizers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface UtilSummary {
  n: number;
  eventsPerPatient: number;
  escRate: number;
  readmit: number;
  meds: number;
  labs: number;
}

function summarizeUtil(g: CaseView[]): UtilSummary {
  return {
    n: g.length,
    eventsPerPatient: mean(g, (v) => v.events.length),
    escRate: rate(g, (v) => v.escalated === true),
    readmit: rate(g, (v) => v.readmitted90d === true),
    meds: mean(g, (v) => v.events.filter((e) => e.category === "medication").length),
    labs: mean(g, (v) => v.events.filter((e) => e.category === "lab").length),
  };
}

const highUtilizers: Analytic = {
  id: "high-utilizers",
  name: "High-utilizers",
  category: "Public health / population",
  description:
    "The top decile of patients by event volume within their observation period, profiled against everyone else on outcomes, medications, and labs.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    // utilization = the patient's own event count within their observation
    // period (first to last event), not a fixed 90-day window
    const sorted = [...views].sort((a, b) => b.events.length - a.events.length);
    const cut = Math.max(1, Math.round(views.length * 0.1));
    const hi = sorted.slice(0, cut);
    const rest = sorted.slice(cut);
    const byCatHi = groupBy(hi, (v) => v.category ?? "(unknown)");
    const catRows = [...byCatHi.entries()]
      .map(([cat, g]) => ({ cat, n: g.length }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n);
    return { hi: summarizeUtil(hi), rest: summarizeUtil(rest), catRows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; utilization is each patient's event count within their own observation period, plus medication/lab counts and outcome flags.",
    process: "Sorts patients by event count, takes the top decile (minimum 1), and profiles that group against the rest on events, escalation, readmission, medications, and labs; counts the top decile per category.",
    output: "hi and rest summaries {n, eventsPerPatient, escRate, readmit, meds, labs}, plus catRows {cat, n} — the utilization-outcome contrast at the top decile.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "Event volume is generated data with intensity tied to polypharmacy and acuity draws — the top decile is a generator artifact.",
      "escRate/readmit contrast is circular: polypharmacyCount feeds both utilization intensity and the modeled outcome formulas.",
      "Fixed top-decile cut has no clinical threshold; membership flips with cohort size.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Utilization is modeled, not observed — do not use the hi/rest contrast for targeting.",
    ],
  },
};

// ── utilization-readmit ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const utilizationReadmit: Analytic = {
  id: "utilization-readmit",
  name: "Event volume vs outcomes",
  category: "Public health / population",
  description:
    "Readmission and escalation rates by banded event volume within each patient's observation period — does prior utilization track what happens next?",
  requiredCategories: ["outcome"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const bands: Array<[number, number, string]> = [
      [0, 3, "0–3"], [4, 6, "4–6"], [7, 9, "7–9"], [10, 99, "10+"],
    ];
    const rows = bands.map(([lo, hi, label]) => {
      const g = views.filter((v) => v.events.length >= lo && v.events.length <= hi);
      return {
        label,
        n: g.length,
        readmitRate: rate(g, (v) => v.readmitted90d === true),
        escRate: rate(g, (v) => v.escalated === true),
      };
    }).filter((r) => r.n > 0);
    return { rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; each patient's total event count within their observation period, plus escalation and 90-day readmission outcome events.",
    process: "Bands patients by event count (0–3, 4–6, 7–9, 10+), then computes the readmission rate and escalation rate within each band; empty bands are dropped.",
    output: "rows: one per non-empty band with {label, n, readmitRate, escRate} — the utilization-outcome gradient.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "Event volume is a modeled draw — the bands partition generator output, not observed utilization.",
      "readmitRate and escRate are themselves formula outputs — the gradient compares generator draws.",
      "Volume and outcome formulas share inputs (polypharmacy, comorbidity) — the gradient is injected, never observed.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Both axes are modeled — the utilization-outcome gradient is not evidence.",
    ],
  },
};

// ── test-intensity ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const testIntensity: Analytic = {
  id: "test-intensity",
  name: "Test-ordering intensity",
  category: "Clinical / diagnostic",
  description:
    "Lab events per episode, banded: does test volume track comorbidity burden or pad the stay?",
  requiredCategories: ["lab", "comorbidity", "encounter", "outcome"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const comCount = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const v of views) {
      comCount.set(v.patientId, v.events.filter((e) => e.category === "comorbidity").length);
    }
    // per-episode lab counts: labs whose date falls inside each episode span
    const rows: Array<{ label: string; n: number; avgComorbidities: number; avgLos: number; escRate: number }> = [];
    const bands: Array<[number, number, string]> = [
      [0, 2, "0–2 tests"], [3, 4, "3–4 tests"], [5, 99, "5+ tests"],
    ];
    const perEpisode: Array<{ patientId: string; los: number; labs: number }> = [];
    for (const v of views) {
      const eps = buildEpisodes(v.events, { gapDays: 30 });
      for (const ep of eps) {
        const labs = v.events.filter(
          (e) => e.category === "lab" && e.t >= ep.start && e.t <= ep.end,
        ).length;
        perEpisode.push({
          patientId: v.patientId,
          los: Math.max(0, Math.round((Date.parse(ep.end) - Date.parse(ep.start)) / DAY)),
          labs,
        });
      }
    }
    for (const [lo, hi, label] of bands) {
      const g = perEpisode.filter((s) => s.labs >= lo && s.labs <= hi);
      if (g.length === 0) continue;
      rows.push({
        label,
        n: g.length,
        avgComorbidities: mean([...new Set(g.map((s) => s.patientId))], (pid) => comCount.get(pid)),
        avgLos: mean(g, (s) => s.los),
        escRate: rate(views.filter((v) => g.some((s) => s.patientId === v.patientId)), (v) => v.escalated === true),
      });
    }
    return { rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; lab events per 30-day-gap episode, comorbidity-event count per patient, episode LOS, and escalation outcome events.",
    process: "Counts lab events inside each episode, bands episodes by lab count (0–2, 3–4, 5+), then averages comorbidity count and episode LOS and computes the patient escalation rate per band.",
    output: "rows: one per band with {label, n, avgComorbidities, avgLos, escRate} — read as whether test volume tracks acuity or pads the stay.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "escRate by test band is circular: comorbidity burden drives both modeled test volume and the modeled escalation formula.",
      "avgLos comparison invites a 'tests pad the stay' reading, but episode LOS is date arithmetic over generated events.",
      "Labs dated by the generator, not order entry — band counts measure the generator's intensity parameter.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Test-volume bands and outcomes come from the same generator formulas — the contrast is injected.",
    ],
  },
};

// ── cohort-details ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const cohortDetails: Analytic = {
  id: "cohort-details",
  name: "Cohort details",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "One row per patient in the active cohort: demographics, category, episode count, event volume, and modeled outcome flags — the drill-down table behind the overview.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter"],
  run(events, params) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const limit = Math.max(1, Number(params?.limit ?? 200));
    const staysByPatient = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const s of episodeStays(views)) {
      staysByPatient.set(s.patientId, (staysByPatient.get(s.patientId) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
    const rows = views
      .map((v) => {
        const enc = v.events.find((e) => e.category === "encounter");
        return {
          patientId: v.patientId,
          age: v.age ?? null,
          sex: v.sex || null,
          insurance: v.insurance || null,
          region: v.region || null,
          category: v.category || null,
          losDays: v.losDays ?? null,
          episodes: staysByPatient.get(v.patientId) ?? 0,
          events: v.events.length,
          meds: v.events.filter((e) => e.category === "medication").length,
          labs: v.events.filter((e) => e.category === "lab").length,
          polypharmacyCount: v.polypharmacyCount ?? null,
          escalated: v.escalated === true,
          readmitted90d: v.readmitted90d === true,
          system: String(enc?.system ?? ""),
        };
      })
      .sort((a, b) => (a.patientId < b.patientId ? -1 : 1))
      .slice(0, limit);
    return { rows, total: views.length, truncated: views.length > rows.length };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; demographics and losDays from the encounter event's attrs, outcome flags from outcome events, episode count from 30-day-gap episodes.",
    process: "Indexes one view per patient, counts episodes, events, medication and lab events, copies demographics and modeled outcome flags, and returns up to params.limit rows (default 200), patientId order.",
    output: "rows: one per patient {patientId, demographics, category, losDays, episodes, events, meds, labs, escalated, readmitted90d}, plus total and truncated — the drill-down table.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "escalated/readmitted90d columns restate the modeled outcome events — flags, not observed outcomes.",
      "Episode count depends on the 30-day gap rule; patients with sparse dating collapse into fewer episodes.",
      "Default 200-row cap truncates large cohorts; total/truncated tell you when the table is partial.",
    ],
  },
};
