Data quality assessment (v3 temporal)

Trustworthiness for the event stream: checks what share of events carry real dates, how many events each patient has, observation span, coverage of all eight event categories, the 30-day-gap episode structure, and whether medication courses have usable start/end dates.

 {
  id: "data-quality",
  name: "Data quality assessment (v3 temporal)",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "Grades the cohort's EVENT STREAM fitness for longitudinal analytics: datedness of events, per-category coverage (encounter, diagnosis, comorbidity, medication, lab, procedure, symptom, outcome), episode structure, and observation span. Weak rows mark the temporal analytics that can't be trusted on this stream.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const total = events.length;
    const patients = views.length;
    const checks: Check[] = [];

    // ── stream-level ──
    const dated = events.filter((e) => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(e.t)).length;
    checks.push({
      check: "Dated events",
      status: grade(total ? Math.round((dated / total) * 100) : 0),
      detail: `${dated}/${total} events (${total ? Math.round((dated / total) * 100) : 0}%)`,
    });
    checks.push({
      check: "Events per patient",
      status: patients && mean(views, (v) => v.events.length) >= 5 ? "good" : patients && mean(views, (v) => v.events.length) >= 2 ? "limited" : "weak",
      detail: `mean ${mean(views, (v) => v.events.length).toFixed(1)} across ${patients} patients`,
    });

    // Observation span: months covered.
    const months = new Set(events.filter((e) => e.t).map((e) => e.t.substring(0, 7)));
    checks.push({
      check: "Observation span",
      status: months.size >= 6 ? "good" : months.size >= 3 ? "limited" : "weak",
      detail: `${months.size} distinct months`,
    });

    // ── per-category coverage (share of patients with ≥1 event) ──
    const categories = ["encounter", "diagnosis", "comorbidity", "medication", "lab", "procedure", "symptom", "outcome"];
    for (const category of categories) {
      const withCat = new Set(events.filter((e) => e.category === category).map((e) => e.patientId));
      const pct = patients ? Math.round((withCat.size / patients) * 100) : 0;
      checks.push({
        check: `"${category}" events`,
        status: withCat.size === 0 ? "weak" : grade(pct),
        detail: withCat.size === 0 ? "none in stream" : `${withCat.size}/${patients} patients (${pct}%)`,
      });
    }

    // Outcome events specifically (modeled escalation/readmission).
    const escCount = events.filter((e) => e.category === "outcome" && e.code === "escalation").length;
    const readmitCount = events.filter((e) => e.category === "outcome" && e.code === "readmission-90d").length;
    checks.push({
      check: "Escalation outcome events",
      status: escCount >= 20 ? "good" : escCount >= 5 ? "limited" : "weak",
      detail: `${escCount} events`,
    });
    checks.push({
      check: "Readmission outcome events",
      status: readmitCount >= 20 ? "good" : readmitCount >= 5 ? "limited" : "weak",
      detail: `${readmitCount} events`,
    });

    // ── episode structure ──
    let episodes = 0;
    for (const v of views) episodes += buildEpisodes(v.events, { gapDays: 30 }).length;
    checks.push({
      check: "Episodes (30-day gap rule)",
      status: episodes >= patients ? "good" : "limited",
      detail: `${episodes} episodes across ${patients} patients`,
    });

    // Medication course dating (temporal analytics depend on it).
    const medEvents = events.filter((e) => e.category === "medication");
    const medWithStart = medEvents.filter((e) => e.start).length;
    checks.push({
      check: "Medication courses with start date",
      status: medEvents.length ? grade(Math.round((medWithStart / medEvents.length) * 100)) : "weak",
      detail: medEvents.length ? `${medWithStart}/${medEvents.length} courses` : "no medication events",
    });

    // Numeric lab values — powers the box/violin distributions analytic.
    const labEvents = events.filter((e) => e.category === "lab");
    const labsWithValues = labEvents.filter((e) => e.value !== undefined && Number.isFinite(Number(e.value))).length;
    checks.push({
      check: "Lab events with numeric value (box/violin)",
      status: labEvents.length ? grade(Math.round((labsWithValues / labEvents.length) * 100)) : "weak",
      detail: labEvents.length ? `${labsWithValues}/${labEvents.length} lab events` : "no lab events",
    });
    const labsWithUnits = labEvents.filter((e) => e.unit).length;
    checks.push({
      check: "Lab events with unit",
      status: labEvents.length ? grade(Math.round((labsWithUnits / labEvents.length) * 100)) : "weak",
      detail: labEvents.length ? `${labsWithUnits}/${labEvents.length} lab events` : "no lab events",
    });

    // Concept-set breadth — powers FP-Growth: distinct concepts per patient.
    const breadth = views.map((v) => new Set(
      v.events
        .filter((e) => e.category === "comorbidity" || e.category === "medication" || e.category === "symptom")
        .map((e) => `${e.category}|${e.display || e.code}`),
    ).size);
    const avgBreadth = breadth.length ? Math.round((breadth.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / breadth.length) * 10) / 10 : 0;
    const breadthShare = breadth.filter((v) => v >= 5).length / Math.max(1, breadth.length);
    checks.push({
      check: "Concept-set breadth (FP-Growth)",
      status: breadthShare >= 0.5 ? "good" : breadthShare >= 0.2 ? "limited" : "weak",
      detail: `avg ${avgBreadth} distinct concepts/patient; ${Math.round(breadthShare * 100)}% of patients carry ≥5`,
    });

    const weak = checks.filter((c) => c.status === "weak").length;
    const limited = checks.filter((c) => c.status === "limited").length;
    const overall: Status = weak > 0 ? "weak" : limited > 2 ? "limited" : "good";

    return {
      version: "v3 — code + temporal (event stream)",
      overall,
      summary: `${checks.length - weak - limited}/${checks.length} checks good; ${limited} limited; ${weak} weak. Escalation modeled for ${rate(views, (v) => v.escalated === true) > 0 ? "some" : "no"} patients.`,
      rows: checks.map((c) => ({ check: c.check, status: c.status, detail: c.detail })),
    };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's flat PatientEvent stream.",
    process: "Checks stream-level fitness (dated share, events/patient, observation span), per-category patient coverage for all eight event categories, modeled-outcome event counts, 30-day-gap episode structure, and medication course dating. Grades good (≥50%), limited (20–50%), weak (<20%; or zero presence).",
    output: "version, overall grade, summary line, and rows: one {check, status, detail} per fitness check — weak rows name the temporal analytics that can't be trusted on this stream.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "All event categories here are generator output — the assessment measures structure, not truth.",
      "Outcome events are modeled formulas; their 'coverage' grades generator behavior, not observed outcomes.",
    ],
  },
};
Lab value distributions (box + violin)

The same box-and-whisker plus violin per lab concept, computed over the dated lab events in each patient's stream.

 {
  id: "lab-box-plots",
  name: "Lab value distributions (box + violin)",
  category: "Visual analytics",
  description:
    "Per lab concept: quartiles, 1.5×IQR whiskers, outliers, and a mirrored Gaussian-KDE violin — pure arithmetic on the recorded lab values. Same cohort in, identical plot out.",
  requiredCategories: ["lab"],
  run(events, params) {
    const minN = Math.max(3, Number(params?.minN ?? 10));
    const byConcept = new Map<string, Array<{ v: number; unit?: string }>>();
    for (const e of events) {
      if (e.category !== "lab" || e.value === undefined) continue;
      const v = Number(e.value);
      if (!Number.isFinite(v)) continue;
      const key = e.display || e.code || "(unknown)";
      const arr = byConcept.get(key) ?? [];
      arr.push({ v, unit: e.unit });
      byConcept.set(key, arr);
    }
    const stats = [...byConcept.entries()]
      .filter(([, vals]) => vals.length >= minN)
      .map(([concept, vals]) => boxStats(concept, vals))
      .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n)
      .slice(0, 24)
      .map((s) => ({ ...s, values: undefined }));
    return { minN, plots: stats };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's lab events with numeric values; param minN (default 10).",
    process: "Groups values per lab concept; computes min/Q1/median/Q3/max with linear-interpolated quartiles, 1.5×IQR whiskers, outlier count, mean/sd; the violin is a deterministic Gaussian KDE (Silverman bandwidth) over the same values.",
    output: "plots: one per concept {concept, n, min, q1, median, q3, max, loWhisker, hiWhisker, outliers, mean, sd, unit} — rendered as box + violin small multiples.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Whisker/outlier flags are distribution heuristics — flagged values may be real clinical extremes, not errors.",
      "KDE bandwidth is Silverman's rule: it smooths multimodal distributions and can hide sub-populations.",
    ],
  },
};
Event series sparklines

Renders each concept's monthly event counts as a tiny trend line, tallied from the dated events in the stream.

 {
  id: "event-sparklines",
  name: "Event series sparklines",
  category: "Visual analytics",
  description:
    "Per concept (top N by volume): the monthly event-count series rendered as sparkline small multiples — just aggregation of already-dated events, nothing more.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter"],
  run(events, params) {
    const topN = Math.max(3, Number(params?.topN ?? 20));
    // monthly counts per concept across ALL categories with volume
    const series = new Map<string, Map<string, number>>();
    for (const e of events) {
      if (!e.t || !e.display) continue;
      const key = `${e.category}|${e.display}`;
      const m = series.get(key) ?? new Map<string, number>();
      const month = e.t.substring(0, 7);
      m.set(month, (m.get(month) ?? 0) + 1);
      series.set(key, m);
    }
    const allMonths = [...new Set(events.map((e) => e.t?.substring(0, 7) ?? ""))].filter(Boolean).sort();
    const rows = [...series.entries()]
      .map(([key, m]) => {
        const [category, ...rest] = key.split("|");
        const display = rest.join("|");
        const values = allMonths.map((mo) => m.get(mo) ?? 0);
        return {
          concept: display,
          category,
          total: values.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0),
          series: values,
          months: allMonths,
        };
      })
      .sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total)
      .slice(0, topN);
    return { months: allMonths, topN, rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's dated events; param topN (default 20).",
    process: "Tallies events per concept per calendar month; renders each concept's monthly counts as a sparkline — aggregation only.",
    output: "months (x axis), rows: one per concept {concept, category, total, series[]} — small multiples.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Month buckets use event dates as recorded — undated events are invisible here.",
      "Concepts are counted per event, not per patient — frequent re-measurement inflates a line.",
    ],
  },
};
Calendar heatmap

Counts events into year-by-month cells using each event's real date, shading by volume.

 {
  id: "calendar-heatmap",
  name: "Calendar heatmap",
  category: "Visual analytics",
  description:
    "All dated events tallied into year × month cells — a count per cell, nothing more. Optionally filter by event category (param category, default: all).",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter"],
  run(events, params) {
    const category = params?.category && params.category !== "all" ? params.category : null;
    const grid = new Map<string, number>(); // "year|month" → count
    let minYear = 9999;
    let maxYear = 0;
    for (const e of events) {
      if (!e.t || e.t.length < 7) continue;
      if (category && e.category !== category) continue;
      const year = Number(e.t.substring(0, 4));
      const month = Number(e.t.substring(5, 7));
      if (!Number.isFinite(year) || !Number.isFinite(month)) continue;
      const key = `${year}|${month}`;
      grid.set(key, (grid.get(key) ?? 0) + 1);
      if (year < minYear) minYear = year;
      if (year > maxYear) maxYear = year;
    }
    const cells: Array<{ year: number; month: number; n: number }> = [];
    for (let y = minYear; y <= maxYear && maxYear > 0; y++) {
      for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {
        cells.push({ year: y, month: m, n: grid.get(`${y}|${m}`) ?? 0 });
      }
    }
    const byCategory = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const e of events) byCategory.set(e.category, (byCategory.get(e.category) ?? 0) + 1);
    return {
      category: category ?? "all",
      years: maxYear > 0 ? [minYear, maxYear] : [],
      cells: cells.sort((a, b) => a.year - b.year || a.month - b.month),
      totalEvents: events.length,
      byCategory: [...byCategory.entries()].map(([cat, n]) => ({ category: cat, n })).sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n),
    };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's dated events; optional param category to filter (e.g. 'medication').",
    process: "Counts events into year × month cells — a tally, nothing more.",
    output: "cells: one per year/month {year, month, n}, years range, per-category totals — rendered as a calendar heatmap.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Cell intensity reflects recorded event volume — documentation intensity, not disease activity.",
      "Months at cohort edges are partial by construction; edge-column dips are artifacts.",
    ],
  },
};
Comorbidity chord diagram

The same chord diagram, with the pair tallies built from distinct coded concepts in the stream.

 {
  id: "comorbidity-chord",
  name: "Comorbidity chord diagram",
  category: "Visual analytics",
  description:
    "Circular chord diagram of comorbidity co-occurrence: arcs sized by patient count, ribbons by shared patients — a re-render of the co-occurrence tallies over the event stream. Click data comes from the ribbon table below the diagram.",
  requiredCategories: ["comorbidity"],
  run(events, params) {
    const topN = Math.max(3, Math.min(Number(params?.topN ?? 12), 20));
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const counts = conceptIndex(events, "comorbidity");
    const top = [...counts.entries()]
      .sort((a, b) => b[1].n - a[1].n)
      .slice(0, topN);
    const chosen = top.map(([code, info]) => ({ code, display: info.display, patients: [...info.patients] }));
    const chosenSet = new Set(chosen.map((c) => c.code));

    // Per-patient comorbidity sets restricted to the chosen top concepts.
    const pairPatients = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
    for (const v of views) {
      const mine = new Set<string>();
      for (const e of v.events) {
        if (e.category !== "comorbidity") continue;
        const code = e.code || e.display.toLowerCase();
        if (chosenSet.has(code)) mine.add(code);
      }
      const list = [...mine];
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
        for (let j = i + 1; j < list.length; j++) {
          const key = list[i] < list[j] ? `${list[i]}~~${list[j]}` : `${list[j]}~~${list[i]}`;
          const set = pairPatients.get(key) ?? new Set<string>();
          set.add(v.patientId);
          pairPatients.set(key, set);
        }
      }
    }

    const matrix = chosen.map((a) =>
      chosen.map((b) => {
        if (a.code === b.code) return a.patients.length; // diagonal = prevalence
        const key = a.code < b.code ? `${a.code}~~${b.code}` : `${b.code}~~${a.code}`;
        return pairPatients.get(key)?.size ?? 0;
      }),
    );
    return {
      nodes: chosen.map((c) => ({ code: c.code, display: c.display, n: c.patients.length })),
      matrix,
      totalPatients: views.length,
    };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's comorbidity events; param topN (default 12, max 20).",
    process: "Tallies per-patient comorbidity sets restricted to the top-N concepts, then counts shared patients per pair into a symmetric matrix (diagonal = prevalence).",
    output: "nodes [{code, display, n}], matrix[n×n] — rendered as a chord diagram; ribbons between arcs = shared patients.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Ribbons are raw co-occurrence — no comparison to chance (see the knowledge graph's lift for that).",
      "Top-N selection hides rare comorbidity pairs entirely.",
    ],
  },
};
Cox proportional hazards

The same classical Cox fit, with time measured over each patient's event stream: clock starts at the first event, censoring at the last.

 {
  id: "cox-proportional-hazards",
  name: "Cox proportional hazards",
  category: "Statistics",
  description:
    "Classical Cox proportional hazards regression: time from first observed event to the escalation outcome (censored at last event), fitted by Newton–Raphson with Efron's tied-times correction. Fixed optimization over a fixed dataset — converges to identical coefficients every run.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter", "outcome"],
  run(events, params) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const subjects: CoxSubject[] = [];
    for (const v of views) {
      const sorted = [...v.events].sort((a, b) => (a.t < b.t ? -1 : a.t > b.t ? 1 : 0));
      if (sorted.length < 2) continue;
      const start = Date.parse(sorted[0].t);
      const last = Date.parse(sorted[sorted.length - 1].t);
      if (!Number.isFinite(start) || !Number.isFinite(last)) continue;
      const esc = sorted.find((e) => e.category === "outcome" && e.code === "escalation");
      const event: 0 | 1 = esc ? 1 : 0;
      const end = esc ? Date.parse(esc.t) : last;
      const time = Math.max(1, Math.round((end - start) / 86_400_000));
      const age = typeof v.age === "number" ? v.age : 0;
      const male = v.sex === "M" ? 1 : 0;
      const poly = typeof v.polypharmacyCount === "number" ? v.polypharmacyCount : 0;
      subjects.push({ time, event, x: [age / 10, male, Math.min(poly, 10)] });
    }
    if (subjects.length < 20) {
      return { error: `only ${subjects.length} subjects with a measurable follow-up — need ≥20` };
    }

    const p = 3;
    const covariateNames = ["age (per 10y)", "sex M vs F", "polypharmacy count (capped 10)"];
    let beta = new Array(p).fill(0);
    let ll = -Infinity;
    let iterations = 0;
    let converged = false;
    for (let it = 0; it < 30; it++) {
      iterations = it + 1;
      const { grad, hess, ll: candidateLl } = coxGradientHessian(subjects, beta);
      // Solve H delta = grad (p×p Gaussian elimination; p = 3).
      const delta = solve3(hess, grad);
      // Guard against separation blow-up: cap the Newton step norm.
      const norm = Math.sqrt(delta.reduce((s, d) => s + d * d, 0));
      if (norm > 4) for (let k = 0; k < p; k++) delta[k] *= 4 / norm;
      // Step halving for stability.
      let step = 1;
      let newLl = ll;
      for (let half = 0; half < 8; half++) {
        const candidate = beta.map((b, k) => b + step * delta[k]);
        newLl = coxGradientHessian(subjects, candidate).ll;
        if (newLl > ll) {
          beta = candidate;
          break;
        }
        step /= 2;
      }
      if (Math.abs(newLl - ll) < 1e-9) {
        ll = newLl;
        converged = true;
        break;
      }
      ll = newLl;
    }

    const { hess } = coxGradientHessian(subjects, beta);
    // var(β̂) = I⁻¹ → se_k = √((I⁻¹)ₖₖ). Solve H·z = e_k for each column.
    const invDiag = beta.map((_, k) => {
      const e = beta.map((_, m) => (m === k ? 1 : 0));
      const z = solve3(hess, e);
      return Math.sqrt(Math.max(1e-12, z[k]));
    });
    const rows = beta.map((b, k) => {
      const se = invDiag[k] || 1;
      const hr = Math.exp(b);
      return {
        covariate: covariateNames[k],
        coef: round(b, 5),
        se: round(se, 5),
        hr: round(hr, 4),
        ciLo: round(Math.exp(b - 1.96 * se), 4),
        ciHi: round(Math.exp(b + 1.96 * se), 4),
        z: round(b / se, 3),
        pValue: round(normSf2sided(b / se), 5),
      };
    });
    // Harrell's concordance (naive pairwise; tied times count 0.5).
    let concordant = 0, discordant = 0, tied = 0;
    const risks = subjects.map((s) => s.x.reduce((acc, xk, k) => acc + beta[k] * xk, 0));
    for (let i = 0; i < subjects.length; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < subjects.length; j++) {
        const a = subjects[i], b = subjects[j];
        if (a.event === 0 && b.event === 0) continue;
        const [ei, ci] = a.event === 1 ? [i, j] : [j, i];
        if (subjects[ci].time > subjects[ei].time) {
          if (risks[ei] > risks[ci]) concordant++;
          else if (risks[ei] < risks[ci]) discordant++;
          else tied++;
        }
      }
    }
    const cIndex = concordant + discordant + tied > 0
      ? round((concordant + tied / 2) / (concordant + discordant + tied), 4)
      : null;
    return {
      n: subjects.length,
      iterations,
      converged,
      logLikelihood: round(ll, 4),
      concordance: cIndex,
      covariates: covariateNames,
      rows,
    };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; time = first event → escalation outcome (days), censored at last event. Covariates: age (per 10y), sex, polypharmacy count.",
    process: "Newton–Raphson maximisation of the Cox partial likelihood with Efron's tied-times correction and step halving; standard errors from the information matrix; p-values two-sided normal; concordance by naive pairwise comparison.",
    output: "rows: one per covariate {coef, se, hr, ciLo, ciHi, z, pValue}, plus n, log-likelihood, concordance — deterministic to machine precision.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "The survival endpoint is a MODELED outcome event — HRs restate the generator's formulas, not observed biology.",
      "Covariates (age, polypharmacy) also feed the generator's escalation probability — circular by construction.",
      "Censoring at last event assumes independent censoring; administrative-vs-event dropout is not distinguished.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Coefficients are fitted to generator output — treat every HR as a simulation parameter.",
    ],
  },
};

function round(x: number, digits: number): number {
  const f = 10 ** digits;
  return Math.round(x * f) / f;
}

/** Solve a small (3×3) linear system H·delta = grad via Gaussian elimination. */
function solve3(h: number[][], g: number[]): number[] {
  const n = g.length;
  const a = h.map((row, i) => [...row, g[i]]);
  for (let col = 0; col < n; col++) {
    let piv = col;
    for (let r = col + 1; r < n; r++) if (Math.abs(a[r][col]) > Math.abs(a[piv][col])) piv = r;
    [a[col], a[piv]] = [a[piv], a[col]];
    const d = a[col][col] || 1e-12;
    for (let r = 0; r < n; r++) {
      if (r === col) continue;
      const f = a[r][col] / d;
      for (let c = col; c <= n; c++) a[r][c] -= f * a[col][c];
    }
  }
  return a.map((row, i) => row[n] / (a[i][i] || 1e-12));
}

// ── FP-Growth frequent itemsets ──────────────────────────────────────────────

interface FpNode {
  item: string;
  count: number;
  children: Map<string, FpNode>;
  parent: FpNode | null;
}

export const frequentItemsets: Analytic = {
  id: "frequent-itemsets",
  name: "Frequent itemsets (FP-Growth)",
  category: "Statistics",
  description:
    "FP-Growth over transactions = each patient's distinct concept set (comorbidities + medications + symptoms). Deterministic tree traversal: same cohort in, same itemsets and support counts every run. Supports reveal which concept bundles travel together.",
  requiredCategories: ["comorbidity"],
  run(events, params) {
    const minSupportPct = Math.max(1, Number(params?.minSupportPct ?? 10));
    const maxItems = Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(params?.maxItemsPerSet ?? 3), 4));
    const maxSets = Math.max(10, Number(params?.maxSets ?? 200));

    // Transactions: per-patient distinct concept labels (top-K concepts to
    // bound combinatorics, default 40 by patient count).
    const topK = Math.max(5, Math.min(Number(params?.topConcepts ?? 40), 100));
    const counts = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const [, info] of conceptIndex(events, "comorbidity")) counts.set(`com:${info.display}`, info.n);
    for (const [, info] of conceptIndex(events, "medication")) counts.set(`med:${info.display}`, info.n);
    for (const [, info] of conceptIndex(events, "symptom")) counts.set(`sym:${info.display}`, info.n);
    const frequent1 = [...counts.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1] || a[0].localeCompare(b[0])).slice(0, topK);
    const allowed = new Map(frequent1);

    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const transactions: string[][] = [];
    for (const v of views) {
      const seen = new Set<string>();
      for (const e of v.events) {
        if (e.category !== "comorbidity" && e.category !== "medication" && e.category !== "symptom") continue;
        const key = `${e.category === "comorbidity" ? "com" : e.category === "medication" ? "med" : "sym"}:${e.display}`;
        if (allowed.has(key)) seen.add(key);
      }
      if (seen.size > 0) transactions.push([...seen]);
    }
    const nTrans = transactions.length;
    if (nTrans === 0) return { error: "no transactions — no patient carries a top concept" };
    const minCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((minSupportPct / 100) * nTrans));

    // FP-tree build (items sorted by global frequency desc, ties by label).
    const order = new Map(frequent1.map(([k], i) => [k, i]));
    const itemOrder = (a: string, b: string) =>
      (allowed.get(b)! - allowed.get(a)!) || a.localeCompare(b);

    const root: FpNode = { item: "(root)", count: 0, children: new Map(), parent: null };
    for (const t of transactions) {
      const items = [...new Set(t)].sort(itemOrder);
      let node = root;
      for (const item of items) {
        let child = node.children.get(item);
        if (!child) {
          child = { item, count: 0, children: new Map(), parent: node };
          node.children.set(item, child);
        }
        child.count++;
        node = child;
      }
    }

    // Mine: for each item (frequency order), build its conditional pattern
    // base by walking the tree, then recursively mine conditional trees.
    const results: Array<{ items: string[]; count: number; supportPct: number }> = [];
    const seenSets = new Set<string>();
    const mine = (prefix: string[], base: Array<[string[], number]>) => {
      if (results.length >= maxSets) return;
      // Conditional FP-tree over the pattern base.
      const localCounts = new Map<string, number>();
      for (const [pattern, cnt] of base) {
        for (const item of pattern) localCounts.set(item, (localCounts.get(item) ?? 0) + cnt);
      }
      const localItems = [...localCounts.entries()]
        .filter(([, c]) => c >= minCount)
        .sort((a, b) => itemOrder(a[0], b[0]));
      if (localItems.length === 0) return;

      const localRoot: FpNode = { item: "(root)", count: 0, children: new Map(), parent: null };
      for (const [pattern, cnt] of base) {
        const items = pattern.filter((it) => (localCounts.get(it) ?? 0) >= minCount).sort(itemOrder);
        let node = localRoot;
        for (const item of items) {
          let child = node.children.get(item);
          if (!child) {
            child = { item, count: 0, children: new Map(), parent: node };
            node.children.set(item, child);
          }
          child.count += cnt;
          node = child;
        }
      }
      for (const [item] of localItems) {
        const newPrefix = [...prefix, item].sort();
        const key = newPrefix.join("|");
        if (seenSets.has(key)) continue;
        seenSets.add(key);
        // Support of prefix∪item = the conditional count collected above.
        let support = 0;
        for (const [pattern, cnt] of base) {
          if (pattern.includes(item)) support += cnt;
        }
        if (support < minCount) continue;
        results.push({ items: newPrefix, count: support, supportPct: Math.round((support / nTrans) * 1000) / 10 });
        // Conditional base for the extended prefix.
        const childBase: Array<[string[], number]> = [];
        const walk = (nd: FpNode, path: string[]) => {
          for (const child of nd.children.values()) {
            const newPath = [...path, child.item];
            if (child.item === item) {
              childBase.push([path, child.count]);
            } else {
              walk(child, newPath);
            }
          }
        };
        walk(localRoot, []);
        if (childBase.length > 0 && newPrefix.length < maxItems) mine(newPrefix, childBase);
      }
    };

    // Seed: conditional bases of each frequent-1 item from the main tree.
    for (const [item] of [...allowed.entries()].sort((a, b) => itemOrder(a[0], b[0]))) {
      const base: Array<[string[], number]> = [];
      const walk = (nd: FpNode, path: string[]) => {
        for (const child of nd.children.values()) {
          const newPath = [...path, child.item];
          if (child.item === item) {
            base.push([path, child.count]);
          } else {
            walk(child, newPath);
          }
        }
      };
      walk(root, []);
      if (base.length === 0) continue;
      // A frequent-1 item's support = patients carrying it.
      const support = allowed.get(item)!;
      results.push({
        items: [item],
        count: support,
        supportPct: Math.round((support / nTrans) * 1000) / 10,
      });
      if (results.length >= maxSets) break;
      mine([item], base);
    }

    results.sort((a, b) => b.items.length - a.items.length || b.count - a.count || a.items.join().localeCompare(b.items.join()));
    return {
      transactions: nTrans,
      minSupportPct,
      minCount,
      conceptsConsidered: allowed.size,
      rows: results.slice(0, maxSets).map((r) => ({
        itemset: r.items.map((x) => x.replace(/^(com|med|sym):/, "")).join(" + "),
        size: r.items.length,
        count: r.count,
        supportPct: r.supportPct,
      })),
    };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's comorbidity/medication/symptom events; params minSupportPct (default 10), maxItemsPerSet (≤4), topConcepts (default 40 by patient count).",
    process: "Builds one transaction per patient (their distinct concept set), constructs the FP-tree with items in global-frequency order, then recursively mines conditional pattern bases — the textbook FP-Growth traversal. Prunes itemsets below minSupportPct.",
    output: "rows: one per frequent itemset {itemset, size, count, supportPct}, deterministic order (largest sets first, then support desc) — same cohort in, identical output every run.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Frequent co-occurrence ≠ association strength — FP-Growth has no comparison to chance; pair it with the knowledge graph's lift.",
      "Restricting to top-K concepts bounds combinatorics but hides rare-item combinations.",
      "Itemsets are generator pools sampled independently — true structure here is unlikely by construction.",
    ],
  },
};

export const STATS_ANALYTICS: Analytic[] = [coxProportionalHazards, frequentItemsets];
Frequent itemsets (FP-Growth)

The same FP-Growth mining over each patient's distinct coded concept set from the stream.

 {
  id: "frequent-itemsets",
  name: "Frequent itemsets (FP-Growth)",
  category: "Statistics",
  description:
    "FP-Growth over transactions = each patient's distinct concept set (comorbidities + medications + symptoms). Deterministic tree traversal: same cohort in, same itemsets and support counts every run. Supports reveal which concept bundles travel together.",
  requiredCategories: ["comorbidity"],
  run(events, params) {

    const maxItems = Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(params?.maxItemsPerSet ?? 3), 4));
    const maxSets = Math.max(10, Number(params?.maxSets ?? 200));

    // Transactions: per-patient distinct concept labels (top-K concepts to
    // bound combinatorics, default 40 by patient count).

    for (const [, info] of conceptIndex(events, "medication")) counts.set(`med:${info.display}`, info.n);

    const allowed = new Map(frequent1);

    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const transactions: string[][] = [];

        const key = `${e.category === "comorbidity" ? "com" : e.category === "medication" ? "med" : "sym"}:${e.display}`;
        if (allowed.has(key)) seen.add(key);
      }
      if (seen.size > 0) transactions.push([...seen]);
    }
    const nTrans = transactions.length;
    if (nTrans === 0) return { error: "no transactions — no patient carries a top concept" };
    const minCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((minSupportPct / 100) * nTrans));

    // FP-tree build (items sorted by global frequency desc, ties by label).

    const itemOrder = (a: string, b: string) =>
      (allowed.get(b)! - allowed.get(a)!) || a.localeCompare(b);

    const root: FpNode = { item: "(root)", count: 0, children: new Map(), parent: null };
    for (const t of transactions) {
      const items = [...new Set(t)].sort(itemOrder);
      let node = root;
      for (const item of items) {
        let child = node.children.get(item);
        if (!child) {
          child = { item, count: 0, children: new Map(), parent: node };
          node.children.set(item, child);
        }
        child.count++;
        node = child;
      }
    }

    // Mine: for each item (frequency order), build its conditional pattern
    // base by walking the tree, then recursively mine conditional trees.
    const results: Array<{ items: string[]; count: number; supportPct: number }> = [];
    const seenSets = new Set<string>();
    const mine = (prefix: string[], base: Array<[string[], number]>) => {
      if (results.length >= maxSets) return;
      // Conditional FP-tree over the pattern base.
      const localCounts = new Map<string, number>();
      for (const [pattern, cnt] of base) {
        for (const item of pattern) localCounts.set(item, (localCounts.get(item) ?? 0) + cnt);
      }
      const localItems = [...localCounts.entries()]
        .filter(([, c]) => c >= minCount)
        .sort((a, b) => itemOrder(a[0], b[0]));
      if (localItems.length === 0) return;

      const localRoot: FpNode = { item: "(root)", count: 0, children: new Map(), parent: null };
      for (const [pattern, cnt] of base) {
        const items = pattern.filter((it) => (localCounts.get(it) ?? 0) >= minCount).sort(itemOrder);
        let node = localRoot;
        for (const item of items) {
          let child = node.children.get(item);
          if (!child) {
            child = { item, count: 0, children: new Map(), parent: node };
            node.children.set(item, child);
          }
          child.count += cnt;
          node = child;
        }
      }
      for (const [item] of localItems) {
        const newPrefix = [...prefix, item].sort();
        const key = newPrefix.join("|");
        if (seenSets.has(key)) continue;
        seenSets.add(key);
        // Support of prefix∪item = the conditional count collected above.
        let support = 0;
        for (const [pattern, cnt] of base) {
          if (pattern.includes(item)) support += cnt;
        }
        if (support < minCount) continue;
        results.push({ items: newPrefix, count: support, supportPct: Math.round((support / nTrans) * 1000) / 10 });
        // Conditional base for the extended prefix.
        const childBase: Array<[string[], number]> = [];
        const walk = (nd: FpNode, path: string[]) => {
          for (const child of nd.children.values()) {
            const newPath = [...path, child.item];
            if (child.item === item) {
              childBase.push([path, child.count]);
            } else {
              walk(child, newPath);
            }
          }
        };
        walk(localRoot, []);
        if (childBase.length > 0 && newPrefix.length < maxItems) mine(newPrefix, childBase);
      }
    };

    // Seed: conditional bases of each frequent-1 item from the main tree.
    for (const [item] of [...allowed.entries()].sort((a, b) => itemOrder(a[0], b[0]))) {
      const base: Array<[string[], number]> = [];
      const walk = (nd: FpNode, path: string[]) => {
        for (const child of nd.children.values()) {
          const newPath = [...path, child.item];
          if (child.item === item) {
            base.push([path, child.count]);
          } else {
            walk(child, newPath);
          }
        }
      };
      walk(root, []);
      if (base.length === 0) continue;
      // A frequent-1 item's support = patients carrying it.
      const support = allowed.get(item)!;
      results.push({
        items: [item],
        count: support,
        supportPct: Math.round((support / nTrans) * 1000) / 10,
      });
      if (results.length >= maxSets) break;
      mine([item], base);
    }

    results.sort((a, b) => b.items.length - a.items.length || b.count - a.count || a.items.join().localeCompare(b.items.join()));
    return {
      transactions: nTrans,
      minSupportPct,
      minCount,
      conceptsConsidered: allowed.size,
      rows: results.slice(0, maxSets).map((r) => ({
        itemset: r.items.map((x) => x.replace(/^(com|med|sym):/, "")).join(" + "),
        size: r.items.length,
        count: r.count,
        supportPct: r.supportPct,
      })),
    };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's comorbidity/medication/symptom events; params minSupportPct (default 10), maxItemsPerSet (≤4), topConcepts (default 40 by patient count).",
    process: "Builds one transaction per patient (their distinct concept set), constructs the FP-tree with items in global-frequency order, then recursively mines conditional pattern bases — the textbook FP-Growth traversal. Prunes itemsets below minSupportPct.",
    output: "rows: one per frequent itemset {itemset, size, count, supportPct}, deterministic order (largest sets first, then support desc) — same cohort in, identical output every run.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Frequent co-occurrence ≠ association strength — FP-Growth has no comparison to chance; pair it with the knowledge graph's lift.",
      "Restricting to top-K concepts bounds combinatorics but hides rare-item combinations.",
      "Itemsets are generator pools sampled independently — true structure here is unlikely by construction.",
    ],
  },
};

export const STATS_ANALYTICS: Analytic[] = [coxProportionalHazards, frequentItemsets];
Cohort overview by age band

The same age-band table, computed from each patient's event stream, with stay length taken from the actual admission-to-discharge events.

 {
  id: "age-bands",
  name: "Cohort overview by age band",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "Headcount, share of cohort, median episode length of stay, and 90-day readmission rate per age band, from each patient's event stream.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter", "outcome"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const stays = episodeStays(views);
    const bands = ["45–64", "65–74", "75+"];
    const rows = bands.map((band) => {
      const members = views.filter((v) => ageBand(v.age) === band);
      const los = stays.filter((s) => members.some((m) => m.patientId === s.patientId)).map((s) => s.los);
      return {
        band,
        count: members.length,
        pct: views.length ? members.length / views.length : 0,
        medianLos: median(los),
        readmitPct: rate(members, (v) => v.readmitted90d === true),
      };
    });
    return { rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; age from the encounter event's attrs, readmission flag from outcome events, stays from 30-day-gap episodes.",
    process: "Bands patients by age (45–64, 65–74, 75+), then counts each band, its share of the cohort, the median episode LOS, and the readmission rate within it.",
    output: "rows: one per age band with {band, count, pct, medianLos, readmitPct} — a demographic baseline, not a claim of causation.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "readmitPct per band is circular: readmission outcome events are modeled with age among the formula inputs.",
      "Age gradient is injected by the generator, not discovered from care.",
      "medianLos describes the modeled event dates and 30-day gap rule, not throughput.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Readmission by age band restates the generator's age-dependent outcome formula.",
    ],
  },
};
Payer mix

The same insurance comparison, with stay length measured from real admission and discharge events.

 {
  id: "payer-mix",
  name: "Payer mix",
  category: "Public health / population",
  description:
    "Escalation, 90-day readmission, and mean episode length of stay by insurance type, sorted by escalation rate.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter", "outcome"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const stays = episodeStays(views);
    const rows = [...groupBy(views, (v) => v.insurance ?? "(unknown)").entries()].map(([ins, g]) => ({
      ins,
      n: g.length,
      escRate: rate(g, (v) => v.escalated === true),
      readmitRate: rate(g, (v) => v.readmitted90d === true),
      los: mean(stays.filter((s) => g.some((v) => v.patientId === s.patientId)), (s) => s.los),
    }));
    return { rows: rows.sort((a, b) => b.escRate - a.escRate) };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; insurance from the encounter event's attrs, escalation/readmission from outcome events, stays from gap-episodes.",
    process: "Groups patients by insurance type, computes escalation rate, 90-day readmission rate, and mean episode LOS per payer; sorted by escalation rate descending.",
    output: "rows: one per payer with {ins, n, escRate, readmitRate, los} — outcomes by coverage type.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "escRate and readmitRate read modeled outcome events — any payer gap is generator-injected association.",
      "Insurance proxies socioeconomic position; this is not a protected-class analysis.",
      "Sorted worst-first with no minimum n or uncertainty — small payers can top the table.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Outcomes by payer are generator-injected, not access evidence.",
    ],
  },
};
Region × insurance

Builds a table of every region-and-insurance combination and reports outcomes in each cell, showing where access disadvantages stack up.

 {
  id: "region-insurance",
  name: "Region × insurance",
  category: "Public health / population",
  description:
    "Escalation and readmission rates for every region × insurance combination with at least five patients — where access disadvantage compounds.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter", "outcome"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const cells = groupBy(views, (v) => `${v.region ?? "?"}|${v.insurance ?? "?"}`);
    const rows = [...cells.entries()]
      .map(([k, g]) => {
        const [region, insurance] = k.split("|");
        return {
          label: `${region} × ${insurance}`,
          n: g.length,
          escRate: rate(g, (v) => v.escalated === true),
          readmitRate: rate(g, (v) => v.readmitted90d === true),
        };
      })
      .filter((r) => r.n >= 5)
      .sort((a, b) => b.escRate - a.escRate);
    return { rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; region and insurance from the encounter event's attrs, escalation/readmission from outcome events.",
    process: "Cross-tabs every region × insurance combination with at least 5 patients, computing escalation and 90-day readmission rates per cell; sorted worst escalation first.",
    output: "rows: one per combination with {label, n, escRate, readmitRate} — where access disadvantage compounds with outcomes.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "Region × insurance proxies socioeconomic disadvantage; no protected-class field exists.",
      "Cell rates read modeled outcome events — gaps are generator artifacts, and n≥5 cells are too small to read.",
      "Cells under 5 patients are silently dropped, biasing the visible table toward common strata.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Both stratifiers and outcomes are modeled — the compounding pattern is planted.",
    ],
  },
};
Age × sex × subgroup

Cross-tabulates age band, sex and subgroup all at once, and shows the twelve combinations with the worst escalation rates (only combinations with enough patients to read).

 {
  id: "triple-strat",
  name: "Age × sex × subgroup",
  category: "Public health / population",
  description:
    "Three-way stratification: escalation and readmission rates by age band, sex, and subgroup, for strata with enough patients to read.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter", "outcome"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const strata = groupBy(views, (v) => `${ageBand(v.age)}|${v.sex || "?"}|${subgroupOf(v)}`);
    const rows = [...strata.entries()]
      .map(([k, g]) => {
        const [age, sex, subgroup] = k.split("|");
        return {
          label: `${age} · ${sex} · ${subgroup}`,
          n: g.length,
          escRate: rate(g, (v) => v.escalated === true),
          readmitRate: rate(g, (v) => v.readmitted90d === true),
        };
      })
      .filter((r) => r.n >= 5)
      .sort((a, b) => b.escRate - a.escRate)
      .slice(0, 12);
    return { rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; age, sex, and subgroup from the encounter event's attrs, escalation/readmission from outcome events.",
    process: "Cross-tabs age band (45–64, 65–74, 75+) × sex × subgroup, keeps strata with at least 5 patients, computes escalation and readmission rates, returns the 12 worst by escalation.",
    output: "rows: one per stratum with {label, n, escRate, readmitRate} — the three-way stratification view.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "Age and subgroup are generator inputs to the modeled outcome formulas — the three-way gradient is injected.",
      "Worst-12 by escalation is a winner's curse over small n≥5 strata — noisiest cells surface.",
      "Subgroup is a coded proxy, not a protected class — rows mislabel as equity if read that way.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Modeled outcomes stratified by variables that feed the outcome formulas — circular by construction.",
    ],
  },
};
LOS distribution

The stay-length histogram, where each stay now comes from real dates in the event stream.

 {
  id: "los-distribution",
  name: "LOS distribution",
  category: "Clinical / diagnostic",
  description:
    "The full per-episode length-of-stay histogram (not just the median): how episodes spread across short, typical, and extended stays.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const stays = episodeStays(views);
    const bands: Array<[number, number, string]> = [
      [0, 3, "0–3d"], [4, 7, "4–7d"], [8, 14, "8–14d"], [15, 21, "15–21d"], [22, 999, "22d+"],
    ];
    const rows = bands.map(([lo, hi, label]) => {
      const g = stays.filter((s) => s.los >= lo && s.los <= hi);
      return { label, n: g.length, share: stays.length ? g.length / stays.length : 0 };
    });
    return { rows, median: median(stays.map((s) => s.los)), episodes: stays.length, patients: views.length };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; stays built per patient as 30-day-gap episodes, LOS from each episode's first-to-last event dates.",
    process: "Computes per-episode LOS, bins episodes into five bands (0–3, 4–7, 8–14, 15–21, 22+ days), and counts episodes and share per band, plus the overall median.",
    output: "rows: one per band with {label, n, share}, plus median, episodes, patients — the full episode-level LOS histogram.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Episode LOS is date arithmetic over modeled event dates; the 30-day gap rule defines a stay, not bed days.",
      "Single-day episodes count as 0 days — the 0–3d band absorbs patients whose dated events cluster on one day.",
      "Descriptive-only; safe as a plumbing check, misleading as an operations baseline.",
    ],
  },
};
High-utilizers

Takes the 10% of patients with the most recorded events and compares them to everyone else on outcomes, medications and labs.

 {
  id: "high-utilizers",
  name: "High-utilizers",
  category: "Public health / population",
  description:
    "The top decile of patients by event volume within their observation period, profiled against everyone else on outcomes, medications, and labs.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    // utilization = the patient's own event count within their observation
    // period (first to last event), not a fixed 90-day window
    const sorted = [...views].sort((a, b) => b.events.length - a.events.length);
    const cut = Math.max(1, Math.round(views.length * 0.1));
    const hi = sorted.slice(0, cut);
    const rest = sorted.slice(cut);
    const byCatHi = groupBy(hi, (v) => v.category ?? "(unknown)");
    const catRows = [...byCatHi.entries()]
      .map(([cat, g]) => ({ cat, n: g.length }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n);
    return { hi: summarizeUtil(hi), rest: summarizeUtil(rest), catRows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; utilization is each patient's event count within their own observation period, plus medication/lab counts and outcome flags.",
    process: "Sorts patients by event count, takes the top decile (minimum 1), and profiles that group against the rest on events, escalation, readmission, medications, and labs; counts the top decile per category.",
    output: "hi and rest summaries {n, eventsPerPatient, escRate, readmit, meds, labs}, plus catRows {cat, n} — the utilization-outcome contrast at the top decile.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "Event volume is generated data with intensity tied to polypharmacy and acuity draws — the top decile is a generator artifact.",
      "escRate/readmit contrast is circular: polypharmacyCount feeds both utilization intensity and the modeled outcome formulas.",
      "Fixed top-decile cut has no clinical threshold; membership flips with cohort size.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Utilization is modeled, not observed — do not use the hi/rest contrast for targeting.",
    ],
  },
};

const utilizationReadmit: Analytic = {
  id: "utilization-readmit",

  category: "Public health / population",
  description:
    "Readmission and escalation rates by banded event volume within each patient's observation period — does prior utilization track what happens next?",
  requiredCategories: ["outcome"],
  run(events) {

    const bands: Array<[number, number, string]> = [

    ];

      const g = views.filter((v) => v.events.length >= lo && v.events.length <= hi);
      return {
        label,
        n: g.length,
        readmitRate: rate(g, (v) => v.readmitted90d === true),
        escRate: rate(g, (v) => v.escalated === true),
      };
    }).filter((r) => r.n > 0);
    return { rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; each patient's total event count within their observation period, plus escalation and 90-day readmission outcome events.",
    process: "Bands patients by event count (0–3, 4–6, 7–9, 10+), then computes the readmission rate and escalation rate within each band; empty bands are dropped.",
    output: "rows: one per non-empty band with {label, n, readmitRate, escRate} — the utilization-outcome gradient.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "Event volume is a modeled draw — the bands partition generator output, not observed utilization.",
      "readmitRate and escRate are themselves formula outputs — the gradient compares generator draws.",
      "Volume and outcome formulas share inputs (polypharmacy, comorbidity) — the gradient is injected, never observed.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Both axes are modeled — the utilization-outcome gradient is not evidence.",
    ],
  },
};

// ── test-intensity ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const testIntensity: Analytic = {
  id: "test-intensity",
  name: "Test-ordering intensity",
  category: "Clinical / diagnostic",
  description:
    "Lab events per episode, banded: does test volume track comorbidity burden or pad the stay?",
  requiredCategories: ["lab", "comorbidity", "encounter", "outcome"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const comCount = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const v of views) {
      comCount.set(v.patientId, v.events.filter((e) => e.category === "comorbidity").length);
    }
    // per-episode lab counts: labs whose date falls inside each episode span
    const rows: Array<{ label: string; n: number; avgComorbidities: number; avgLos: number; escRate: number }> = [];
    const bands: Array<[number, number, string]> = [
      [0, 2, "0–2 tests"], [3, 4, "3–4 tests"], [5, 99, "5+ tests"],
    ];
    const perEpisode: Array<{ patientId: string; los: number; labs: number }> = [];
    for (const v of views) {
      const eps = buildEpisodes(v.events, { gapDays: 30 });
      for (const ep of eps) {
        const labs = v.events.filter(
          (e) => e.category === "lab" && e.t >= ep.start && e.t <= ep.end,
        ).length;
        perEpisode.push({
          patientId: v.patientId,
          los: Math.max(0, Math.round((Date.parse(ep.end) - Date.parse(ep.start)) / DAY)),
          labs,
        });
      }
    }
    for (const [lo, hi, label] of bands) {
      const g = perEpisode.filter((s) => s.labs >= lo && s.labs <= hi);
      if (g.length === 0) continue;
      rows.push({
        label,
        n: g.length,
        avgComorbidities: mean([...new Set(g.map((s) => s.patientId))], (pid) => comCount.get(pid)),
        avgLos: mean(g, (s) => s.los),
        escRate: rate(views.filter((v) => g.some((s) => s.patientId === v.patientId)), (v) => v.escalated === true),
      });
    }
    return { rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; lab events per 30-day-gap episode, comorbidity-event count per patient, episode LOS, and escalation outcome events.",
    process: "Counts lab events inside each episode, bands episodes by lab count (0–2, 3–4, 5+), then averages comorbidity count and episode LOS and computes the patient escalation rate per band.",
    output: "rows: one per band with {label, n, avgComorbidities, avgLos, escRate} — read as whether test volume tracks acuity or pads the stay.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "escRate by test band is circular: comorbidity burden drives both modeled test volume and the modeled escalation formula.",
      "avgLos comparison invites a 'tests pad the stay' reading, but episode LOS is date arithmetic over generated events.",
      "Labs dated by the generator, not order entry — band counts measure the generator's intensity parameter.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Test-volume bands and outcomes come from the same generator formulas — the contrast is injected.",
    ],
  },
};

// ── cohort-details ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const cohortDetails: Analytic = {
  id: "cohort-details",
  name: "Cohort details",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "One row per patient in the active cohort: demographics, category, episode count, event volume, and modeled outcome flags — the drill-down table behind the overview.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter"],
  run(events, params) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const limit = Math.max(1, Number(params?.limit ?? 200));
    const staysByPatient = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const s of episodeStays(views)) {
      staysByPatient.set(s.patientId, (staysByPatient.get(s.patientId) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
    const rows = views
      .map((v) => {
        const enc = v.events.find((e) => e.category === "encounter");
        return {
          patientId: v.patientId,
          age: v.age ?? null,
          sex: v.sex || null,
          insurance: v.insurance || null,
          region: v.region || null,
          category: v.category || null,
          losDays: v.losDays ?? null,
          episodes: staysByPatient.get(v.patientId) ?? 0,
          events: v.events.length,
          meds: v.events.filter((e) => e.category === "medication").length,
          labs: v.events.filter((e) => e.category === "lab").length,
          polypharmacyCount: v.polypharmacyCount ?? null,
          escalated: v.escalated === true,
          readmitted90d: v.readmitted90d === true,
          system: String(enc?.system ?? ""),
        };
      })
      .sort((a, b) => (a.patientId < b.patientId ? -1 : 1))
      .slice(0, limit);
    return { rows, total: views.length, truncated: views.length > rows.length };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; demographics and losDays from the encounter event's attrs, outcome flags from outcome events, episode count from 30-day-gap episodes.",
    process: "Indexes one view per patient, counts episodes, events, medication and lab events, copies demographics and modeled outcome flags, and returns up to params.limit rows (default 200), patientId order.",
    output: "rows: one per patient {patientId, demographics, category, losDays, episodes, events, meds, labs, escalated, readmitted90d}, plus total and truncated — the drill-down table.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "escalated/readmitted90d columns restate the modeled outcome events — flags, not observed outcomes.",
      "Episode count depends on the 30-day gap rule; patients with sparse dating collapse into fewer episodes.",
      "Default 200-row cap truncates large cohorts; total/truncated tell you when the table is partial.",
    ],
  },
};

export const DESCRIPTIVE_ANALYTICS: Analytic[] = [
  cohortOverview,
  ageBands,

  regionInsurance,
  tripleStrat,
  losDistribution,
  losDisposition,
  highUtilizers,
  utilizationReadmit,
  testIntensity,
  cohortDetails,
];
