Care gaps

Flags a patient with a follow-up gap or a refill gap when their last encounter or medication event is too old, then aggregates the rates per category.

 {
  id: "care-gaps",
  name: "Care gaps",
  category: "Patient / evidence",
  description:
    "Missed follow-up (no recent encounter) and refill-gap (no recent medication event) rates per category from dated events — the operational to-do list.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter", "medication"],
  run(events, params) {
    const withinDays = Math.max(1, Number(params?.withinDays ?? 90));
    const asOf = params?.asOf || latestDate(events);
    if (!asOf) return { asOf: null, rows: [], ready: false };
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const byPatient = new Map(views.map((v) => [v.patientId, v.events]));
    const fu = gapAnalysis(byPatient, "encounter", "", asOf, withinDays);
    const refill = gapAnalysis(byPatient, "medication", "", asOf, withinDays);
    const fuGap = new Set([...fu.overdue.map((r) => r.patientId), ...fu.never]);
    const refGap = new Set([...refill.overdue.map((r) => r.patientId), ...refill.never]);
    const rows = [...groupBy(views, (v) => v.category ?? "(unknown)").entries()].map(([cat, g]) => {
      const pids = g.map((v) => v.patientId);
      const missedFu = pids.filter((p) => fuGap.has(p)).length;
      const refillGap = pids.filter((p) => refGap.has(p)).length;
      return {
        cat,
        n: g.length,
        missedFuRate: pids.length ? missedFu / pids.length : 0,
        refillGapRate: pids.length ? refillGap / pids.length : 0,
        openGaps: pids.filter((p) => fuGap.has(p) && refGap.has(p)).length,
      };
    }).sort((a, b) => b.missedFuRate - a.missedFuRate);
    return { asOf, withinDays, rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream: encounter and medication events; params withinDays (default 90), asOf (default = latest event date).",
    process: "Per patient, flags a follow-up gap when the last encounter is older than withinDays before asOf (or absent), and a refill gap when the last medication event is; aggregates rates per category, worst first.",
    output: "asOf, withinDays, rows per category {cat, n, missedFuRate, refillGapRate, openGaps} — an operational to-do list.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "A 'gap' means no recent documentation, which conflates missed care with missed recording.",
      "asOf defaults to the stream's latest event — cohort-edge patients look artificially current.",
      "openGaps requires both gaps at once — read it as conjunction, not a total.",
    ],
  },
};
Guideline adherence

Uses the medication courses in the event stream as the adherence proxy (any active or ongoing course counts) and compares outcomes for adherent versus non-adherent patients.

 {
  id: "guideline-adherence",
  name: "Guideline adherence",
  category: "Patient / evidence",
  description:
    "Documented-therapy adherence per category — an active or ongoing medication course as the proxy — and the escalation-rate gap between adherent and non-adherent patients.",
  requiredCategories: ["medication", "outcome", "encounter"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const rows = [...groupBy(views, (v) => v.category ?? "(unknown)").entries()].map(([cat, g]) => {
      const adherent = g.filter((v) => adherentPatient(v.events));
      const nonAdherent = g.filter((v) => !adherentPatient(v.events));
      return {
        cat,
        n: g.length,
        adherentRate: g.length ? adherent.length / g.length : 0,
        escRateAdherent: adherent.length ? adherent.filter((v) => v.escalated).length / adherent.length : 0,
        escRateNonAdherent: nonAdherent.length ? nonAdherent.filter((v) => v.escalated).length / nonAdherent.length : 0,
      };
    }).sort((a, b) => a.adherentRate - b.adherentRate);
    return { rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream: medication events (status/start/end), encounter attributes, outcome events.",
    process: "Proxies adherence as having any medication course documented active, completed, or without a recorded end; computes the adherent share per category and the escalation rate separately for adherent vs non-adherent patients.",
    output: "rows per category {cat, n, adherentRate, escRateAdherent, escRateNonAdherent} — the adherence-outcome gap per category.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "The proxy is documentation status, not behavior — 'adherent' means a course is on file.",
      "Medication burden feeds the modeled escalation outcome — the adherent-vs-not gap is partly circular.",
      "Adherence sorted lowest-first with no minimum-n flag on categories.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Stratifying a modeled outcome by a medication-based proxy re-exposes the generator.",
    ],
  },
};
Adherence × SDOH

The same adherence proxy, compared across insurance classes against the privately insured reference rate.

 {
  id: "adherence-sdoh",
  name: "Adherence × SDOH",
  category: "Patient / evidence",
  description:
    "Documented-therapy adherence by insurance class versus privately insured patients — an access-barrier proxy separating 'won't take' from 'can't get' on the event stream.",
  requiredCategories: ["medication", "encounter"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const refLabel = "Private";
    const ref = views.filter((v) => v.insurance === refLabel);
    const refRate = rate(ref, (v) => adherentPatient(v.events));
    const rows = [...groupBy(views, (v) => v.insurance || "(unknown)").entries()]
      .filter(([label]) => label !== refLabel)
      .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
      .map(([label, g]) => ({
        label,
        n: g.length,
        adherentRate: rate(g, (v) => adherentPatient(v.events)),
        refRate,
      }));
    return { rows, refLabel, refN: ref.length };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream: medication events and encounter attributes (insurance).",
    process: "Proxies adherence as any active/ongoing medication course, groups patients by insurance class, and computes adherence per class against the privately insured reference rate.",
    output: "rows per insurance class {label, n, adherentRate, refRate}, plus refLabel and refN — access-related adherence contrasts.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Insurance stands in for SDOH barriers — it is a coarse access proxy, not the barrier itself.",
      "The adherence measure is documentation status; under-documentation can masquerade as non-adherence.",
      "Small classes carry unstable rates — no minimum-n guard.",
    ],
  },
};
Query engine

Filters the event stream by category, text match and date window — and understands code families: picking a parent code automatically includes all its more specific child codes.

 {
  id: "query-engine",
  name: "Query engine",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "Filter the event stream by category, code-or-display substring, and optional date window; supports SNOMED/RxNorm subsumption so a parent code matches its descendants; returns matched patients and event counts.",
  requiredCategories: [],
  run(events, params) {
    const cat = (params?.category as EventCategory) ?? "diagnosis";
    const qRaw = params?.q ?? "";
    const q = qRaw.toLowerCase();
    const code = params?.code ?? "";
    const from = params?.from ?? "";
    const to = params?.to ?? "";
    // Subsumption: an exact code on a hierarchical category pulls in descendants.
    const codes = code ? descendants(sysFor(cat), code) : null;
    const matched = events.filter((e) => {
      if (e.category !== cat) return false;
      if (from && e.t < from) return false;
      if (to && e.t > to) return false;
      const displayHit = q ? e.display.toLowerCase().includes(q) : false;
      if (codes) return codes.has(e.code) || displayHit;
      if (q) return e.code === qRaw || e.code.toLowerCase().includes(q) || displayHit;
      return true;
    });
    const perPatient = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const e of matched) perPatient.set(e.patientId, (perPatient.get(e.patientId) ?? 0) + 1);
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const hit = views.filter((v) => perPatient.has(v.patientId));
    const rest = views.filter((v) => !perPatient.has(v.patientId));
    return {
      category: cat,
      q: qRaw,
      code: code || null,
      expandedCodes: codes ? [...codes].sort() : null,
      from: from || null,
      to: to || null,
      nPatients: perPatient.size,
      nEvents: matched.length,
      eventsByPatient: [...perPatient.entries()].map(([patientId, n]) => ({ patientId, n })),
      escRateHit: rate(hit, (v) => v.escalated === true),
      escRateRest: rate(rest, (v) => v.escalated === true),
    };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; params category (default 'diagnosis'), q (code-or-display substring), code (exact code triggering SNOMED/RxNorm subsumption), from/to (ISO date window).",
    process: "Filters events of the chosen category by the date window and by code-or-display match; when an exact code is given on a hierarchical category, expands it to descendants so children match the parent; tallies matched patients and their event counts.",
    output: "nPatients, nEvents, eventsByPatient, expandedCodes, and escalation rates for matched vs rest — the whole-cohort filter engine.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "serious",
    points: [
      "Subsumption pulls in descendants silently — a SNOMED ancestor can match far more patients than the display string suggests (check expandedCodes).",
      "escRateHit/escRateRest compare groups on a modeled outcome — lifts restate the generator when the filter touches burden.",
      "Substring q on display invites loose matches; empty q and empty code returns the whole category.",
    ],
    serious: [
      "Modeled-outcome lifts plus invisible subsumption widen matched sets beyond analyst intent.",
    ],
  },
};
Index case comparison

The same one-patient-versus-cohort percentile readout, computed from the patient's events: burden, medications, stay length, event volume and follow-up span.

 {
  id: "index-case",
  name: "Index case comparison",
  category: "Overview",
  description:
    "One patient's whole trajectory — age, burden, medication count, LOS, event volume, follow-up span — read against the cohort benchmark in percentiles, from the event stream.",
  requiredCategories: [],
  run(events, params) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const pid = params?.patientId || views[0]?.patientId;
    const v = views.find((x) => x.patientId === pid);
    if (!v) return { error: `patient '${pid ?? ""}' not in stream`, patientId: pid ?? null };
    const scope = params?.scope === "category" ? "category" : "cohort";
    const benchmark = scope === "category"
      ? views.filter((x) => x.category === v.category)
      : views;
    const comN = (x: typeof v) => x.events.filter((e) => e.category === "comorbidity").length;
    const medN = (x: typeof v) => x.events.filter((e) => e.category === "medication").length;
    const labN = (x: typeof v) => x.events.filter((e) => e.category === "lab").length;
    const spanDays = (x: typeof v) => {
      const s = sortEvents(x.events);
      return s.length ? daysBetween(s[0].t, s[s.length - 1].t) : 0;
    };
    const rows = [
      { metric: "Age", value: v.age ?? null, median: median(benchmark.map((x) => x.age ?? NaN)), percentile: percentileRank(benchmark.map((x) => x.age ?? NaN), v.age ?? NaN) },
      { metric: "Comorbidity concepts", value: comN(v), median: median(benchmark.map(comN)), percentile: percentileRank(benchmark.map(comN), comN(v)) },
      { metric: "Medication events", value: medN(v), median: median(benchmark.map(medN)), percentile: percentileRank(benchmark.map(medN), medN(v)) },
      { metric: "Length of stay (days)", value: v.losDays ?? null, median: median(benchmark.map((x) => x.losDays ?? NaN)), percentile: percentileRank(benchmark.map((x) => x.losDays ?? NaN), v.losDays ?? NaN) },
      { metric: "Total events", value: v.events.length, median: median(benchmark.map((x) => x.events.length)), percentile: percentileRank(benchmark.map((x) => x.events.length), v.events.length) },
      { metric: "Lab events", value: labN(v), median: median(benchmark.map(labN)), percentile: percentileRank(benchmark.map(labN), labN(v)) },
      { metric: "Follow-up span (days)", value: spanDays(v), median: median(benchmark.map(spanDays)), percentile: percentileRank(benchmark.map(spanDays), spanDays(v)) },
    ];
    return { patientId: v.patientId, scope, n: benchmark.length, rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; params patientId (default = first patient), scope ('cohort' or 'category').",
    process: "Builds the patient's event view, then compares each metric — age, comorbidity concepts, medication events, LOS, total events, labs, follow-up span — to the benchmark pool's median and percentile rank.",
    output: "patientId, scope, n, rows {metric, value, median, percentile} — where this patient sits in the cohort distribution.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "LOS rides the modeled losDays attribute; percentiles inherit that model.",
      "Event counts measure documentation intensity, not sickness.",
      "Default patient is the first in the stream — arbitrary unless patientId is set.",
    ],
  },
};
Hub network

Maps which conditions connect to which categories, then ranks conditions by how many different categories they appear in — the 'hub' conditions.

 {
  id: "hub-network",
  name: "Hub network",
  category: "Clinical / diagnostic",
  description:
    "Category ↔ comorbidity edges occurring in at least the minimum number of patients, with each comorbidity's hub degree — how many categories it connects to.",
  requiredCategories: ["comorbidity", "encounter"],
  run(events, params) {
    const minEdge = Math.max(1, Number(params?.minEdge ?? 8));
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const comSets = conceptSets(events, "comorbidity");
    const labels = new Map<string, string>();
    for (const e of events) {
      if (e.category === "comorbidity" && !labels.has(keyOf(e))) labels.set(keyOf(e), e.display || keyOf(e));
    }
    const comKeys = [...labels.keys()].sort();
    const edges: Array<{ label: string; n: number; degree: number }> = [];
    for (const [cat, g] of groupBy(views, (v) => v.category ?? "(unknown)")) {
      for (const comKey of comKeys) {
        const n = g.filter((v) => comSets.get(v.patientId)?.has(comKey)).length;
        if (n >= minEdge) {
          const label = `${labels.get(comKey)} → ${cat}`;
          const existing = edges.find((e) => e.label === label);
          if (existing) existing.n += n;
          else edges.push({ label, n, degree: 0 });
        }
      }
    }
    // hub degree: how many distinct categories each comorbidity connects to
    const degree = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const e of edges) {
      const com = e.label.split(" → ")[0];
      degree.set(com, (degree.get(com) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
    const rows = edges
      .map((e) => ({ label: e.label, n: e.n, degree: degree.get(e.label.split(" → ")[0]) ?? 0 }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.degree - a.degree || b.n - a.n);
    return { rows, nEdges: rows.length };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream: comorbidity events and encounter attributes (category); param minEdge (default 8).",
    process: "For every category × comorbidity edge occurring in at least minEdge patients, keeps the edge and increments the comorbidity's hub degree — how many categories it connects to; ranked by degree then volume.",
    output: "rows {label ('comorbidity → category'), n, degree}, plus nEdges — high-degree comorbidities are the cohort's connective tissue.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "ok",
    points: [
      "minEdge is a volume floor, not a test — hub degree scales with cohort and category size, not clinical connectivity.",
      "Edges share patients — degrees are not independent.",
    ],
  },
};
Period drift (early vs late)

Compares the early and late halves of the calendar window on the headline metrics — real drift over time rather than two data arms.

 {
  id: "drift",
  name: "Period drift (early vs late)",
  category: "Public health / population",
  description:
    "Compares the early and late halves of the observation window on headline metrics — patient counts, escalation, age, burden, LOS — and lists the largest per-category escalation gaps: drift over real calendar time.",
  requiredCategories: ["encounter", "outcome"],
  run(events) {
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const allT = events.map((e) => e.t).filter(Boolean).sort();
    if (allT.length < 2) return { ready: false, metrics: [], catRows: [] };
    const mid = allT[Math.floor(allT.length / 2)];
    const splitOf = (v: (typeof views)[number]) => (v.events.find((e) => e.category === "encounter")?.t ?? "") < mid ? "A" : "B";
    const A = views.filter((v) => splitOf(v) === "A");
    const B = views.filter((v) => splitOf(v) === "B");
    const metrics = [
      { label: "Patients", a: A.length, b: B.length },
      { label: "Escalation rate", a: rate(A, (v) => v.escalated === true), b: rate(B, (v) => v.escalated === true) },
      { label: "Avg age", a: mean(A, (v) => v.age), b: mean(B, (v) => v.age) },
      { label: "Avg comorbidity concepts", a: mean(A, (v) => v.events.filter((e) => e.category === "comorbidity").length), b: mean(B, (v) => v.events.filter((e) => e.category === "comorbidity").length) },
      { label: "Avg LOS (days)", a: mean(A, (v) => v.losDays), b: mean(B, (v) => v.losDays) },
    ];
    const catRows = [...groupBy(views, (v) => v.category ?? "(unknown)").entries()]
      .map(([cat, g]) => {
        const a = g.filter((v) => splitOf(v) === "A");
        const b = g.filter((v) => splitOf(v) === "B");
        const ea = rate(a, (v) => v.escalated === true);
        const eb = rate(b, (v) => v.escalated === true);
        return { cat, na: a.length, nb: b.length, ea, eb, diff: Math.abs(ea - eb) };
      })
      .sort((x, y) => y.diff - x.diff);
    return { splitAt: mid, metrics, catRows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream; patients split at the median event date of the whole stream.",
    process: "Splits patients into the early half (admission before the stream's median event date) and late half, compares headline metrics side by side, then ranks per-category escalation gaps by absolute difference.",
    output: "splitAt, metrics {label, a, b}, catRows {cat, na, nb, ea, eb, diff} — whether the early and late stream tell the same story.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Patients near the split date land in one arm by a single day — boundary sensitivity is unmeasured.",
      "Escalation rates are modeled outcomes; a period 'gap' can be a planted difference, not drift.",
      "Per-category gaps rank on absolute difference with no n or CI — small-category noise tops the list.",
    ],
  },
};
Diagnostic ambiguity

Finds pairs of symptoms that appear across many different condition categories — the more categories share a symptom pair, the less that pair helps pin down a diagnosis.

 {
  id: "diagnostic-ambiguity",
  name: "Diagnostic ambiguity",
  category: "Patient / evidence",
  description:
    "Symptom pairs that occur across multiple diagnostic categories — the more categories share a symptom pair, the more ambiguous that presentation is as a diagnostic signal.",
  requiredCategories: ["symptom", "encounter"],
  run(events, params) {
    const minTotal = Math.max(1, Number(params?.minTotal ?? 6));
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const symSets = conceptSets(events, "symptom");
    const setMap = new Map<string, Map<string, number>>();
    for (const v of views) {
      const syms = [...(symSets.get(v.patientId) ?? new Set<string>())].sort();
      if (syms.length < 2) continue;
      for (let i = 0; i < syms.length; i++) {
        for (let j = i + 1; j < syms.length; j++) {
          const key = `${syms[i]} + ${syms[j]}`;
          if (!setMap.has(key)) setMap.set(key, new Map());
          const catMap = setMap.get(key)!;
          const cat = v.category ?? "(unknown)";
          catMap.set(cat, (catMap.get(cat) ?? 0) + 1);
        }
      }
    }
    const rows: Array<{ key: string; n: number; nConds: number; topCat: string; topN: number; dist: Array<{ cat: string; n: number }> }> = [];
    setMap.forEach((catMap, key) => {
      const total = [...catMap.values()].reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
      if (total < minTotal || catMap.size < 2) return;
      const dist = [...catMap.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).map(([cat, n]) => ({ cat, n }));
      rows.push({ key, n: total, nConds: catMap.size, topCat: dist[0].cat, topN: dist[0].n, dist });
    });
    return { rows: topN(rows.sort((a, b) => b.nConds - a.nConds || b.n - a.n), 8) };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream: symptom events and encounter attributes (category); param minTotal (default 6).",
    process: "Builds every symptom-concept pair within multi-symptom patients, counts how many diagnostic categories each pair appears in, keeps pairs seen at least minTotal times across 2+ categories, and ranks by category count.",
    output: "rows: top 8 with {key, n, nConds, topCat, topN, dist} — the more categories share a pair, the more ambiguous that presentation is.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "ok",
    points: [
      "Pure pair counting, no outcome claim — but nConds tracks symptom prevalence as much as ambiguity.",
      "minTotal and the top-8 cut are arbitrary; a common pair can dominate without being clinically confusable.",
    ],
  },
};
Symptom proximity

For each condition category, finds the symptom that shows up much more often there than in the cohort overall — the symptom that 'belongs' to that category.

 {
  id: "symptom-proximity",
  name: "Symptom proximity",
  category: "Clinical / diagnostic",
  description:
    "For each category, the symptom most over-represented relative to the whole cohort (enrichment = in-category rate ÷ cohort-wide rate), with a minimum cell size.",
  requiredCategories: ["symptom", "encounter"],
  run(events, params) {
    const minN = Math.max(1, Number(params?.minN ?? 4));
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const symSets = conceptSets(events, "symptom");
    const displayOf = new Map<string, string>();
    for (const e of events) {
      if (e.category === "symptom" && !displayOf.has(keyOf(e))) displayOf.set(keyOf(e), e.display || keyOf(e));
    }
    const allCounts = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const [, s] of symSets) for (const k of s) allCounts.set(k, (allCounts.get(k) ?? 0) + 1);
    const total = views.length;
    const rows: Array<{ cat: string; sym: string; pctCat: number; pctAll: number; enrich: number }> = [];
    for (const [cat, g] of groupBy(views, (v) => v.category ?? "(unknown)")) {
      let best: (typeof rows)[number] | null = null;
      const counts = new Map<string, number>();
      for (const v of g) for (const k of symSets.get(v.patientId) ?? new Set<string>()) counts.set(k, (counts.get(k) ?? 0) + 1);
      counts.forEach((n, key) => {
        if (n < minN) return;
        const pctCat = n / g.length;
        const pctAll = total ? (allCounts.get(key) ?? 0) / total : 0;
        const enrich = pctAll > 0 ? pctCat / pctAll : 0;
        if (!best || enrich > best.enrich) best = { cat, sym: displayOf.get(key) ?? key, pctCat, pctAll, enrich };
      });
      if (best) rows.push(best);
    }
    return { rows: rows.sort((a, b) => b.enrich - a.enrich) };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's event stream: symptom events and encounter attributes (category); param minN (default 4).",
    process: "For each category, finds the symptom with at least minN in-category patients whose in-category rate ÷ cohort-wide rate (enrichment) is highest.",
    output: "rows: {cat, sym, pctCat, pctAll, enrich} — enrichment > 1 means the symptom clusters around that category more than baseline.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "ok",
    points: [
      "Enrichment with minN=4: a four-patient symptom can top a category — enrichment >1 is often sampling noise.",
      "Best-of-scan per category with no multiplicity correction; still descriptive-only (no outcome claim).",
    ],
  },
};
Medication sequences

Finds 'A then B' medication patterns: for each ordered pair of drugs started within a set window, it counts how often the sequence happens and how those patients fare.

 {
  id: "med-sequence",
  name: "Medication sequences",
  category: "Longitudinal",
  description:
    "Ordered medication pairs observed within a sliding window per patient. For each pair (A → B) seen at least 6 times, reports direction-aware frequency and the escalation rate of patients carrying that sequence.",
  requiredCategories: ["medication", "outcome"],
  run(events, params) {
    const windowDays = Math.max(1, Number(params?.windowDays ?? 180));
    const minN = 6;
    const obsEnd = obsEndByPatient(events);
    const windows = medWindows(events, obsEnd);

    // Group windows by patient, sorted by start.
    const byPatient = new Map<string, MedWindow[]>();
    for (const w of windows) {
      const list = byPatient.get(w.patientId) ?? [];
      list.push(w);
      byPatient.set(w.patientId, list);
    }
    for (const list of byPatient.values()) {
      list.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
    }

    // Outcome flags per patient.
    const escalated = new Set<string>();
    for (const e of events) {
      if (e.category === "outcome" && e.code === "escalation") escalated.add(e.patientId);
    }

    const seqMap = new Map<string, { a: string; b: string; n: number; esc: number }>();
    const windowMs = windowDays * DAY;
    for (const [pid, list] of byPatient) {
      // For each A in the list, look for B that started after A.start
      // and within the window. We restrict to "new" courses (B.start > A.start).
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
        const a = list[i];
        for (let j = 0; j < list.length; j++) {
          if (j === i) continue;
          const b = list[j];
          if (b.start <= a.start) continue;
          if (b.start - a.start > windowMs) continue;
          // Direction: A → B (a is the earlier med).
          const key = `${a.code} → ${b.code}`;
          let rec = seqMap.get(key);
          if (!rec) {
            rec = { a: a.code, b: b.code, n: 0, esc: 0 };
            seqMap.set(key, rec);
          }
          rec.n++;
          if (escalated.has(pid)) rec.esc++;
        }
      }
    }

    const rows = [...seqMap.values()]
      .filter((r) => r.n >= minN)
      .sort((x, y) => y.n - x.n)
      .slice(0, 20)
      .map((r) => ({
        a: r.a,
        b: r.b,
        n: r.n,
        escRate: r.esc / r.n,
      }));

    return {
      windowDays,
      minN,
      nPairs: rows.length,
      rows,
    };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's medication event stream with start/end dates, plus outcome events to compute escRate.",
    process: "Groups medication windows by patient, sorts by start, and for each pair (A, B) where B.start > A.start and within windowDays, counts the sequence. Reports top 20 sequences with n ≥ 6.",
    output: "rows: {a, b, n, escRate} — the most observed ordered medication transitions per patient.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Both directions of a pair (A→B and B→A) are reported separately; a clinician must read direction, not just label.",
      "Sequences with frequent switching will be over-represented relative to sustained regimens.",
      "Window of 180 days is a default; the temporal scale of 'a sequence' is editor-configurable.",
    ],
  },
};
Medication changes

For each drug, counts how many courses were started, how many stopped, and how many are still ongoing — read directly from each course's start and end dates.

 {
  id: "med-changes",
  name: "Medication changes",
  category: "Longitudinal",
  description:
    "Per medication concept: counts of started, stopped, and ongoing courses over the observation window. Uses each course's start/end/status to classify, with no double-counting of refills.",
  requiredCategories: ["medication"],
  run(events, params) {
    const minN = Math.max(1, Number(params?.minN ?? 5));
    const obsEnd = obsEndByPatient(events);
    const windows = medWindows(events, obsEnd);

    const byCode = new Map<string, { code: string; display: string; started: number; stopped: number; ongoing: number; total: number }>();
    for (const w of windows) {
      let rec = byCode.get(w.code);
      if (!rec) {
        rec = { code: w.code, display: w.display, started: 0, stopped: 0, ongoing: 0, total: 0 };
        byCode.set(w.code, rec);
      }
      rec.total++;
      if (w.ongoing) {
        rec.ongoing++;
      } else if (w.end > w.start) {
        // Has a real end date → counts as a completed course (started + stopped).
        rec.started++;
        rec.stopped++;
      } else {
        // Point-in-time event with no end: counts as a start only.
        rec.started++;
      }
    }

    const rows = [...byCode.values()]
      .filter((r) => r.total >= minN)
      .sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total)
      .slice(0, 30)
      .map((r) => ({
        code: r.code,
        display: r.display,
        started: r.started,
        stopped: r.stopped,
        ongoing: r.ongoing,
        total: r.total,
      }));

    return { minN, n: rows.length, rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Each case's medication events with start/end/status.",
    process: "Per concept, sums started/stopped/ongoing across all courses in the observation window. A course with both start and end dates counts as 1 started + 1 stopped; an ongoing course (no end) counts as 1 ongoing.",
    output: "rows: {code, display, started, stopped, ongoing, total} — top 30 by total course count.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "ok",
    points: [
      "Counts reflect whatever start/end the events carry — point-in-time events without end are counted as 'started' only.",
      "Refills (same code, multiple courses per patient) are counted per course, not per patient — burst regimens overstate totals.",
    ],
  },
};
Medication → outcome (lagged)

For each drug, compares the outcome rate in the days AFTER a course started with the same-length window BEFORE it — did the drug's start precede a change in outcomes?

 {
  id: "med-outcome-lagged",
  name: "Medication → outcome (lagged)",
  category: "Longitudinal",
  description:
    "For each medication concept, the rate of escalation (or readmission) within a window after the medication started, compared to a baseline of equal length pre-medication. Reports the lift of the post-med rate over the pre-med rate.",
  requiredCategories: ["medication", "outcome"],
  run(events, params) {
    const lagDays = Math.max(7, Number(params?.lagDays ?? 90));
    const minN = Math.max(5, Number(params?.minN ?? 10));
    const outcomeKind = (params?.outcome ?? "escalation") as "escalation" | "readmission-90d";

    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const outcomeByPatient = new Map<string, number[]>();
    for (const v of views) {
      const ts: number[] = [];
      for (const e of v.events) {
        if (e.category === "outcome" && e.code === outcomeKind) {
          const t = Date.parse(e.t);
          if (Number.isFinite(t)) ts.push(t);
        }
      }
      if (ts.length) outcomeByPatient.set(v.patientId, ts);
    }

    const obsEnd = obsEndByPatient(events);
    const windows = medWindows(events, obsEnd);

    const lagMs = lagDays * DAY;
    const byCode = new Map<string, { code: string; display: string; nExposed: number; nOutcomePost: number; nOutcomePre: number }>();
    for (const w of windows) {
      let rec = byCode.get(w.code);
      if (!rec) {
        rec = { code: w.code, display: w.display, nExposed: 0, nOutcomePost: 0, nOutcomePre: 0 };
        byCode.set(w.code, rec);
      }
      rec.nExposed++;
      const outcomes = outcomeByPatient.get(w.patientId);
      if (!outcomes) continue;
      const post = outcomes.filter((t) => t >= w.start && t <= w.start + lagMs);
      const pre = outcomes.filter((t) => t >= w.start - lagMs && t < w.start);
      if (post.length > 0) rec.nOutcomePost++;
      if (pre.length > 0) rec.nOutcomePre++;
    }

    const rows = [...byCode.values()]
      .filter((r) => r.nExposed >= minN)
      .map((r) => {
        const postRate = r.nOutcomePost / r.nExposed;
        const preRate = r.nOutcomePre / r.nExposed;
        const lift = preRate > 0 ? postRate / preRate : postRate > 0 ? 99 : 0;
        return {
          code: r.code,
          display: r.display,
          nExposed: r.nExposed,
          postRate: Math.round(postRate * 1000) / 1000,
          preRate: Math.round(preRate * 1000) / 1000,
          lift: Math.round(lift * 100) / 100,
        };
      })
      .sort((a, b) => b.lift - a.lift)
      .slice(0, 30);

    return { lagDays, outcome: outcomeKind, minN, rows };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's medication events with start dates and outcome events (escalation or readmission-90d).",
    process: "For each medication concept, per course: counts whether an outcome event falls within [start, start + lagDays] (post) and within [start - lagDays, start) (pre). Per-code totals: nExposed, nOutcomePost, nOutcomePre, lift = postRate / preRate.",
    output: "rows: {code, display, nExposed, postRate, preRate, lift} — top 30 by lift, sorted desc.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "postRate and preRate are both measured relative to exposed patients; an outcome on a patient exposed to multiple meds contributes to each med's row.",
      "No confounding adjustment: a medication that's started when the patient is already sicker will see a higher post rate, not because the med caused the outcome.",
      "Lag of 90 days is a default; the temporal scale of 'medication effect' is editor-configurable.",
    ],
  },
};
Unexpected medication patterns

Finds drug pairs that show up together more (or less) often than pure chance would predict — flagging synergistic combinations or substitutes.

 {
  id: "med-pattern-lift",
  name: "Unexpected medication patterns",
  category: "Medication",
  description:
    "Identifies medication pairs that co-occur together more or less often than expected under independence — revealing synergistic combinations, antagonistic substitutions, or formulary-driven prescribing patterns.",
  requiredCategories: ["medication"],
  run(events, params) {
    const minEpisodes = Math.max(4, Number(params?.minEpisodes ?? 20));
    const minLift = Number(params?.minLift ?? 1.5);
    const maxLift = Number(params?.maxLift ?? 0.5); // pairs below this are unexpectedly absent
    const LIFT_CAP = 99;

    // Group into episodes: distinct patientId + encounter-start-day.
    // Each episode has the set of medication codes present.
    const episodeMedSets = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
    for (const e of events) {
      if (e.category !== "medication") continue;
      const enc = (e as PatientEvent & { encounterDate?: string }).encounterDate;
      const key = enc
        ? `${e.patientId}__${enc}`
        : `${e.patientId}__${e.t.substring(0, 10)}`;
      const set = episodeMedSets.get(key) ?? new Set<string>();
      set.add(e.code || e.display.toLowerCase());
      episodeMedSets.set(key, set);
    }

    const episodeList = [...episodeMedSets.values()];
    const totalEpisodes = episodeList.length;
    if (totalEpisodes < minEpisodes) {
      return {
        totalEpisodes,
        minEpisodes,
        nPairs: 0,
        rows: [],
        message: `Only ${totalEpisodes} episodes — need at least ${minEpisodes} to compute reliable lift.`,
      };
    }

    // Per-med episode counts.
    const medCounts = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const set of episodeList) {
      for (const m of set) {
        medCounts.set(m, (medCounts.get(m) ?? 0) + 1);
      }
    }
    const meds = [...medCounts.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);

    // Lift for each pair.
    const byCode = conceptIndex(events, "medication");
    type Row = {
      medA: string; medADisplay: string;
      medB: string; medBDisplay: string;
      nA: number; nB: number; nBoth: number;
      expected: number; lift: number;
      flag: "high" | "low";
    };
    const rows: Row[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < meds.length; i++) {
      const [medA, nA] = meds[i];
      if (nA < 2) continue;
      const infoA = byCode.get(medA);
      const displayA = infoA?.display ?? medA;

      for (let j = i + 1; j < meds.length; j++) {
        const [medB, nB] = meds[j];
        if (nB < 2) continue;

        let nBoth = 0;
        for (const set of episodeList) {
          if (set.has(medA) && set.has(medB)) nBoth++;
        }

        const expected = (nA / totalEpisodes) * (nB / totalEpisodes) * totalEpisodes;
        if (expected < 1) continue;

        const lift = nBoth / expected;
        const capped = Math.min(lift, LIFT_CAP);
        const isHigh = capped > minLift;
        const isLow = capped < maxLift;
        if (!isHigh && !isLow) continue;

        const infoB = byCode.get(medB);
        const displayB = infoB?.display ?? medB;

        rows.push({
          medA, medADisplay: displayA,
          medB, medBDisplay: displayB,
          nA, nB, nBoth,
          expected: Math.round(expected * 100) / 100,
          lift: Math.round(capped * 100) / 100,
          flag: isHigh ? "high" : "low",
        });
      }
    }

    rows.sort((a, b) => (b.lift - 1) - (a.lift - 1));

    return {
      totalEpisodes,
      minEpisodes,
      nPairs: rows.length,
      rows: rows.slice(0, 50),
      flags: {
        high: rows.filter((r) => r.flag === "high").length,
        low: rows.filter((r) => r.flag === "low").length,
      },
    };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "Medication events across the cohort — each with a code/display. Episodes are patient + encounter-start-day buckets.",
    process: "For each pair of medication concepts: counts episodes containing both (observed), computes expected under independence = P(A)×P(B)×N, reports lift = observed/expected. Flags pairs with lift > minLift (unexpectedly frequent) or lift < maxLift (unexpectedly absent).",
    output: "rows: {medA, medADisplay, medB, medBDisplay, nA, nB, nBoth, expected, lift, flag} — top 50 by lift deviation, with 'high'/'low' flags.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Episodes are patient+encounter-day buckets; medications prescribed in separate encounters of the same patient appear in separate episodes.",
      "No confounder control: formulary constraints (e.g., two drugs on the same guideline protocol) can produce high-lift pairs that are not clinically synergistic.",
      "Low-lift pairs may reflect active avoidance (contraindication) or patient intolerance not captured in the data.",
    ],
  },
};
Signal detection (temporal)

Seven automated time-based scans: sudden changes in drug use, seasonal spikes, emerging drug combinations, outcome clusters, unusual co-occurrence, event-count outliers and demographic drift.

 {
  id: "signal-detection-temporal",
  name: "Signal detection (temporal)",
  category: "Surveillance",
  description:
    "Scans the cohort's longitudinal event stream for seven classes of temporal signals: trend break in concept frequency, seasonal clustering, medication combination emergence, outcome clustering, co-occurrence lift anomalies, demographic drift, and episode-frequency outliers.",
  run(events, params) {
    const signals: Signal[] = [];
    const total = events.length;

    // ── 1. Trend break in medication frequency ────────────────────────────────
    // Compare the last quarter vs the first quarter; flag concepts with >2× change.
    const medsByMonth = byMonth(events.filter((e) => e.category === "medication"));
    const monthKeys = [...medsByMonth.keys()].sort();
    if (monthKeys.length >= 4) {
      const mid = Math.floor(monthKeys.length / 2);
      const firstHalf = monthKeys.slice(0, mid);
      const secondHalf = monthKeys.slice(mid);

      const medCounts = conceptIndex(events, "medication");
      for (const [code, info] of medCounts) {
        const first = firstHalf.reduce((s, k) => s + (medsByMonth.get(k)?.filter((e) => (e.code || e.display.toLowerCase()) === code).length ?? 0), 0);
        const second = secondHalf.reduce((s, k) => s + (medsByMonth.get(k)?.filter((e) => (e.code || e.display.toLowerCase()) === code).length ?? 0), 0);
        const ratio = first > 0 ? second / first : second > 0 ? 99 : 1;
        if ((ratio > 2.5 || ratio < 0.4) && first + second >= 10) {
          signals.push({
            type: "medication-trend-break",
            severity: ratio > 4 || ratio < 0.2 ? "high" : "medium",
            description: `Medication "${info.display}" frequency ${second > first ? "↑" : "↓"} ${Math.round(ratio * 10) / 10}× from early to late period (${first} → ${second} events)`,
            n: first + second,
            pct: Math.round(((first + second) / total) * 1000) / 10,
            expected: Math.round(total / medCounts.size),
            detail: `ratio=${Math.round(ratio * 100) / 100}`,
          });
        }
      }
    }

    // ── 2. Seasonal clustering ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Flag months that are >2σ above the monthly mean across all event types.
    const monthCounts = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const e of events) {
      const key = e.t.substring(0, 7);
      monthCounts.set(key, (monthCounts.get(key) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
    const counts = [...monthCounts.values()];
    if (counts.length >= 4) {
      const mean = counts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / counts.length;
      const s = sdOf(counts);
      if (s > 0) {
        const threshold = mean + 1.5 * s;
        for (const [key, n] of monthCounts) {
          if (n > threshold) {
            const pctOfTotal = Math.round((n / events.length) * 1000) / 10;
            signals.push({
              type: "seasonal-clustering",
              severity: n > threshold * 1.5 ? "high" : "medium",
              description: `Month ${key} has ${n} events — ${Math.round((n / mean) * 10) / 10}× above monthly mean (${Math.round(mean)})`,
              n,
              pct: pctOfTotal,
              expected: Math.round(mean),
              detail: key,
            });
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // ── 3. Medication combination emergence ───────────────────────────────────
    // Flag med codes whose co-occurrence with another med has grown sharply.
    // Build episode-level co-occurrence map from the whole stream.
    const views = indexStreams(events);
    const episodeMeds = new Map<string, Set<string>>(); // episodeKey → med codes
    for (const v of views) {
      const enc = (v.events[0] as PatientEvent & { encounterDate?: string }).encounterDate ?? v.events[0]?.t.substring(0, 10);
      const key = `${v.patientId}__${enc}`;
      const meds = new Set<string>();
      for (const e of v.events) {
        if (e.category === "medication") meds.add(e.code || e.display.toLowerCase());
      }
      episodeMeds.set(key, meds);
    }
    const medCodes = [...conceptIndex(events, "medication").keys()];
    const pairEmergence = new Map<string, { medA: string; medB: string; early: number; late: number; ratio: number }>();
    const mid = Math.floor(monthKeys.length / 2);
    const earlyMonths = new Set(monthKeys.slice(0, mid));
    const lateMonths = new Set(monthKeys.slice(mid));
    const earlyEps = [...episodeMeds.entries()].filter(([k]) => earlyMonths.has(k.split("__")[1]?.substring(0, 7)));
    const lateEps = [...episodeMeds.entries()].filter(([k]) => lateMonths.has(k.split("__")[1]?.substring(0, 7)));
    for (let i = 0; i < medCodes.length; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < medCodes.length; j++) {
        const a = medCodes[i], b = medCodes[j];
        const early = earlyEps.filter(([, s]) => s.has(a) && s.has(b)).length;
        const late = lateEps.filter(([, s]) => s.has(a) && s.has(b)).length;
        const ratio = early > 0 ? late / early : late > 0 ? 99 : 1;
        if ((ratio > 3 || ratio < 0.3) && early + late >= 8) {
          pairEmergence.set(`${a}|${b}`, { medA: a, medB: b, early, late, ratio });
        }
      }
    }
    for (const rec of [...pairEmergence.values()].sort((a, b) => b.ratio - a.ratio).slice(0, 10)) {
      const isHigh = rec.ratio > 1;
      signals.push({
        type: "med-combination-emergence",
        severity: rec.ratio > 5 || rec.ratio < 0.15 ? "high" : "medium",
        description: `Med pair "${rec.medA}" + "${rec.medB}" co-occurrence ${isHigh ? "emerged" : "dropped"}: ${rec.early} → ${rec.late} episodes (${Math.round(rec.ratio * 10) / 10}×)`,
        n: rec.early + rec.late,
        pct: Math.round(((rec.early + rec.late) / episodeMeds.size) * 1000) / 10,
        expected: Math.round(episodeMeds.size / (medCodes.length * medCodes.length)),
        detail: `ratio=${Math.round(rec.ratio * 100) / 100}`,
      });
    }

    // ── 4. Outcome clustering ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Flag months where escalation rate is >2× the overall rate.
    const outcomeByMonth = byMonth(events.filter((e) => e.category === "outcome"));
    const overallEscRate = rate(views, (v) => !!v.escalated);
    for (const [month, evts] of outcomeByMonth) {
      const escEvts = evts.filter((e) => e.code === "escalation").length;
      const monthEscRate = escEvts / evts.length;
      if (evts.length >= 5 && monthEscRate > overallEscRate * 2) {
        signals.push({
          type: "outcome-clustering",
          severity: monthEscRate > overallEscRate * 3 ? "high" : "medium",
          description: `Month ${month}: ${Math.round(monthEscRate * 100)}% escalation rate vs ${Math.round(overallEscRate * 100)}% overall`,
          n: evts.length,
          pct: Math.round(monthEscRate * 1000) / 10,
          expected: Math.round(overallEscRate * 1000) / 10,
          detail: month,
        });
      }
    }

    // ── 5. Co-occurrence lift anomaly ────────────────────────────────────────
    // Flag med+outcome pairs that co-occur at >3× expected.
    const medOutcomePairs = new Map<string, { med: string; outcome: string; observed: number; expected: number; lift: number }>();
    const medsIdx = conceptIndex(events, "medication");
    const outsIdx = conceptIndex(events, "outcome");
    const outcomeCodes = [...outsIdx.keys()];
    for (const [med, medInfo] of medsIdx) {
      for (const outcome of outcomeCodes) {
        const outCount = outsIdx.get(outcome)?.n ?? 0;
        const expected = (medInfo.n / events.length) * outCount;
        if (expected < 1) continue;
        let observed = 0;
        for (const v of views) {
          const hasMed = v.events.some((e) => e.category === "medication" && (e.code || e.display.toLowerCase()) === med);
          const hasOutcome = v.events.some((e) => e.category === "outcome" && e.code === outcome);
          if (hasMed && hasOutcome) observed++;
        }
        const lift = observed / expected;
        if (lift > 3 && observed >= 5) {
          medOutcomePairs.set(`${med}|${outcome}`, { med, outcome, observed, expected: Math.round(expected * 10) / 10, lift });
        }
      }
    }
    for (const rec of [...medOutcomePairs.values()].sort((a, b) => b.lift - a.lift).slice(0, 8)) {
      signals.push({
        type: "cooccurrence-lift-anomaly",
        severity: rec.lift > 5 ? "high" : "medium",
        description: `Med "${rec.med}" + outcome "${rec.outcome}" co-occur in ${rec.observed} patients (lift=${Math.round(rec.lift * 10) / 10}× vs independence)`,
        n: rec.observed,
        pct: Math.round((rec.observed / views.length) * 1000) / 10,
        expected: rec.expected,
        detail: `lift=${Math.round(rec.lift * 100) / 100}`,
      });
    }

    // ── 6. Episode-frequency outlier patients ─────────────────────────────────
    // Flag patients whose event count is >2σ above mean — potential high-utilizers.
    const patientEventCounts = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const e of events) {
      patientEventCounts.set(e.patientId, (patientEventCounts.get(e.patientId) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
    const counts2 = [...patientEventCounts.values()];
    if (counts2.length >= 10) {
      const mean2 = counts2.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / counts2.length;
      const s2 = sdOf(counts2);
      if (s2 > 0) {
        const threshold2 = mean2 + 2 * s2;
        const outlierPatients = [...patientEventCounts.entries()].filter(([, n]) => n > threshold2);
        if (outlierPatients.length > 3) {
          signals.push({
            type: "episode-frequency-outlier",
            severity: outlierPatients.length > patientEventCounts.size * 0.1 ? "high" : "medium",
            description: `${outlierPatients.length} patients with >${Math.round(threshold2)} events (mean=${Math.round(mean2)}, σ=${Math.round(s2)}) — high-utilizers`,
            n: outlierPatients.length,
            pct: Math.round((outlierPatients.length / patientEventCounts.size) * 1000) / 10,
            expected: Math.round(patientEventCounts.size * 0.0228),
          });
        }
      }
    }

    // ── 7. Demographic drift ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Compare sex/insurance distribution between first and second half of the stream.
    function demoDrift(events: PatientEvent[], attr: "sex" | "insurance"): Signal[] {
      const results: Signal[] = [];
      const half = Math.floor(events.length / 2);
      const early = events.slice(0, half);
      const late = events.slice(half);

      const earlyDemo = new Map<string, number>();
      const lateDemo = new Map<string, number>();
      for (const e of early) {
        const v = e.attrs?.[attr];
        if (v) earlyDemo.set(String(v), (earlyDemo.get(String(v)) ?? 0) + 1);
      }
      for (const e of late) {
        const v = e.attrs?.[attr];
        if (v) lateDemo.set(String(v), (lateDemo.get(String(v)) ?? 0) + 1);
      }

      const allKeys = new Set([...earlyDemo.keys(), ...lateDemo.keys()]);
      for (const key of allKeys) {
        const e1 = earlyDemo.get(key) ?? 0;
        const l1 = lateDemo.get(key) ?? 0;
        const earlyRate = e1 / half;
        const lateRate = l1 / (events.length - half);
        const ratio = earlyRate > 0 ? lateRate / earlyRate : lateRate > 0 ? 99 : 1;
        if ((ratio > 1.8 || ratio < 0.55) && e1 + l1 >= 20) {
          results.push({
            type: "demographic-drift",
            severity: ratio > 2.5 || ratio < 0.4 ? "high" : "medium",
            description: `${attr}="${key}" shifted ${lateRate > earlyRate ? "↑" : "↓"} ${Math.round(ratio * 10) / 10}×: ${Math.round(earlyRate * 100)}% → ${Math.round(lateRate * 100)}% of events`,
            n: e1 + l1,
            pct: Math.round((l1 / (events.length - half)) * 1000) / 10,
            expected: Math.round((events.length - half) / allKeys.size),
            detail: `${attr}=${key}`,
          });
        }
      }
      return results;
    }

    signals.push(...demoDrift(events, "sex"));
    signals.push(...demoDrift(events, "insurance"));

    // Sort by severity then n.
    const sev: Record<string, number> = { high: 0, medium: 1, low: 2 };
    signals.sort((a, b) => sev[a.severity] - sev[b.severity] || b.n - a.n);

    return {
      nSignals: signals.length,
      bySeverity: {
        high: signals.filter((s) => s.severity === "high").length,
        medium: signals.filter((s) => s.severity === "medium").length,
        low: signals.filter((s) => s.severity === "low").length,
      },
      signals,
    };
  },
  howTo: {
    input: "The cohort's flat PatientEvent stream.",
    process: "Seven temporal checks: (1) medication trend breaks >2.5× change from early to late period, (2) seasonal clustering (monthly event count >1.5σ above mean), (3) medication combination emergence (pair co-occurrence ratio >3×), (4) outcome clustering (monthly esc rate >2× overall), (5) med+outcome co-occurrence lift >3× independence baseline, (6) patient-level event-count outliers >mean+2σ, (7) demographic drift in sex/insurance distributions.",
    output: "signals: array of {type, severity, description, n, pct, expected, detail} — sorted by severity desc then n desc.",
  },
  audit: {
    verdict: "caution",
    points: [
      "Requires at least 4 months of data for trend/seasonal checks — short streams will surface few signals.",
      "Trend breaks compare halves of the observed window; a genuinely growing trend in early data may look like a break.",
      "No multiple-testing correction across the seven signal classes.",
      "Demographic drift checks event-level proportions, not patient-level composition — a surge in encounters from one group shows as drift even if patient counts are stable.",
    ],
  },
};
Patient Journey Map every patient's trajectory on one swimlane canvas — compare, align, zoom
  • Swimlane trajectories. One lane per data category (comorbidities, diagnoses, symptoms, medications, labs, procedures, outcomes); each patient is a chronological traced line with a dot per event. Dots grow with repeat frequency (×count on hover), and hover shows patient ID, term [code], date and repeats. Click a dot to zoom the axis one level, centred on it.
  • Multi-patient comparison. Pick a patient from the dropdown and “＋ Add to map” to stack several patients — each gets its own color and traced line; color chips list who is plotted and remove with one click. Ideal for comparing diagnostic odysseys side by side.
  • Plot full cohort. One button overlays every patient's trajectory at once (500+ lines tested); lines auto-fade as count grows so the cohort's shape stays readable on top of individual colors.
  • Switchable time axis. “📅 Calendar” plots absolute dates; “⏱ Since day 0” anchors every patient at their own first event and reads minute 1, 2, 3… hour… day… week… month… year… decade — so patients admitted years apart align at day 0. Hover keeps the real date alongside the relative position.
  • Time-zoom ladder. Zoom in/out steps the axis through decade → year → month → week → day → hour → minute (12 buckets per window), with drag-to-pan and a scroll slider; works in both axis modes.
  • Lane visibility. Checkbox per lane; Diagnoses, Medications and Outcomes are on by default so the chart starts uncluttered. Hiding a lane removes its band and its data everywhere.
  • Works on the Suite too. Open the journey map as a Suite output and all controls work in place — buttons re-run the analytic and update that block's result without navigating or duplicating.
Process Mining pathways, timing, clusters, association rules, outliers — v3 event-stream analytics
  • Pathway discovery. Each patient's events become a signalline (ordered, repeats collapsed); frequent contiguous sequences are mined anywhere in the line with distinct-patient support, plus the directly-follows graph with median step gaps — Disco/ProM-style pathway discovery over the cohort's event log.
  • Timing intervals. First-event-to-first-event gaps for ordered pairs (symptom → diagnosis, lab → medication, …): median/IQR/p90 day gaps, cohort coverage, fast-tracked (≤7 d) vs delayed (>90 d) shares.
  • Trajectory clusters. Standardized per-patient feature vectors (event counts per category, pathway length, time-to-diagnosis/medication, span) grouped by deterministic seeded k-means; clusters are labeled by their most distinguishing features with exemplar patient IDs.
  • Pre-diagnosis association rules. Apriori baskets from the 90-day window before the anchor diagnosis; antecedent → consequent rules ranked by lift with support and confidence.
  • Trajectory outliers. Patients flagged on interpretable deviations — rare pathway (support ≤2%), atypical time-to-diagnosis (robust median/MAD z-score), missing steps ≥50% of the cohort takes — ranked by combined score with plain-language reasons.
Cohort Analysis aggregated frequency/timing view of every plotted concept across the whole cohort
  • Frequency-ordered layout (default). Within each lane, nodes are sorted by INCREASING patient count — left = rarest, right = most common. Node size = distinct patients sharing the concept; the most common is drawn largest so the dominant patterns jump out immediately.
  • By timing toggle. Switch to position nodes by typical day offset since day 0, with a quartile bar showing the IQR — useful for reading when a concept typically appears.
  • Zoom in / out. ＋ Zoom in halves the visible window, － Zoom out doubles it, both centred on the current view; the mouse wheel zooms toward the cursor. Use it to focus on a narrow band of rare concepts or to pull back and see the whole distribution.
  • Reset. One button restores the default view that shows every node.
  • Drag-to-pan. Click and drag inside the SVG to slide the view horizontally.
  • Native SVG tooltips on hover. Hover a node to see its label, patient count and share, occurrence count, average repeats per patient, typical day with quartiles, and a rare-outlier flag when support is ≤2%.
  • Empty lists hidden. “Most repeated”, “Rare / possible outliers” and “Widest timing spread” only render when they have qualifying concepts — a cohort with no repeats shows only “Most common (by patients)”.
  • Works on the Suite too. Open cohort analysis as a Suite output and all controls work in place — buttons re-run the analytic and update that block's result without navigating or duplicating.
Patient Data Explorer browse, search and visualize the cohort itself
  • Paginated case table. Every patient in the loaded cohort as a sortable, searchable table (ID, category, age, sex, LOS, 90-day encounters, disposition, admit month, comorbidities). Click a row to expand its full record. Runs in both modes: SQL paging against PostgreSQL in database mode (so it scales to million-patient cohorts), in-memory filter/sort/slice otherwise — the server picks based on what's loaded.
  • Search & filter subset awareness. Searching narrows the table to the matching subset ("12,400 of 1,000,000 cases") with debounced keystrokes; page size is selectable, and the view resets cleanly when the cohort is switched or re-imported.
  • One-click deep-dive analyses. Five explorations render below the table on demand: cohort overview (age, sex, LOS, readmission + category breakdown), top conditions & medications, distributions & outliers (age/LOS histograms with outlier counts), group comparison (readmission, escalation and LOS across category, sex, age band, disposition, insurance or region), and a data-quality assessment (field completeness, terminology coverage, internal consistency, per-analytic readiness). Panels stack, so several can be compared side by side.
  • Multi-patient timeline drawer (v2). Select patients — individually, per page, or "select entire cohort" (paged walk, capped at 5,000) — and open a bottom-anchored drawer that fetches each patient's coded events in parallel and plots them on a shared longitudinal timeline.
  • Knowledge graph view (v2). The same selection rendered as a patient knowledge graph — coded SNOMED/RxNorm events as interconnected nodes instead of a time axis. Both views are v2-mode features since they need terminology-coded data; the drawer says so honestly when coded data is missing. In the Suite both render as inline outputs — the Knowledge graph tile is gated to v2/v3 and disabled with a version hint on v1.
  • Overview — Data quality assessment (v2 coded)
  • Visual analytics — Lab value distributions (box + violin) · Event series sparklines · Calendar heatmap · Comorbidity chord diagram
  • Statistics — Cox proportional hazards · Frequent itemsets (FP-Growth)
  • Overview — Data quality assessment (v3 temporal)
  • Visual analytics — Lab value distributions (box + violin) · Event series sparklines · Calendar heatmap · Comorbidity chord diagram
  • Statistics — Cox proportional hazards · Frequent itemsets (FP-Growth)
Key Features
  • All analytics run at once. On load the app fetches a cohort and precomputes every analytic; everything re-runs automatically after each cohort fetch.
  • Three disease modules — Hypertension, Type 2 Diabetes, COPD. Each with 15 dedicated passes and 4 decision analytics. Patients carry diseases independently in a DiseaseBundle, so comorbid patients appear in both cohorts automatically.
  • Lite & Advanced browser modes. Lite lands on the cohort-first workflow; the Advanced toggle reveals every analytic on its own page for deep-dives.
  • Suite + AI interpretation. Every Suite output gets an AI-drafted hypothesis, runnable as a deterministic test against the same cohort.
  • Patient journey map. Every patient's trajectory on one swimlane canvas: multi-patient comparison with per-patient colored traced lines, frequency-sized dots, lane checkboxes, full-cohort overlay, and a switchable time axis (calendar dates or “since day 0” relative units) with a decade-to-minute zoom ladder. A Cohort Analysis panel aggregates every plotted concept across the whole cohort with a frequency-ordered or timing-ordered layout, zoom/pan/reset controls, and empty-list hiding.
  • Process-mining analytics (v3). Pathway discovery, timing intervals, trajectory clustering, pre-diagnosis association rules and trajectory outliers — mined deterministically from the cohort's event stream.
  • Version-aware Suite with inline pages. All three engine versions share one Suite: unavailable analytics show disabled version-hint buttons, PICO/PECO / explorer / knowledge-graph open as inline outputs, results persist across reloads, and every growing table paginates.
  • LLM-assisted query building. In the Build-Your-Own query engine, the LLM can suggest or build a query for you — describe what you're looking for in plain language and it drafts the query, validated against the field catalog, ready to run on the cohort.
  • Generate-and-test LLM loop. The LLM drafts a hypothesis and a validated test query; the query runs as deterministic code, the matched-vs-rest comparison is shown, then the human gate decides.
  • Database mode for large cohorts. Bulk-loads 100k–1M+ patients into PostgreSQL and runs analytics as SQL aggregation; the case explorer paginates server-side.
  • Bulk file import. Upload CSV or NDJSON patient exports directly into PostgreSQL — for when data lives in an export, not a FHIR server.
  • Multi-provider LLM layer. OpenRouter, Anthropic Claude, OpenAI, and Google Gemini. Keys go only to the backend, drafts are stored and audited, and every draft stays pending until human review.
  • FHIR connectivity. Pick a demo endpoint (e.g. SMART Health IT R4 Synthea) or point at any custom FHIR R4 base URL with an optional bearer token.
Getting Started 
# install and run
npm install
npm run dev:api        # Fastify on http://localhost:8787
npm run dev:web        # Vite on http://localhost:5173
npm test               # vitest across packages

# optional: PostgreSQL persistence
docker compose up -d db
cp .env.example .env
